- [ ] Step 3: Write the module

`libs/codemod/src/core-api.ts`:

```ts
import {
  Node,
  type Expression,
  type Project,
  type SourceFile,
  type Symbol as MorphSymbol,
  type Type,
} from 'ts-morph';

/** The package whose 0.3 API the codemod migrates. */
export const CORE = '@nexusdi/core';

/** How deep `exportName` follows re-exports through a project's own files. */
const MAX_REEXPORT_DEPTH = 8;

/**
 * The name `symbol` has in `@nexusdi/core`'s exports, when it is an import
 * from `@nexusdi/core` or a re-export of one through the project's own files.
 *
 * Detection reads imports, not the declarations they resolve to. A project
 * being migrated may resolve `@nexusdi/core` to 0.3.1, to a workspace 0.4
 * source, or to nothing, and `Service` is still the 0.3 decorator in every
 * case because the import names it.
 */
function coreNameOfSymbol(
  symbol: MorphSymbol | undefined,
  depth: number,
): string | undefined {
  if (!symbol || depth > MAX_REEXPORT_DEPTH) return undefined;
  for (const declaration of symbol.getDeclarations()) {
    if (Node.isImportSpecifier(declaration)) {
      const imported = declaration.getName();
      const importDeclaration = declaration.getImportDeclaration();
      if (importDeclaration.getModuleSpecifierValue() === CORE) return imported;
      const target = importDeclaration.getModuleSpecifierSourceFile();
      if (target) {
        const found = coreNameOfExport(target, imported, depth + 1);
        if (found) return found;
      }
    }
    if (Node.isImportClause(declaration)) {
      const importDeclaration = declaration.getParent();
      if (
        Node.isImportDeclaration(importDeclaration) &&
        importDeclaration.getModuleSpecifierValue() === CORE
      ) {
        return 'default';
      }
    }
    if (Node.isExportSpecifier(declaration)) {
      const exportDeclaration = declaration.getExportDeclaration();
      const imported = declaration.getName();
      if (exportDeclaration.getModuleSpecifierValue() === CORE) return imported;
    }
  }
  return undefined;
}

function coreNameOfExport(
  file: SourceFile,
  exported: string,
  depth: number,
): string | undefined {
  for (const declaration of file.getExportDeclarations()) {
    for (const specifier of declaration.getNamedExports()) {
      const alias = specifier.getAliasNode()?.getText() ?? specifier.getName();
      if (alias !== exported) continue;
      if (declaration.getModuleSpecifierValue() === CORE)
        return specifier.getName();
      const target = declaration.getModuleSpecifierSourceFile();
      if (target)
        return coreNameOfExport(target, specifier.getName(), depth + 1);
      return coreNameOfSymbol(specifier.getNameNode().getSymbol(), depth + 1);
    }
  }
  const local = file.getLocal(exported);
  return coreNameOfSymbol(local, depth + 1);
}

/**
 * The `@nexusdi/core` export an expression names: `Service` for
 * `Service`, `Svc` imported as `{ Service as Svc }`, or `core.Service`
 * with `import * as core`. `default` for the default import.
 */
export function coreExportName(node: Node): string | undefined {
  if (Node.isIdentifier(node)) {
    return coreNameOfSymbol(node.getSymbol(), 0);
  }
  if (Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(node)) {
    const target = node.getExpression();
    if (!Node.isIdentifier(target)) return undefined;
    const declaration = target.getSymbol()?.getDeclarations()[0];
    if (!declaration || !Node.isNamespaceImport(declaration)) return undefined;
    const importDeclaration = declaration.getParent()?.getParent();
    if (
      importDeclaration &&
      Node.isImportDeclaration(importDeclaration) &&
      importDeclaration.getModuleSpecifierValue() === CORE
    ) {
      return node.getName();
    }
  }
  return undefined;
}

/** `coreExportName(node) === name`, with the default import counted as `Nexus`. */
export function isCoreExport(node: Node, ...names: string[]): boolean {
  const found = coreExportName(node);
  if (found === undefined) return false;
  return names.includes(found === 'default' ? 'Nexus' : found);
}

/** The callee of a decorator or call, when it names a core export. */
export function coreCallName(node: Node): string | undefined {
  if (Node.isDecorator(node)) {
    const call = node.getCallExpression();
    const name = coreExportName(
      call ? call.getExpression() : node.getExpression(),
    );
    return name === 'default' ? 'Nexus' : name;
  }
  if (Node.isCallExpression(node) || Node.isNewExpression(node)) {
    const name = coreExportName(node.getExpression());
    return name === 'default' ? 'Nexus' : name;
  }
  return undefined;
}

/** The declaration an identifier or property access resolves to, through imports. */
export function declarationOf(expr: Node): Node | undefined {
  if (!Node.isIdentifier(expr) && !Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(expr))
    return undefined;
  const symbol = expr.getSymbol();
  const target = symbol?.isAlias() ? symbol.getAliasedSymbol() : symbol;
  return target?.getDeclarations()[0];
}

/** Finds the declaration a `declarationKey` names among `files`, after any number of edits. */
export function findDeclaration(
  files: readonly SourceFile[],
  key: string,
): Node | undefined {
  const at = key.lastIndexOf('#');
  const path = key.slice(0, at);
  const name = key.slice(at + 1);
  const file = files.find((f) => f.getFilePath() === path);
  if (!file) return undefined;
  return (
    file.getVariableDeclaration(name) ??
    file.getClass(name) ??
    file.getFunction(name) ??
    file.getEnum(name)
  );
}

/** A stable key for a declaration: its file and its name. */
export function declarationKey(node: Node): string {
  const name =
    (Node.hasName(node) && node.getName()) ||
    (Node.isVariableDeclaration(node) && node.getName()) ||
    `@${node.getStart()}`;
  return `${node.getSourceFile().getFilePath()}#${name}`;
}

/**
 * Knows which types are the 0.3 container and token classes in one project.
 *
 * The keys are built from the declarations every `@nexusdi/core` import of
 * `Nexus`, `IContainer` and `Token` resolves to. They are file-and-name
 * strings, because ts-morph hands out new symbol objects each time the
 * program is rebuilt after an edit.
 */
export class CoreTypes {
  private readonly project: Project;
  private keys: Map<string, Set<string>> | undefined;

  constructor(project: Project) {
    this.project = project;
  }

  private load(): Map<string, Set<string>> {
    if (this.keys) return this.keys;
    const keys = new Map<string, Set<string>>([
      ['container', new Set()],
      ['token', new Set()],
    ]);
    for (const file of this.project.getSourceFiles()) {
      for (const declaration of file.getImportDeclarations()) {
        if (declaration.getModuleSpecifierValue() !== CORE) continue;
        const named = declaration.getNamedImports().map((s) => ({
          name: s.getName(),
          node: s.getNameNode(),
        }));
        const def = declaration.getDefaultImport();
        if (def) named.push({ name: 'Nexus', node: def });
        for (const { name, node } of named) {
          const kind =
            name === 'Nexus' || name === 'IContainer'
              ? 'container'
              : name === 'Token'
                ? 'token'
                : undefined;
          if (!kind) continue;
          const symbol = node.getSymbol();
          const target = symbol?.isAlias() ? symbol.getAliasedSymbol() : symbol;
          for (const decl of target?.getDeclarations() ?? []) {
            keys.get(kind)?.add(declarationKey(decl));
          }
        }
      }
    }
    this.keys = keys;
    return keys;
  }

  private typeHasKey(type: Type, kind: 'container' | 'token'): boolean {
    const keys = this.load().get(kind) ?? new Set<string>();
    const candidates = type.isUnion() ? type.getUnionTypes() : [type];
    return candidates.some((candidate) => {
      const symbol = candidate.getSymbol() ?? candidate.getAliasSymbol();
      return (symbol?.getDeclarations() ?? []).some((d) =>
        keys.has(declarationKey(d)),
      );
    });
  }

  /** Whether `expr` is a 0.3 container: typed as one, or bound to `new Nexus()`. */
  isContainer(expr: Expression): boolean {
    if (this.typeHasKey(expr.getType(), 'container')) return true;
    return boundToNew(expr, 'Nexus');
  }

  /** Whether `expr` is a 0.3 `Token`. */
  isToken(expr: Expression): boolean {
    if (this.typeHasKey(expr.getType(), 'token')) return true;
    return boundToNew(expr, 'Token');
  }
}

const coreTypesByProject = new WeakMap<Project, CoreTypes>();

/** The `CoreTypes` of `project`, built once per run. */
export function coreTypesOf(project: Project): CoreTypes {
  let types = coreTypesByProject.get(project);
  if (!types) {
    types = new CoreTypes(project);
    coreTypesByProject.set(project, types);
  }
  return types;
}

/** Whether `expr` is an identifier whose declaration initializes it with `new <core name>()`. */
function boundToNew(expr: Expression, name: string): boolean {
  if (!Node.isIdentifier(expr)) return false;
  for (const declaration of expr.getSymbol()?.getDeclarations() ?? []) {
    if (!Node.isVariableDeclaration(declaration)) continue;
    const init = declaration.getInitializer();
    if (init && Node.isNewExpression(init) && coreCallName(init) === name)
      return true;
    if (name === 'Nexus' && init && Node.isAwaitExpression(init)) {
      // `await Nexus.create(Root)`, which container-bootstrap writes before 0.4 is installed.
      const call = init.getExpression();
      const callee = Node.isCallExpression(call)
        ? call.getExpression()
        : undefined;
      if (
        callee &&
        Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) &&
        callee.getName() === 'create' &&
        callee.getExpression().getText() === 'Nexus'
      ) {
        return true;
      }
    }
    const typeNode = declaration.getTypeNode();
    if (typeNode && Node.isTypeReference(typeNode)) {
      const typeName = typeNode.getTypeName();
      if (isCoreExport(typeName, name, name === 'Nexus' ? 'IContainer' : name))
        return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}
```

Files:

Interfaces:

- Produces: `specifierFor(from: SourceFile, toPath: string): string`; `nameOf(node): string | undefined`; `planImport(edits: TextEdits, declaration: Node, options?: { typeOnly?: boolean }): boolean`; `renderType(type: Type, edits: TextEdits): string | undefined`.

A relative specifier follows the style the importing file already uses: `.js` when its relative imports carry one, as nodenext requires, and none otherwise.

- [ ] Step 1: Write the failing test

`libs/codemod/src/edit/imports.test.ts`:

```ts
import { Project, SyntaxKind } from 'ts-morph';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { planImport, specifierFor } from './imports.js';
import { renderType } from './render-type.js';
import { TextEdits } from './text-edits.js';

function project(sources: Record<string, string>) {
  const p = new Project({ useInMemoryFileSystem: true });
  return Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(sources).map(([path, text]) => [
      path,
      p.createSourceFile(path, text),
    ]),
  );
}

describe('specifierFor', () => {
  it('writes a relative path in the style the file already uses', () => {
    const files = project({
      '/src/a.ts': "import { x } from './x.js';\n",
      '/src/b.ts': '',
    });
    expect(specifierFor(files['/src/a.ts'] as never, '/src/lib/token.ts')).toBe(
      './lib/token.js',
    );
    expect(specifierFor(files['/src/b.ts'] as never, '/lib/token.ts')).toBe(
      '../lib/token',
    );
  });

  it('writes the package name for a file inside node_modules', () => {
    const files = project({ '/src/a.ts': '' });
    expect(
      specifierFor(
        files['/src/a.ts'] as never,
        '/node_modules/@acme/tokens/index.d.ts',
      ),
    ).toBe('@acme/tokens');
  });
});

describe('planImport', () => {
  it('plans the import of an exported declaration', () => {
    const files = project({
      '/src/token.ts': 'export const TOKEN = 1;\n',
      '/src/a.ts': 'use(TOKEN);\n',
    });
    const edits = new TextEdits(files['/src/a.ts'] as never);
    expect(
      planImport(
        edits,
        files['/src/token.ts']?.getVariableDeclarationOrThrow('TOKEN') as never,
      ),
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(edits.render()).toBe(
      "import { TOKEN } from './token';\n\nuse(TOKEN);\n",
    );
  });

  it('refuses a declaration its file does not export', () => {
    const files = project({
      '/src/token.ts': 'const TOKEN = 1;\n',
      '/src/a.ts': '',
    });
    const edits = new TextEdits(files['/src/a.ts'] as never);
    expect(
      planImport(
        edits,
        files['/src/token.ts']?.getVariableDeclarationOrThrow('TOKEN') as never,
      ),
    ).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('renderType', () => {
  it('writes a named type and plans its type import', () => {
    const files = project({
      '/src/logger.ts': 'export interface Logger { log(m: string): void }\n',
      '/src/use.ts':
        "import type { Logger } from './logger';\ndeclare const l: Logger | undefined;\n",
      '/src/token.ts': 'export const T = 1;\n',
    });
    const type = files['/src/use.ts']
      ?.getFirstDescendantByKindOrThrow(SyntaxKind.VariableDeclaration)
      .getType()
      .getNonNullableType();
    const edits = new TextEdits(files['/src/token.ts'] as never);
    expect(renderType(type as never, edits)).toBe('Logger');
    expect(edits.render()).toBe(
      "import { type Logger } from './logger';\n\nexport const T = 1;\n",
    );
  });

  it('gives up on an anonymous object type', () => {
    const files = project({ '/src/a.ts': 'declare const v: { a: number };\n' });
    const type = files['/src/a.ts']
      ?.getFirstDescendantByKindOrThrow(SyntaxKind.VariableDeclaration)
      .getType();
    expect(
      renderType(type as never, new TextEdits(files['/src/a.ts'] as never)),
    ).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

- [ ] Step 3: Write the import planner

`libs/codemod/src/edit/imports.ts`:

```ts
import { dirname, relative } from 'node:path';

import { Node, type SourceFile } from 'ts-morph';

import type { TextEdits } from './text-edits.js';

const TS_EXTENSION = /\.(d\.)?[cm]?tsx?$/;

/** Whether `file` writes relative imports with a `.js` extension, as nodenext requires. */
function usesJsExtensions(file: SourceFile): boolean {
  return file
    .getImportDeclarations()
    .some((d) => /^\.\.?\/.*\.js$/.test(d.getModuleSpecifierValue()));
}

/** The package name of a file inside node_modules, or undefined. */
function packageOf(path: string): string | undefined {
  return /\/node_modules\/((?:@[^/]+\/)?[^/]+)\//.exec(path)?.[1];
}

/**
 * The specifier `from` uses to import `toPath`: the package name for a file
 * inside node_modules, otherwise a relative path in the style `from`
 * already uses.
 */
export function specifierFor(from: SourceFile, toPath: string): string {
  const pkg = packageOf(toPath);
  if (pkg) return pkg;
  const spec = relative(dirname(from.getFilePath()), toPath)
    .replace(/\\/g, '/')
    .replace(TS_EXTENSION, usesJsExtensions(from) ? '.js' : '');
  return spec.startsWith('.') ? spec : `./${spec}`;
}

/** The name a declaration binds, when it binds one. */
export function nameOf(node: Node): string | undefined {
  if (Node.isVariableDeclaration(node)) return node.getName();
  if (Node.hasName(node)) return node.getName();
  return undefined;
}

function isExported(node: Node): boolean {
  if (Node.isVariableDeclaration(node))
    return node.getVariableStatement()?.isExported() === true;
  return Node.isExportable(node) && node.isExported();
}

/**
 * Plans the import that makes `declaration` available under its own name
 * in the file `edits` belongs to. Returns false when that cannot work: the
 * declaration is not exported, or another binding of the file has the name.
 */
export function planImport(
  edits: TextEdits,
  declaration: Node,
  options: { typeOnly?: boolean } = {},
): boolean {
  const file = edits.file;
  const name = nameOf(declaration);
  if (!name) return false;
  const home = declaration.getSourceFile();
  if (home === file) return true;
  const local = file.getLocal(name);
  if (local) {
    const target = local.isAlias() ? local.getAliasedSymbol() : local;
    return (target?.getDeclarations() ?? []).includes(declaration);
  }
  if (!isExported(declaration)) return false;
  edits.addImport(
    specifierFor(file, home.getFilePath()),
    name,
    options.typeOnly === true,
  );
  return true;
}
```

- [ ] Step 4: Write the type renderer

`libs/codemod/src/edit/render-type.ts`:

```ts
import { Node, type Type } from 'ts-morph';

import { nameOf, planImport } from './imports.js';
import type { TextEdits } from './text-edits.js';

/**
 * Writes `type` as source text valid in the file `edits` belongs to, and
 * plans the type imports it needs. Returns undefined for a type with no
 * such text: `any`, `unknown`, an anonymous object, or a named type whose
 * declaration the file cannot import. Nothing is planned unless the whole
 * type renders.
 */
export function renderType(type: Type, edits: TextEdits): string | undefined {
  const declarations: Node[] = [];
  const text = render(type, declarations);
  if (text === undefined) return undefined;
  if (!declarations.every((d) => importable(edits, d))) return undefined;
  for (const declaration of declarations)
    planImport(edits, declaration, { typeOnly: true });
  return text;
}

function importable(edits: TextEdits, declaration: Node): boolean {
  const file = edits.file;
  if (declaration.getSourceFile() === file) return true;
  const name = nameOf(declaration);
  if (!name) return false;
  const local = file.getLocal(name);
  if (local) {
    const target = local.isAlias() ? local.getAliasedSymbol() : local;
    return (target?.getDeclarations() ?? []).includes(declaration);
  }
  return Node.isExportable(declaration) && declaration.isExported();
}

function render(type: Type, declarations: Node[]): string | undefined {
  if (type.isAny() || type.isUnknown() || type.isNever()) return undefined;
  if (type.isString() || type.isStringLiteral()) return 'string';
  if (type.isNumber() || type.isNumberLiteral()) return 'number';
  if (type.isBoolean() || type.isBooleanLiteral()) return 'boolean';
  if (type.isUndefined()) return 'undefined';
  if (type.isNull()) return 'null';
  if (type.getText() === 'bigint' || type.getText() === 'symbol')
    return type.getText();
  if (type.isArray()) {
    const element = type.getArrayElementType();
    const inner = element ? render(element, declarations) : undefined;
    if (inner === undefined) return undefined;
    return element?.isUnion() ? `(${inner})[]` : `${inner}[]`;
  }
  if (type.isUnion()) {
    const members = type.getUnionTypes();
    // TypeScript models boolean as the union true | false.
    if (members.every((m) => m.isBooleanLiteral())) return 'boolean';
    const parts = new Set<string>();
    for (const member of members) {
      const part = render(member, declarations);
      if (part === undefined) return undefined;
      parts.add(part);
    }
    return [...parts].join(' | ');
  }
  const symbol = type.getAliasSymbol() ?? type.getSymbol();
  const declaration = symbol?.getDeclarations()[0];
  if (
    !symbol ||
    !declaration ||
    !(
      Node.isInterfaceDeclaration(declaration) ||
      Node.isClassDeclaration(declaration) ||
      Node.isTypeAliasDeclaration(declaration) ||
      Node.isEnumDeclaration(declaration)
    )
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }
  declarations.push(declaration);
  const args = type.getAliasSymbol()
    ? type.getAliasTypeArguments()
    : type.getTypeArguments();
  if (args.length === 0) return symbol.getName();
  const rendered: string[] = [];
  for (const arg of args) {
    const part = render(arg, declarations);
    if (part === undefined) return undefined;
    rendered.push(part);
  }
  return `${symbol.getName()}<${rendered.join(', ')}>`;
}
```

### Task 9: A JSONC editor for tsconfig files

Files:

- Create: `libs/codemod/src/edit/jsonc.ts`, `libs/codemod/src/edit/jsonc.test.ts`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: nothing from earlier tasks.
- Produces: `readJsonc(text, path): unknown`; `removeJsonc(text, path): string`; `setJsonc(text, path, value): string`; `commentAboveJsonc(text, path, comment): string`.

jsonc-parser's `modify` removes a property from the end of the previous one, which leaves the removed property's same-line comment attached to its neighbour. `removeJsonc` removes a property that sits on lines of its own together with those lines and falls back to `modify` otherwise.

- [ ] Step 1: Write the failing test

`libs/codemod/src/edit/jsonc.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import {
  commentAboveJsonc,
  readJsonc,
  removeJsonc,
  setJsonc,
} from './jsonc.js';

const config = `{
  // Shared settings
  "extends": "./base.json",
  "compilerOptions": {
    "strict": true,
    "experimentalDecorators": true, // NexusDI 0.3
    "target": "es2022",
  },
}
`;

describe('readJsonc', () => {
  it('reads a value from JSON with comments and trailing commas', () => {
    expect(
      readJsonc(config, ['compilerOptions', 'experimentalDecorators']),
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(
      readJsonc(config, ['compilerOptions', 'emitDecoratorMetadata']),
    ).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('removeJsonc', () => {
  it('removes one property and keeps every other comment and trailing comma', () => {
    expect(removeJsonc(config, ['compilerOptions', 'experimentalDecorators']))
      .toBe(`{
  // Shared settings
  "extends": "./base.json",
  "compilerOptions": {
    "strict": true,
    "target": "es2022",
  },
}
`);
  });

  it('leaves the text alone when the property is absent', () => {
    expect(
      removeJsonc(config, ['compilerOptions', 'emitDecoratorMetadata']),
    ).toBe(config);
  });
});

describe('setJsonc', () => {
  it('creates a property in the indentation the file uses', () => {
    const text = '{\n    "extends": "@acme/tsconfig"\n}\n';
    expect(
      setJsonc(text, ['compilerOptions', 'experimentalDecorators'], false),
    ).toBe(
      '{\n    "extends": "@acme/tsconfig",\n    "compilerOptions": {\n        "experimentalDecorators": false\n    }\n}\n',
    );
  });
});

describe('commentAboveJsonc', () => {
  it('writes a comment line above a property once', () => {
    const once = commentAboveJsonc(
      config,
      ['compilerOptions', 'strict'],
      'note',
    );
    expect(once).toContain('    // note\n    "strict": true,');
    expect(commentAboveJsonc(once, ['compilerOptions', 'strict'], 'note')).toBe(
      once,
    );
  });
});
```

- [ ] Step 3: Write the editor

`libs/codemod/src/edit/jsonc.ts`:

```ts
import {
  applyEdits,
  findNodeAtLocation,
  type FormattingOptions,
  getNodeValue,
  type JSONPath,
  modify,
  parseTree,
} from 'jsonc-parser';

/**
 * Edits JSON with comments, such as a tsconfig, and keeps every comment and
 * trailing comma the edit does not touch.
 */

/** The indentation and line ending `text` already uses. */
function formattingOf(text: string): FormattingOptions {
  const indent = /\n([ \t]+)\S/.exec(text)?.[1] ?? '  ';
  return {
    insertSpaces: !indent.startsWith('\t'),
    tabSize: indent.startsWith('\t') ? 1 : indent.length,
    eol: text.includes('\r\n') ? '\r\n' : '\n',
  };
}

/** The property node at `path`: the key and its value. */
function propertyAt(text: string, path: JSONPath) {
  const root = parseTree(text);
  return root ? findNodeAtLocation(root, path)?.parent : undefined;
}

/** The value at `path`, or undefined when absent. */
export function readJsonc(text: string, path: JSONPath): unknown {
  const root = parseTree(text);
  const node = root ? findNodeAtLocation(root, path) : undefined;
  return node ? getNodeValue(node) : undefined;
}

/**
 * `text` without the property at `path`. A property on lines of its own
 * goes with those lines and the comment after it on the same line, which
 * describes it; the comment lines above it stay. Unchanged when absent.
 */
export function removeJsonc(text: string, path: JSONPath): string {
  const property = propertyAt(text, path);
  if (!property) return text;
  const start = property.offset;
  const end = property.offset + property.length;
  const lineStart = text.lastIndexOf('\n', start - 1) + 1;
  const tail = /^[ \t]*,?[ \t]*(\/\/[^\n]*)?\r?\n/.exec(text.slice(end));
  if (text.slice(lineStart, start).trim() === '' && tail)
    return text.slice(0, lineStart) + text.slice(end + tail[0].length);
  return applyEdits(
    text,
    modify(text, path, undefined, { formattingOptions: formattingOf(text) }),
  );
}

/** `text` with the property at `path` set to `value`, created when absent. */
export function setJsonc(text: string, path: JSONPath, value: unknown): string {
  return applyEdits(
    text,
    modify(text, path, value, { formattingOptions: formattingOf(text) }),
  );
}

/** `text` with `// <comment>` on a line of its own above the property at `path`, once. Unchanged when absent. */
export function commentAboveJsonc(
  text: string,
  path: JSONPath,
  comment: string,
): string {
  const property = propertyAt(text, path);
  if (!property) return text;
  const lineStart = text.lastIndexOf('\n', property.offset - 1) + 1;
  const indent = /^[ \t]*/.exec(text.slice(lineStart))?.[0] ?? '';
  const line = `${indent}// ${comment}`;
  const previousStart = text.lastIndexOf('\n', lineStart - 2) + 1;
  if (
    lineStart > 0 &&
    text.slice(previousStart, lineStart - 1).replace(/\r$/, '') === line
  )
    return text;
  const eol = text.includes('\r\n') ? '\r\n' : '\n';
  return `${text.slice(0, lineStart)}${line}${eol}${text.slice(lineStart)}`;
}
```

Interfaces:

- Consumes: `Migration`, `Transform`, `MigrationContext`, `runMigration`, `RunOptions`, `MigrationResult` (Task 5); `loadProjects` (Task 6); `coreCallName` (Task 7).
- Produces: `MIGRATIONS: ReadonlyMap<string, MigrationEntry>` with `MigrationEntry { transformIds; run(options) }`; `TRANSFORM_IDS` (the spec order); `migration04: Migration<Migration04State>`; `interface ServiceToken { text; declaration }`; `interface Migration04State { serviceTokens: Map<string, ServiceToken[]>; dynamicModules: Map<string, boolean>; roots: string[]; legacyDecorators: boolean | undefined }` (Task 18 adds `moduleGraph`); `createState()`; `legacyDecoratorsInUse(context): boolean`; harness `FIXTURE_ROOT`, `FixtureCore`, `CaseConfig { transforms?; core?; paths?; typecheck? }`, `FixtureReport`, `FixtureCase`, `loadCases(root)`, `runFixture(files, options)`, `updateFixture(fixture, run)`.

A fixture runs the whole migration as registered at that point; see "Decisions". Each transform task below adds its transform to `migration04.transforms` at its place in `TRANSFORM_IDS`, and its fixtures pin its behaviour. Every case runs twice: once for its expected output, and once over that output, which must come back unchanged with an empty report (Review Focus 4).

```ts
import { readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { loadCases, runFixture, updateFixture } from '../../testing/harness.js';

const FIXTURES = join(import.meta.dirname, '__fixtures__');
const UPDATE = process.env['NEXUSDI_UPDATE_FIXTURES'] === '1';

const groups = readdirSync(FIXTURES, { withFileTypes: true })
  .filter((entry) => entry.isDirectory())
  .map((entry) => entry.name)
  .sort();

for (const group of groups) {
  describe(group, () => {
    for (const fixture of loadCases(join(FIXTURES, group))) {
      it(`migrates the ${fixture.name} case`, () => {
        const run = runFixture(fixture.input, fixture.config);
        if (UPDATE) updateFixture(fixture, run);
        expect(Object.fromEntries(run.output)).toEqual(
          Object.fromEntries(fixture.output),
        );
        expect(run.report).toEqual(fixture.report);
      });

      it(`leaves the ${fixture.name} output as it is on a second run`, () => {
        const inputs = new Map(
          [...fixture.input].map(([path, text]) => [
            path,
            fixture.output.get(path) ?? text,
          ]),
        );
        const run = runFixture(inputs, fixture.config);
        const own = [...inputs].filter(
          ([path]) => !path.startsWith('node_modules/'),
        );
        expect(Object.fromEntries(run.output)).toEqual(Object.fromEntries(own));
        expect(run.report).toEqual({ files: [] });
      });
    }
  });
}
```

- [ ] Step 5: Write the shared state

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/state.ts`:

```ts
import { SyntaxKind } from 'ts-morph';

import { coreCallName } from '../../core-api.js';
import type { MigrationContext } from '../../transform.js';

/** A token named in `@Service(TOKEN)` or `@Provider(TOKEN)`. */
export interface ServiceToken {
  /** The token expression as written, such as `LOGGER_TOKEN`. */
  readonly text: string;
  /** `file#name` of the token's declaration, when it is a named declaration. */
  readonly declaration: string | undefined;
}

/** The state the 0.4 transforms share across files and projects. */
export interface Migration04State {
  /** `file#ClassName` of a service class to the tokens its decorator named. */
  readonly serviceTokens: Map<string, ServiceToken[]>;
  /** `file#ClassName` of each `DynamicModule` subclass to whether module-config converts it. */
  readonly dynamicModules: Map<string, boolean>;
  /** `file#name` of every root module container-bootstrap created. */
  readonly roots: string[];
  /** Whether the project applies decorators that do not come from `@nexusdi/core`. Computed once. */
  legacyDecorators: boolean | undefined;
}

export function createState(): Migration04State {
  return {
    serviceTokens: new Map(),
    dynamicModules: new Map(),
    roots: [],
    legacyDecorators: undefined,
  };
}

/**
 * Whether any file of the run applies a decorator `@nexusdi/core` did not
 * export. Such a project keeps experimentalDecorators, under which the 0.4
 * decorators throw NEXUS_LEGACY_DECORATORS.
 */
export function legacyDecoratorsInUse(
  context: MigrationContext<Migration04State>,
): boolean {
  context.state.legacyDecorators ??= context.files.some((file) =>
    file
      .getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.Decorator)
      .some(
        (d) =>
          coreCallName(d) === undefined && !OWN_DECORATORS.has(d.getName()),
      ),
  );
  return context.state.legacyDecorators;
}

/** The 0.4 decorators transforms write. Before `imports` runs they resolve to nothing. */
const OWN_DECORATORS = new Set(['Injectable', 'Inject', 'Module']);
```

```ts
import type { Migration, Transform } from '../../transform.js';
import { createState, type Migration04State } from './state.js';

/**
 * The 0.4 transform ids in the order the spec fixes. Later transforms read
 * the output of earlier ones, so the order is part of the contract.
 */
export const TRANSFORM_IDS = [
  'symbol-tokens',
  'services',
  'property-injection',
  'module-config',
  'container-bootstrap',
  'module-exports',
  'await-get',
  'container-leftovers',
  'imports',
  'tsconfig',
] as const;

// Each transform task adds its transform here, at its place in TRANSFORM_IDS.
const transforms: readonly Transform<Migration04State>[] = [];

export const migration04: Migration<Migration04State> = {
  version: '0.4',
  transforms,
  createState,
};
```

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/index.ts`:

```ts
import {
  type MigrationResult,
  type RunOptions,
  runMigration,
} from '../runner.js';
import { migration04 } from './0.4/index.js';

/** What the CLI needs of one migration, independent of the state its transforms share. */
export interface MigrationEntry {
  readonly transformIds: readonly string[];
  run(options: Omit<RunOptions<never>, 'migration'>): MigrationResult;
}

/** Every migration the CLI offers, keyed by the version it migrates to. A later migration adds a key. */
export const MIGRATIONS: ReadonlyMap<string, MigrationEntry> = new Map([
  [
    migration04.version,
    {
      transformIds: migration04.transforms.map((t) => t.id),
      run: (options) => runMigration({ ...options, migration: migration04 }),
    },
  ],
]);
```

- [ ] Step 7: Write the harness

`libs/codemod/src/testing/harness.ts`:

```ts
import {
  existsSync,
  mkdirSync,
  readdirSync,
  readFileSync,
  rmSync,
  statSync,
  writeFileSync,
} from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, join, relative } from 'node:path';

import { InMemoryFileSystemHost, Project, ts } from 'ts-morph';

import { migration04 } from '../migrations/0.4/index.js';
import { loadProjects } from '../project/load-projects.js';
import { type MigrationResult, runMigration } from '../runner.js';

/** Where fixture files live inside the in-memory file system. */
export const FIXTURE_ROOT = '/fixture';

const VENDOR = join(import.meta.dirname, 'vendor');

/** Which `@nexusdi/core` declarations a case compiles against. */
export type FixtureCore = 'core-0.3.1' | 'core-async-branch';

/** `case.json`: optional settings for one case. */
export interface CaseConfig {
  /** The transforms to run. Every transform of the migration when absent. */
  readonly transforms?: readonly string[];
  /** The `@nexusdi/core` declarations the input compiles against. `core-0.3.1` when absent. */
  readonly core?: FixtureCore;
  /** Files to pass as the CLI's `[paths...]`, relative to the case. */
  readonly paths?: readonly string[];
  /** Why the output is exempt from the 0.4 typecheck. Checked when absent. */
  readonly typecheck?: string;
}

export interface FixtureReportFile {
  readonly path: string;
  readonly todos: readonly {
    line: number;
    column: number;
    code: string;
    reason: string;
  }[];
  readonly notes: readonly {
    line: number;
    column: number;
    code: string;
    message: string;
  }[];
}

/** `report.json`: the TODOs and notes a case produces, per file. */
export interface FixtureReport {
  readonly files: readonly FixtureReportFile[];
}

export interface FixtureCase {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly dir: string;
  readonly config: CaseConfig;
  /** Input files by path relative to the case. */
  readonly input: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
  /** Expected output files by path relative to the case. */
  readonly output: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
  readonly report: FixtureReport;
}

function readTree(root: string): Map<string, string> {
  const files = new Map<string, string>();
  const walk = (dir: string): void => {
    for (const name of readdirSync(dir)) {
      const path = join(dir, name);
      if (statSync(path).isDirectory()) walk(path);
      else
        files.set(
          relative(root, path).replace(/\\/g, '/'),
          readFileSync(path, 'utf8'),
        );
    }
  };
  walk(root);
  return files;
}

/** Reads one side of a case: `input.ts` alone, or the `input/` directory. */
function readSide(dir: string, side: 'input' | 'output'): Map<string, string> {
  const single = join(dir, `${side}.ts`);
  if (existsSync(single))
    return new Map([['index.ts', readFileSync(single, 'utf8')]]);
  const tree = join(dir, side);
  return existsSync(tree) ? readTree(tree) : new Map();
}

/** Every case under `root`, one directory each, sorted by name. */
export function loadCases(root: string): FixtureCase[] {
  return readdirSync(root)
    .filter((name) => statSync(join(root, name)).isDirectory())
    .sort()
    .map((name) => {
      const dir = join(root, name);
      const configPath = join(dir, 'case.json');
      const reportPath = join(dir, 'report.json');
      return {
        name,
        dir,
        config: existsSync(configPath)
          ? (JSON.parse(readFileSync(configPath, 'utf8')) as CaseConfig)
          : {},
        input: readSide(dir, 'input'),
        output: readSide(dir, 'output'),
        report: existsSync(reportPath)
          ? (JSON.parse(readFileSync(reportPath, 'utf8')) as FixtureReport)
          : { files: [] },
      };
    });
}

function mountCore(host: InMemoryFileSystemHost, core: FixtureCore): void {
  const files = readTree(join(VENDOR, core));
  for (const [path, text] of files) {
    host.writeFileSync(
      `${FIXTURE_ROOT}/node_modules/@nexusdi/core/${path}`,
      text,
    );
  }
}

const SOURCE = /\.(?:[cm]?ts|tsx)$/;

/** The compiler options a case without its own tsconfig runs under: a 0.3 project. */
const DEFAULT_OPTIONS: ts.CompilerOptions = {
  strict: true,
  target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022,
  module: ts.ModuleKind.ESNext,
  moduleResolution: ts.ModuleResolutionKind.Bundler,
  experimentalDecorators: true,
  jsx: ts.JsxEmit.ReactJSX,
  noEmit: true,
};

export interface FixtureRun {
  readonly output: Map<string, string>;
  readonly report: FixtureReport;
  readonly result: MigrationResult;
}

/**
 * Runs the 0.4 migration over `files` in an in-memory project.
 *
 * A case with a `tsconfig.json` at its root is loaded the way the CLI loads
 * a project. Any other case compiles as one project under DEFAULT_OPTIONS.
 */
export function runFixture(
  files: ReadonlyMap<string, string>,
  options: {
    transforms?: readonly string[];
    core?: FixtureCore;
    paths?: readonly string[];
  } = {},
): FixtureRun {
  const host = new InMemoryFileSystemHost();
  mountCore(host, options.core ?? 'core-0.3.1');
  for (const [path, text] of files)
    host.writeFileSync(`${FIXTURE_ROOT}/${path}`, text);

  const readTextFile = (path: string): string | undefined =>
    host.fileExistsSync(path) ? host.readFileSync(path) : undefined;

  let workspace;
  if (files.has('tsconfig.json')) {
    workspace = loadProjects({
      tsconfig: `${FIXTURE_ROOT}/tsconfig.json`,
      fileSystem: host,
      paths: options.paths?.map((p) => `${FIXTURE_ROOT}/${p}`),
    });
  } else {
    const project = new Project({
      fileSystem: host,
      compilerOptions: DEFAULT_OPTIONS,
    });
    const sources = [...files.keys()]
      .filter((path) => SOURCE.test(path) && !path.startsWith('node_modules/'))
      .map((path) => project.addSourceFileAtPath(`${FIXTURE_ROOT}/${path}`));
    workspace = {
      projects: [{ project, files: sources }],
      tsconfigs: [],
      warnings: [],
    };
  }

  const result = runMigration({
    migration: migration04,
    projects: workspace.projects,
    tsconfigs: workspace.tsconfigs,
    only: options.transforms,
    readTextFile,
  });

  const changed = new Map(result.files.map((f) => [f.path, f.after]));
  const output = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const [path, text] of files) {
    if (path.startsWith('node_modules/')) continue;
    output.set(path, changed.get(`${FIXTURE_ROOT}/${path}`) ?? text);
  }

  const byFile = new Map<
    string,
    {
      todos: FixtureReportFile['todos'][number][];
      notes: FixtureReportFile['notes'][number][];
    }
  >();
  const entry = (path: string) => {
    const key = path.slice(FIXTURE_ROOT.length + 1);
    let found = byFile.get(key);
    if (!found) {
      found = { todos: [], notes: [] };
      byFile.set(key, found);
    }
    return found;
  };
  for (const t of result.todos)
    entry(t.path).todos.push({
      line: t.line,
      column: t.column,
      code: t.code,
      reason: t.reason,
    });
  for (const n of result.notes)
    entry(n.path).notes.push({
      line: n.line,
      column: n.column,
      code: n.code,
      message: n.message,
    });
  const report: FixtureReport = {
    files: [...byFile]
      .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
      .map(([path, e]) => ({ path, ...e })),
  };

  return { output, report, result };
}

/**
 * Writes what a case produced as its expected output. The fixture test
 * calls it under NEXUSDI_UPDATE_FIXTURES=1, for the corpus cases no one
 * writes by hand; the diff is reviewed before it is committed.
 */
export function updateFixture(fixture: FixtureCase, run: FixtureRun): void {
  const single = existsSync(join(fixture.dir, 'input.ts'));
  for (const [path, text] of run.output) {
    const target = single
      ? join(fixture.dir, 'output.ts')
      : join(fixture.dir, 'output', path);
    mkdirSync(dirname(target), { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync(target, text);
  }
  const reportPath = join(fixture.dir, 'report.json');
  if (run.report.files.length > 0)
    writeFileSync(reportPath, `${JSON.stringify(run.report, null, 2)}\n`);
  else rmSync(reportPath, { force: true });
}
```

`libs/codemod/src/report/report.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import type { MigrationResult } from '../runner.js';
import { renderDiff } from './diff.js';
import { buildReport, renderMarkdown, renderSummary } from './report.js';

const result: MigrationResult = {
  files: [
    {
      path: '/app/src/a.ts',
      before: 'a\n',
      after: 'b\n',
      transforms: ['services', 'imports'],
    },
  ],
  todos: [
    {
      path: '/app/src/a.ts',
      line: 3,
      column: 5,
      code: 'set-after-startup',
      reason: 'register it in a module',
    },
  ],
  notes: [
    {
      path: '/app/src/b.ts',
      line: 1,
      column: 1,
      code: 'undecorated-param-injected',
      message: 'Logger is injected now',
    },
  ],
};

describe('buildReport', () => {
  it('writes the report shape of the spec, with paths relative to the working directory', () => {
    expect(
      buildReport(result, {
        migration: '0.4',
        codemodVersion: '0.4.0-rc.0',
        cwd: '/app',
      }),
    ).toEqual({
      version: 1,
      migration: '0.4',
      codemodVersion: '0.4.0-rc.0',
      files: [
        {
          path: 'src/a.ts',
          transforms: ['services', 'imports'],
          todos: [
            {
              line: 3,
              column: 5,
              code: 'set-after-startup',
              reason: 'register it in a module',
            },
          ],
          notes: [],
        },
        {
          path: 'src/b.ts',
          transforms: [],
          todos: [],
          notes: [
            {
              line: 1,
              column: 1,
              code: 'undecorated-param-injected',
              message: 'Logger is injected now',
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
      summary: {
        filesChanged: 1,
        todos: 1,
        notes: 1,
        byCode: { 'set-after-startup': 1, 'undecorated-param-injected': 1 },
      },
    });
  });
});

describe('renderMarkdown', () => {
  it('lists every TODO and note under its file', () => {
    const markdown = renderMarkdown(
      buildReport(result, {
        migration: '0.4',
        codemodVersion: '0.4.0-rc.0',
        cwd: '/app',
      }),
    );
    expect(markdown).toContain('## src/a.ts');
    expect(markdown).toContain(
      '- TODO `set-after-startup` at 3:5: register it in a module',
    );
    expect(markdown).toContain(
      '- Note `undecorated-param-injected` at 1:1: Logger is injected now',
    );
  });
});

describe('renderSummary', () => {
  it('prints the counts and each TODO location', () => {
    const summary = renderSummary(
      buildReport(result, {
        migration: '0.4',
        codemodVersion: '0.4.0-rc.0',
        cwd: '/app',
      }),
    );
    expect(summary).toBe(
      '1 files changed, 1 TODOs, 1 notes.\nsrc/a.ts:3:5  set-after-startup  register it in a module\n',
    );
  });
});

describe('renderDiff', () => {
  it('prints a unified diff with a/ and b/ paths', () => {
    expect(renderDiff(result.files, '/app')).toBe(
      '--- a/src/a.ts\n+++ b/src/a.ts\n@@ -1,1 +1,1 @@\n-a\n+b\n',
    );
  });
});
```

- [ ] Step 3: Write the report

`libs/codemod/src/report/report.ts`:

```ts
import { relative } from 'node:path';

import type { MigrationResult } from '../runner.js';

export interface ReportTodo {
  readonly line: number;
  readonly column: number;
  readonly code: string;
  readonly reason: string;
}

export interface ReportNote {
  readonly line: number;
  readonly column: number;
  readonly code: string;
  readonly message: string;
}

export interface ReportFile {
  readonly path: string;
  readonly transforms: readonly string[];
  readonly todos: readonly ReportTodo[];
  readonly notes: readonly ReportNote[];
}

/** The report the CLI writes, in the shape section 13.2 of the spec fixes. */
export interface Report {
  readonly version: 1;
  readonly migration: string;
  readonly codemodVersion: string;
  readonly files: readonly ReportFile[];
  readonly summary: {
    readonly filesChanged: number;
    readonly todos: number;
    readonly notes: number;
    readonly byCode: Readonly<Record<string, number>>;
  };
}

/** Builds the report of a run, with paths relative to `cwd`. */
export function buildReport(
  result: MigrationResult,
  options: { migration: string; codemodVersion: string; cwd: string },
): Report {
  const files = new Map<
    string,
    { transforms: string[]; todos: ReportTodo[]; notes: ReportNote[] }
  >();
  const entry = (path: string) => {
    let found = files.get(path);
    if (!found) {
      found = { transforms: [], todos: [], notes: [] };
      files.set(path, found);
    }
    return found;
  };
  for (const file of result.files)
    entry(file.path).transforms.push(...file.transforms);
  for (const { path, line, column, code, reason } of result.todos)
    entry(path).todos.push({ line, column, code, reason });
  for (const { path, line, column, code, message } of result.notes)
    entry(path).notes.push({ line, column, code, message });

  const byCode: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const { code } of [...result.todos, ...result.notes])
    byCode[code] = (byCode[code] ?? 0) + 1;

  return {
    version: 1,
    migration: options.migration,
    codemodVersion: options.codemodVersion,
    files: [...files]
      .map(([path, f]) => ({
        path: relative(options.cwd, path).replace(/\\/g, '/'),
        ...f,
      }))
      .sort((a, b) => a.path.localeCompare(b.path)),
    summary: {
      filesChanged: result.files.length,
      todos: result.todos.length,
      notes: result.notes.length,
      byCode: Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(byCode).sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b)),
      ),
    },
  };
}

/** The report as Markdown, for a `--report` path ending in `.md`. */
export function renderMarkdown(report: Report): string {
  const lines = [
    `# NexusDI ${report.migration} migration report`,
    '',
    `Codemod ${report.codemodVersion}. ${report.summary.filesChanged} files changed, ${report.summary.todos} TODOs, ${report.summary.notes} notes.`,
    '',
  ];
  const codes = Object.entries(report.summary.byCode);
  if (codes.length > 0) {
    lines.push(
      '| Code | Count |',
      '| --- | --- |',
      ...codes.map(([code, count]) => `| \`${code}\` | ${count} |`),
      '',
    );
  }
  for (const file of report.files) {
    if (file.todos.length === 0 && file.notes.length === 0) continue;
    lines.push(`## ${file.path}`, '');
    for (const todo of file.todos)
      lines.push(
        `- TODO \`${todo.code}\` at ${todo.line}:${todo.column}: ${todo.reason}`,
      );
    for (const note of file.notes)
      lines.push(
        `- Note \`${note.code}\` at ${note.line}:${note.column}: ${note.message}`,
      );
    lines.push('');
  }
  return `${lines.join('\n')}`;
}

/** The summary and every TODO location, as the CLI prints them. */
export function renderSummary(report: Report): string {
  const lines = [
    `${report.summary.filesChanged} files changed, ${report.summary.todos} TODOs, ${report.summary.notes} notes.`,
  ];
  for (const file of report.files) {
    for (const todo of file.todos)
      lines.push(
        `${file.path}:${todo.line}:${todo.column}  ${todo.code}  ${todo.reason}`,
      );
  }
  return `${lines.join('\n')}\n`;
}
```

- [ ] Step 4: Write the diff

`libs/codemod/src/report/diff.ts`:

```ts
import { relative } from 'node:path';

import { createTwoFilesPatch } from 'diff';

import type { FileResult } from '../runner.js';

/** A unified diff of every changed file, with `a/` and `b/` paths relative to `cwd`, as git prints them. */
export function renderDiff(files: readonly FileResult[], cwd: string): string {
  return files
    .map((file) => {
      const path = relative(cwd, file.path).replace(/\\/g, '/');
      return createTwoFilesPatch(
        `a/${path}`,
        `b/${path}`,
        file.before,
        file.after,
        undefined,
        undefined,
        { context: 3 },
      )
        .split('\n')
        .slice(1)
        .join('\n');
    })
    .join('');
}
```

## Phase 2: Transforms

The transforms are built in the order their fixtures need, which differs from the order they run in. `imports` comes first, because every fixture output needs its import rewrite to be 0.4 code. Every case's input uses only 0.3 constructs that the transforms registered so far handle, so a later task never changes an earlier task's golden output. Each task registers its transform at its place in `TRANSFORM_IDS`.

All ten transforms share one rule: they read through ts-morph and write through `context.edits(file)`, `context.todo()` and `context.note()`. A TODO for text a transform generates is written into that text with `todoMarker()` and recorded with `context.todoInText()`, because the node it would anchor to does not exist yet.

### Task 12: The `imports` transform

Files:

- Create: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/imports.ts`
- Modify: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/index.ts`
- Test: fixture cases under `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/__fixtures__/imports/`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: `CORE` (Task 7); `TextEdits` (Task 4); `commentLinesAbove`, `TODO_PREFIX`, `todoMarker` (Task 3); `Transform` (Task 5); `Migration04State` (Task 10).
- Produces: `imports: Transform<Migration04State>` with id `imports`.

It renames the 0.3 exports with a 0.4 counterpart (`IContainer` to `Nexus`, `TokenType` to `InjectionToken`, `ProviderType`, `ModuleProvider` and `ProviderConfig` to `Provider`, `ModuleConfig` to `ModuleDefinition`, the exception classes to their 0.4 names), expands `instanceof NoProvider` to both errors 0.4 throws for it, turns the default import into `{ Nexus }`, adds the 0.4 names earlier transforms wrote, removes unused specifiers, moves each removed name still in use into its own import under a TODO, and marks string and regex literals that match a 0.3 error message.

```ts
import Nexus, { ContainerException, IContainer, NoProvider, Token, TokenType } from '@nexusdi/core';

export const LOGGER = new Token<string>('Logger');

export function lookup(container: IContainer, token: TokenType): unknown {
  try {
    return container.get(token);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof NoProvider) return undefined;
    if (error instanceof ContainerException) throw error;
    throw error;
  }
}

export const tokens: TokenType<string>[] = [LOGGER];
export type Container = Nexus;
```

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/__fixtures__/imports/renames/output.ts`:

```ts
import { Nexus, NexusError, MissingProviderError, Token, InjectionToken, NotVisibleError } from '@nexusdi/core';

export const LOGGER = new Token<string>('Logger');

export function lookup(container: Nexus, token: InjectionToken<unknown>): unknown {
  try {
    return container.get(token);
  } catch (error) {
    if ((error instanceof MissingProviderError || error instanceof NotVisibleError)) return undefined;
    if (error instanceof NexusError) throw error;
    throw error;
  }
}

export const tokens: InjectionToken<string>[] = [LOGGER];
export type Container = Nexus;
```

- [ ] Step 3: Write the transform

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/imports.ts`:

```ts
import {
  type Identifier,
  type ImportDeclaration,
  type ImportSpecifier,
  Node,
  type SourceFile,
  SyntaxKind,
} from 'ts-morph';

import type { TodoCode } from '../../../codes.js';
import { CORE } from '../../../core-api.js';
import type { TextEdits } from '../../../edit/text-edits.js';
import {
  commentLinesAbove,
  TODO_PREFIX,
  todoMarker,
} from '../../../markers.js';
import type { Transform } from '../../../transform.js';
import type { Migration04State } from '../state.js';

/**
 * 0.3 exports with a 0.4 export under another name. `generic` marks a type
 * whose 0.4 name takes a required type argument; a bare use gets `<unknown>`.
 */
const RENAMES: ReadonlyMap<
  string,
  { readonly to: string; readonly generic?: boolean }
> = new Map([
  ['IContainer', { to: 'Nexus' }],
  ['TokenType', { to: 'InjectionToken', generic: true }],
  ['ProviderType', { to: 'Provider', generic: true }],
  ['ModuleProvider', { to: 'Provider', generic: true }],
  ['ProviderConfig', { to: 'Provider', generic: true }],
  ['ModuleConfig', { to: 'ModuleDefinition' }],
  ['ContainerException', { to: 'NexusError' }],
  ['NoProvider', { to: 'MissingProviderError' }],
  ['InvalidToken', { to: 'InvalidTokenError' }],
  ['InvalidProvider', { to: 'InvalidProviderError' }],
  ['InvalidModule', { to: 'InvalidModuleError' }],
]);

/** 0.3 exports TypeScript's own lib now declares. The import goes and the name resolves globally. */
const GLOBALS = new Set(['Disposable', 'AsyncDisposable']);

/** 0.3 exports with no 0.4 counterpart, by the TODO code a remaining use gets. An unused one goes without a TODO. */
const REMOVED: ReadonlyMap<string, TodoCode> = new Map<string, TodoCode>([
  ...[
    'Constructor',
    'ClassProvider',
    'ValueProvider',
    'FactoryProvider',
    'ProviderConfigObject',
    'RegistrationOptions',
  ].map((name) => [name, 'removed-provider-type'] as const),
  ...[
    'InjectionMetadata',
    'SYMBOL_METADATA',
    'METADATA_KEYS',
    'setMetadata',
    'getMetadata',
    'Service',
    'Optional',
  ].map((name) => [name, 'removed-metadata-api'] as const),
  ...[
    'isToken',
    'isSymbol',
    'isTokenType',
    'isProvider',
    'isFactory',
    'isConstructor',
    'isService',
    'isContainer',
    'isModuleClass',
    'isModuleConfig',
    'isPromise',
  ].map((name) => [name, 'removed-guard'] as const),
  ...['DynamicModule', 'createModuleConfig'].map(
    (name) => [name, 'dynamic-module-config-api'] as const,
  ),
]);

/** 0.4 names transforms write into code, and whether each is a type. */
const V04_NAMES: ReadonlyMap<string, 'value' | 'type'> = new Map([
  ['Nexus', 'value'],
  ['Token', 'value'],
  ['MultiToken', 'value'],
  ['Injectable', 'value'],
  ['Inject', 'value'],
  ['Module', 'value'],
  ['provide', 'value'],
  ['defineModule', 'value'],
  ['optional', 'value'],
  ['lazy', 'value'],
  ['all', 'value'],
  ['NexusError', 'value'],
  ['MissingProviderError', 'value'],
  ['NotVisibleError', 'value'],
  ['InvalidTokenError', 'value'],
  ['InvalidProviderError', 'value'],
  ['InvalidModuleError', 'value'],
  ['InjectionToken', 'type'],
  ['Provider', 'type'],
  ['ModuleDefinition', 'type'],
]);

/** Fragments of 0.3 error messages. A literal holding one was written to match text 0.4 changed. */
const OLD_MESSAGES = [
  'No provider found for token',
  'Invalid token:',
  'Invalid provider:',
  'is not properly decorated with @Module',
];

const REMOVED_CODES = new Set(REMOVED.values());

/**
 * Every use of the binding `specifier` creates, in `file`. A same-named
 * identifier the checker cannot resolve counts too: `import type { Provider }`
 * of 0.3.1's `Provider` decorator leaves every `Provider<T>` unresolved.
 */
function usesOf(file: SourceFile, specifier: ImportSpecifier): Identifier[] {
  const binding = specifier.getAliasNode() ?? specifier.getNameNode();
  const alias = binding.getSymbol();
  const bound = new Set([
    alias?.compilerSymbol,
    alias?.isAlias() ? alias.getAliasedSymbol()?.compilerSymbol : undefined,
  ]);
  return file.getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.Identifier).filter((id) => {
    if (id === binding || id.getText() !== binding.getText()) return false;
    const parent = id.getParent();
    if (Node.isImportSpecifier(parent)) return false;
    if (
      (Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(parent) ||
        Node.isPropertyAssignment(parent)) &&
      parent.getNameNode() === id
    )
      return false;
    const own = id.getSymbol();
    // The checker gives an unresolvable name a placeholder symbol with no declarations.
    return (
      own === undefined ||
      own.getDeclarations().length === 0 ||
      bound.has(own.compilerSymbol)
    );
  });
}

function isTypePosition(id: Identifier): boolean {
  const parent = id.getParent();
  return (
    Node.isTypeReference(parent) ||
    Node.isExpressionWithTypeArguments(parent) ||
    Node.isTypeQuery(parent)
  );
}

/** Whether an earlier run marked this import as naming removed exports. It is then left alone. */
function isMarked(declaration: ImportDeclaration): boolean {
  const text = declaration.getSourceFile().getFullText();
  return commentLinesAbove(text, declaration.getStart(true)).some(
    (line) =>
      line.startsWith(TODO_PREFIX) &&
      [...REMOVED_CODES].some((code) => line.endsWith(`(${code})`)),
  );
}

/** Renames the uses of a renamed export, and returns the 0.4 names the new text needs imported. */
function renameUses(
  edits: TextEdits,
  uses: readonly Identifier[],
  name: string,
  to: string,
  generic: boolean,
): string[] {
  const introduced = [to];
  for (const use of uses) {
    const parent = use.getParent();
    if (
      name === 'NoProvider' &&
      Node.isBinaryExpression(parent) &&
      parent.getRight() === use &&
      parent.getOperatorToken().getKind() === SyntaxKind.InstanceOfKeyword
    ) {
      // 0.4 splits NoProvider in two: a token no module provides, and one a module keeps private.
      const left = edits.textOf(parent.getLeft());
      edits.replace(
        parent,
        `(${left} instanceof MissingProviderError || ${left} instanceof NotVisibleError)`,
      );
      introduced.push('NotVisibleError');
    } else if (
      generic &&
      Node.isTypeReference(parent) &&
      parent.getTypeArguments().length === 0
    ) {
      edits.replace(parent, `${to}<unknown>`);
    } else {
      edits.replace(use, to);
    }
  }
  return introduced;
}

/** Identifiers named after a 0.4 export that nothing in the file binds, and whether each is used only as a type. */
function unboundNames(file: SourceFile): Map<string, 'value' | 'type'> {
  const found = new Map<string, 'value' | 'type'>();
  for (const id of file.getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.Identifier)) {
    const name = id.getText();
    if (
      !V04_NAMES.has(name) ||
      found.get(name) === 'value' ||
      id.getSymbol() !== undefined
    )
      continue;
    const parent = id.getParent();
    if (
      (Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(parent) ||
        Node.isPropertyAssignment(parent)) &&
      parent.getNameNode() === id
    )
      continue;
    found.set(name, isTypePosition(id) ? 'type' : 'value');
  }
  return found;
}

function renderImport(names: readonly string[], typeOnly: boolean): string {
  return `import ${typeOnly ? 'type ' : ''}{ ${names.join(', ')} } from '${CORE}';`;
}

/** The specifiers a 0.3 import keeps under 0.4 names, and the removed names still in use, by code. */
function migrateSpecifiers(
  file: SourceFile,
  declaration: ImportDeclaration,
  edits: TextEdits,
  removed: Map<TodoCode, string[]>,
  needed: Set<string>,
): string[] {
  const kept: string[] = [];
  const def = declaration.getDefaultImport();
  if (def)
    kept.push(
      def.getText() === 'Nexus' ? 'Nexus' : `Nexus as ${def.getText()}`,
    );
  for (const specifier of declaration.getNamedImports()) {
    const name = specifier.getName();
    const local = specifier.getAliasNode()?.getText() ?? name;
    const aliased = local !== name;
    const uses = usesOf(file, specifier);
    if (GLOBALS.has(name) || uses.length === 0) continue;
    // 0.3.1 exported the Provider decorator; the async branch exported a Provider type, which 0.4 keeps.
    const providerType = name === 'Provider' && uses.every(isTypePosition);
    const code = providerType
      ? undefined
      : (REMOVED.get(name) ??
        (name === 'Provider' ? 'removed-metadata-api' : undefined));
    if (code) {
      removed.set(code, [
        ...(removed.get(code) ?? []),
        aliased ? `${name} as ${local}` : name,
      ]);
      continue;
    }
    const rename = providerType
      ? { to: 'Provider', generic: true }
      : RENAMES.get(name);
    const imported = rename?.to ?? name;
    if (rename && !aliased) {
      for (const extra of renameUses(
        edits,
        uses,
        name,
        rename.to,
        rename.generic === true,
      ))
        needed.add(extra);
    }
    const text = `${specifier.isTypeOnly() ? 'type ' : ''}${aliased ? `${imported} as ${local}` : imported}`;
    if (!kept.includes(text)) kept.push(text);
  }
  return kept;
}

export const imports: Transform<Migration04State> = {
  id: 'imports',
  description:
    'Rewrites @nexusdi/core imports to 0.4 names, adds the names earlier transforms wrote, and removes unused ones.',
  run(file, context) {
    const edits = context.edits(file);
    const removed = new Map<TodoCode, string[]>();
    const declarations = file
      .getImportDeclarations()
      .filter(
        (d) =>
          d.getModuleSpecifierValue() === CORE &&
          !isMarked(d) &&
          !d.getNamespaceImport(),
      );
    const missing = unboundNames(file);
    const introduced = new Set<string>();
    const kept = new Map(
      declarations.map((d) => [
        d,
        migrateSpecifiers(file, d, edits, removed, introduced),
      ]),
    );

    // Names earlier transforms wrote, and names this one introduced, join the first value import.
    const bound = new Set(
      [...kept.values()].flat().map((text) =>
        text
          .replace(/^type /, '')
          .split(' as ')
          .pop(),
      ),
    );
    for (const name of introduced)
      if (!bound.has(name) && !missing.has(name)) missing.set(name, 'value');
    const host = declarations.find((d) => !d.isTypeOnly());
    const additions = [...missing]
      .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
      .filter(([name]) => !bound.has(name));
    if (host)
      kept
        .get(host)
        ?.push(
          ...additions.map(([name, kind]) =>
            kind === 'type' ? `type ${name}` : name,
          ),
        );
    else
      for (const [name, kind] of additions)
        edits.addImport(CORE, name, kind === 'type');

    for (const [declaration, names] of kept) {
      if (names.length === 0) edits.removeStatement(declaration);
      else
        edits.replace(
          declaration,
          renderImport(names, declaration.isTypeOnly()),
        );
    }

    for (const declaration of file.getExportDeclarations()) {
      if (declaration.getModuleSpecifierValue() !== CORE) continue;
      for (const specifier of declaration.getNamedExports()) {
        const rename = RENAMES.get(specifier.getName());
        const code = REMOVED.get(specifier.getName());
        if (rename) edits.replace(specifier.getNameNode(), rename.to);
        else if (code)
          context.todo(
            declaration,
            code,
            `@nexusdi/core 0.4 does not export ${specifier.getName()}`,
          );
      }
    }

    // Removed names still in use keep an import of their own, under a marker, above the first @nexusdi/core import.
    const anchor = declarations[0];
    for (const [code, names] of removed) {
      const reason = `@nexusdi/core 0.4 does not export ${names.join(', ')}`;
      if (anchor)
        edits.insert(
          anchor.getStart(),
          `${todoMarker(code, reason)}\n${renderImport(names, false)}\n`,
        );
      context.todoInText(file.getFilePath(), code, reason);
    }

    const literals = [
      ...file.getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.StringLiteral),
      ...file.getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.NoSubstitutionTemplateLiteral),
      ...file.getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.RegularExpressionLiteral),
    ];
    for (const literal of literals) {
      if (literal.getFirstAncestorByKind(SyntaxKind.ImportDeclaration))
        continue;
      const fragment = OLD_MESSAGES.find((m) => literal.getText().includes(m));
      if (fragment)
        context.todo(
          literal,
          'error-message-match',
          `"${fragment}" is 0.3 error text; 0.4 messages start with a code, so match error.code`,
        );
    }
  },
};
```

```ts
import type { Migration, Transform } from '../../transform.js';
import { createState, type Migration04State } from './state.js';
import { imports } from './transforms/imports.js';

/**
 * The 0.4 transform ids in the order the spec fixes. Later transforms read
 * the output of earlier ones, so the order is part of the contract.
 */
export const TRANSFORM_IDS = [
  'symbol-tokens',
  'services',
  'property-injection',
  'module-config',
  'container-bootstrap',
  'module-exports',
  'await-get',
  'container-leftovers',
  'imports',
  'tsconfig',
] as const;

const transforms: readonly Transform<Migration04State>[] = [imports];

export const migration04: Migration<Migration04State> = {
  version: '0.4',
  transforms,
  createState,
};
```

### Task 13: The `services` transform

Files:

- Create: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/services.ts`
- Modify: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/index.ts`
- Test: fixture cases under `__fixtures__/services/`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: `coreCallName`, `declarationKey`, `declarationOf` (Task 7); `indentOf`, `TextEdits` (Task 4); `legacyDecoratorsInUse`, `Migration04State`, `ServiceToken` (Task 10).
- Produces: `services: Transform<Migration04State>` with id `services`. It records `context.state.serviceTokens.set('file#Class', ServiceToken[])` for every `@Service(TOKEN)`, `@Provider(TOKEN)` and `{ token }` config, which `module-config` and `container-bootstrap` read (Tasks 16 and 17).

One pass per class, per spec §13.2: `@Service` and `@Provider` become `@Injectable({ deps })`, and a class with `@Inject` or `@Optional` parameters and no class decorator gets one too. A class whose constructor takes no parameters, or has none of its own, loses the decorator: 0.4 builds a parameterless class bare, and a subclass without `@Injectable` inherits its parent's deps. An undecorated class-typed parameter becomes a dep with the `undecorated-param-injected` note, and its `import type` becomes a value import. An undecorated interface or primitive parameter gets `TODO_<name>` in `deps`, which fails to compile at the exact spot, and the `undecorated-param` TODO. A trailing parameter with a default or `?` is left out of `deps`: 0.3.1 passed `undefined`, and 0.4 passes nothing, which applies the same default. `singleton: false`, `scope` and `type` get a TODO each; `eager` is dropped.

```ts
import { Inject, Optional, Service, Token } from '@nexusdi/core';

export interface Logger {
  log(message: string): void;
}

export interface Clock {
  now(): number;
}

export const LOGGER = new Token<Logger>('Logger');
export const CLOCK = new Token<Clock>('Clock');

export class Database {
  query(sql: string): string[] {
    return [sql];
  }
}

/** Reads users. */
@Service()
export class UserRepository {
  constructor(
    @Inject(LOGGER) private readonly logger: Logger,
    @Optional(CLOCK) private readonly clock: Clock | undefined,
    private readonly db: Database,
    private readonly table = 'users',
  ) {}

  all(): string[] {
    this.logger.log(`reading ${this.table} at ${this.clock?.now() ?? 0}`);
    return this.db.query(`select * from ${this.table}`);
  }
}
```

```ts
import { Token, Injectable, optional } from '@nexusdi/core';

export interface Logger {
  log(message: string): void;
}

export interface Clock {
  now(): number;
}

export const LOGGER = new Token<Logger>('Logger');
export const CLOCK = new Token<Clock>('Clock');

export class Database {
  query(sql: string): string[] {
    return [sql];
  }
}

/** Reads users. */
@Injectable({ deps: [LOGGER, optional(CLOCK), Database] })
export class UserRepository {
  constructor(
    private readonly logger: Logger,
    private readonly clock: Clock | undefined,
    private readonly db: Database,
    private readonly table = 'users',
  ) {}

  all(): string[] {
    this.logger.log(`reading ${this.table} at ${this.clock?.now() ?? 0}`);
    return this.db.query(`select * from ${this.table}`);
  }
}
```

```ts
  constructor(private readonly from: string) {}

  send(to: string): void {
    void `${this.from} -> ${to}`;
  }
}

export class NullMailer implements Mailer {
  send(): void {}
}
```

- [ ] Step 3: Write the transform

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/services.ts`:

```ts
import {
  type ClassDeclaration,
  type Decorator,
  type ImportDeclaration,
  Node,
  type ParameterDeclaration,
  type SourceFile,
  SyntaxKind,
} from 'ts-morph';

import {
  coreCallName,
  declarationKey,
  declarationOf,
} from '../../../core-api.js';
import { indentOf, type TextEdits } from '../../../edit/text-edits.js';
import type { MigrationContext, Transform } from '../../../transform.js';
import {
  legacyDecoratorsInUse,
  type Migration04State,
  type ServiceToken,
} from '../state.js';

type Context = MigrationContext<Migration04State>;

const CLASS_DECORATORS = new Set(['Service', 'Provider']);
const PARAM_DECORATORS = new Set(['Inject', 'Optional']);
const CONFIG_KEYS = new Set(['token', 'singleton', 'scope', 'type', 'eager']);

/** What one constructor parameter contributes to `deps`. */
type Dep =
  | { readonly kind: 'token'; readonly text: string }
  | {
      readonly kind: 'injected';
      readonly text: string;
      readonly param: ParameterDeclaration;
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: 'unresolved';
      readonly text: string;
      readonly param: ParameterDeclaration;
    }
  | { readonly kind: 'omitted' };

function tokenOf(expr: Node): ServiceToken {
  const declaration = declarationOf(expr);
  return {
    text: expr.getText(),
    declaration: declaration ? declarationKey(declaration) : undefined,
  };
}

/** Removes `node` and the whitespace after it, so the next token takes its place. */
function removeWithTrailingSpace(edits: TextEdits, node: Node): void {
  const text = node.getSourceFile().getFullText();
  let end = node.getEnd();
  while (end < text.length && /\s/.test(text.charAt(end))) end++;
  edits.replaceRange(node.getStart(), end, '');
}

/** Whether `param` and every parameter after it may be left out of a call. */
function omittableTail(
  params: readonly ParameterDeclaration[],
  index: number,
): boolean {
  return params
    .slice(index)
    .every(
      (p) => p.hasInitializer() || p.hasQuestionToken() || p.isRestParameter(),
    );
}

/**
 * What `params[index]` contributes to `deps`. `typeOnlyClasses` collects
 * the class names a type-only import brings in, which `deps` now needs as
 * values.
 */
function depOf(
  params: readonly ParameterDeclaration[],
  index: number,
  typeOnlyClasses: Set<string>,
): Dep {
  const param = params[index] as ParameterDeclaration;
  const decorators = param
    .getDecorators()
    .filter((d) => PARAM_DECORATORS.has(coreCallName(d) ?? ''));
  const optional = decorators.find((d) => coreCallName(d) === 'Optional');
  const arg = (optional ?? decorators[0])
    ?.getCallExpression()
    ?.getArguments()[0];
  if (arg)
    return {
      kind: 'token',
      text: optional ? `optional(${arg.getText()})` : arg.getText(),
    };

  // 0.3.1 passed undefined to an undecorated parameter; one with a default or `?` at the end keeps that meaning without a dep.
  if (omittableTail(params, index)) return { kind: 'omitted' };

  const typeNode = param.getTypeNode();
  const declaration = param.getType().getSymbol()?.getDeclarations()[0];
  if (
    typeNode &&
    Node.isTypeReference(typeNode) &&
    declaration &&
    Node.isClassDeclaration(declaration)
  ) {
    const name = typeNode.getTypeName().getText();
    typeOnlyClasses.add(name);
    return { kind: 'injected', text: name, param };
  }
  return { kind: 'unresolved', text: `TODO_${param.getName()}`, param };
}

/** Reads `@Service(...)`'s argument: a token, or a config object whose options 0.3.1 ignored. */
function configure(
  cls: ClassDeclaration,
  decorator: Decorator,
  context: Context,
): ServiceToken[] {
  const arg = decorator.getCallExpression()?.getArguments()[0];
  if (!arg) return [];
  const isConfig =
    Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(arg) &&
    arg
      .getProperties()
      .some(
        (p) => Node.isPropertyAssignment(p) && CONFIG_KEYS.has(p.getName()),
      );
  if (!isConfig) return [tokenOf(arg)];
  const tokens: ServiceToken[] = [];
  const name = cls.getName() ?? 'the class';
  for (const property of arg.getProperties()) {
    if (!Node.isPropertyAssignment(property)) continue;
    const value = property.getInitializerOrThrow();
    const key = property.getName();
    if (key === 'token') tokens.push(tokenOf(value));
    if (key === 'singleton' && value.getText() !== 'true') {
      context.todo(
        cls,
        'provider-singleton-false',
        `${name} had singleton: ${value.getText()}, which 0.3.1 ignored and cached anyway; add lifetime: 'transient' to @Injectable for a new instance per get()`,
      );
    }
    if (key === 'scope') {
      context.todo(
        cls,
        'provider-scope-option',
        `${name} had scope: ${value.getText()}, which 0.3.1 ignored; set lifetime in @Injectable`,
      );
    }
    if (key === 'type') {
      context.todo(
        cls,
        'provider-type-option',
        `${name} had type: ${value.getText()}, which 0.3.1 ignored and 0.4 does not have`,
      );
    }
  }
  return tokens;
}

function migrateClass(
  cls: ClassDeclaration,
  context: Context,
  typeOnlyClasses: Set<string>,
): void {
  const decorator = cls
    .getDecorators()
    .find((d) => CLASS_DECORATORS.has(coreCallName(d) ?? ''));
  const ctor =
    cls.getConstructors().find((c) => c.hasBody()) ?? cls.getConstructors()[0];
  const params = ctor?.getParameters() ?? [];
  const decorated = params.some((p) =>
    p.getDecorators().some((d) => PARAM_DECORATORS.has(coreCallName(d) ?? '')),
  );
  if (!decorator && !decorated) return;

  const edits = context.edits(cls.getSourceFile());
  const name = cls.getName() ?? 'the class';
  if (decorator) {
    const tokens = configure(cls, decorator, context);
    if (tokens.length > 0)
      context.state.serviceTokens.set(declarationKey(cls), tokens);
  }

  const deps = params.map((_, i) => depOf(params, i, typeOnlyClasses));
  for (const param of params) {
    for (const d of param.getDecorators()) {
      if (PARAM_DECORATORS.has(coreCallName(d) ?? ''))
        removeWithTrailingSpace(edits, d);
    }
  }

  const listed = deps.flatMap((d) => (d.kind === 'omitted' ? [] : [d.text]));
  const injectable = `@Injectable({ deps: [${listed.join(', ')}] })`;
  // A class with nothing to inject needs no decorator: 0.4 builds a parameterless class bare.
  if (listed.length > 0 && decorator) edits.replace(decorator, injectable);
  else if (listed.length > 0)
    edits.insert(cls.getStart(), `${injectable}\n${indentOf(cls)}`);
  else if (decorator) removeWithTrailingSpace(edits, decorator);

  for (const dep of deps) {
    if (dep.kind === 'injected') {
      context.note(
        dep.param,
        'undecorated-param-injected',
        `${dep.text} was undefined in 0.3.1; 0.4 injects it`,
      );
    }
    if (dep.kind === 'unresolved') {
      context.todo(
        cls,
        'undecorated-param',
        `${name}'s parameter ${dep.param.getName()} had no @Inject and received undefined in 0.3.1; replace ${dep.text} in deps with its token`,
      );
    }
  }
  if (listed.length > 0 && legacyDecoratorsInUse(context)) {
    context.todo(
      cls,
      'legacy-decorators-in-use',
      `the project keeps experimentalDecorators, so @Injectable on ${name} throws NEXUS_LEGACY_DECORATORS; register ${name} with provide(${name}, { deps }) instead`,
    );
  }
}

/**
 * Rewrites each import that brings in one of `names` type-only, so the
 * name is a value: `deps` names the class at runtime.
 */
function promoteImports(
  file: SourceFile,
  names: ReadonlySet<string>,
  context: Context,
): void {
  const edits = context.edits(file);
  const touched = new Set<ImportDeclaration>();
  for (const declaration of file.getImportDeclarations()) {
    for (const specifier of declaration.getNamedImports()) {
      const local = specifier.getAliasNode()?.getText() ?? specifier.getName();
      if (!names.has(local)) continue;
      if (declaration.isTypeOnly()) touched.add(declaration);
      else if (specifier.isTypeOnly())
        edits.replaceRange(
          specifier.getStart(),
          specifier.getNameNode().getStart(),
          '',
        );
    }
  }
  for (const declaration of touched) {
    // `import type { A, Db }` becomes `import { type A, Db }`.
    const specifiers = declaration.getNamedImports().map((s) => {
      const local = s.getAliasNode()?.getText() ?? s.getName();
      return names.has(local) ? s.getText() : `type ${s.getText()}`;
    });
    edits.replace(
      declaration,
      `import { ${specifiers.join(', ')} } from '${declaration.getModuleSpecifierValue()}';`,
    );
  }
}

export const services: Transform<Migration04State> = {
  id: 'services',
  description:
    '@Service and @Provider become @Injectable; constructor @Inject and @Optional become deps.',
  runProject(context) {
    // Read before the first edit: afterwards @Injectable, not yet imported, looks foreign.
    legacyDecoratorsInUse(context);
  },
  run(file, context) {
    const typeOnlyClasses = new Set<string>();
    for (const cls of file.getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.ClassDeclaration))
      migrateClass(cls, context, typeOnlyClasses);
    if (typeOnlyClasses.size > 0)
      promoteImports(file, typeOnlyClasses, context);
  },
};
```

### Task 14: The `property-injection` transform

Files:

- Create: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/property-injection.ts`
- Modify: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/index.ts`
- Test: `__fixtures__/property-injection/inject-property/`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: `coreCallName` (Task 7); `Transform` (Task 5); `Migration04State` (Task 10).
- Produces: `propertyInjection: Transform<Migration04State>` with id `property-injection`.

Every property `@Inject` and `@Optional` gets the `property-injection` TODO and stays as it is. Turning a field into an `accessor` moves it from the instance to a getter and setter on the prototype, which changes `Object.keys`, spreads, JSON output and subclass overrides, and the codemod cannot see which of those the class relies on (spec §13.2).
