- [ ] Step 3: Write the transform

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/property-injection.ts`:

```ts
import { Node, SyntaxKind } from 'ts-morph';

import { coreCallName } from '../../../core-api.js';
import type { Transform } from '../../../transform.js';
import type { Migration04State } from '../state.js';

/**
 * Marks every property `@Inject` and `@Optional` for the user.
 *
 * 0.4 injects properties only through `accessor` fields. Turning a field
 * into an accessor moves it from the instance to a getter and setter on the
 * prototype, which changes `Object.keys`, spreads, JSON output and
 * subclass overrides. The codemod cannot see which of those the class
 * relies on, so it rewrites nothing.
 */
export const propertyInjection: Transform<Migration04State> = {
  id: 'property-injection',
  description:
    'Marks property @Inject and @Optional for a manual move to @Inject(X) accessor.',
  run(file, context) {
    for (const property of file.getDescendantsOfKind(
      SyntaxKind.PropertyDeclaration,
    )) {
      if (property.hasAccessorKeyword()) continue;
      for (const decorator of property.getDecorators()) {
        const name = coreCallName(decorator);
        if (name !== 'Inject' && name !== 'Optional') continue;
        const token =
          decorator.getCallExpression()?.getArguments()[0]?.getText() ?? '';
        const dep = name === 'Optional' ? `optional(${token})` : token;
        const cls = property.getParent();
        const owner = Node.isClassDeclaration(cls)
          ? (cls.getName() ?? 'the class')
          : 'the class';
        context.todo(
          property,
          'property-injection',
          `move ${owner}.${property.getName()} to @Inject(${dep}) accessor ${property.getName()}!: ${property.getTypeNode()?.getText() ?? 'T'}`,
        );
      }
    }
  },
};
```

### Task 15: The `symbol-tokens` transform

Files:

- Create: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/token-sites.ts`, `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/symbol-tokens.ts`
- Modify: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/index.ts`
- Test: fixture cases under `__fixtures__/symbol-tokens/`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: `coreCallName`, `coreExportName`, `coreTypesOf`, `CoreTypes` (Task 7); `renderType` (Task 8); `TextEdits` (Task 4).
- Produces: `type TokenSite`; `classifyTokenSite(ref: Node, types: CoreTypes): TokenSite | undefined`; `symbolTokens: Transform<Migration04State>` with id `symbol-tokens`.

A declaration `Symbol('x')`, `Symbol.for('x')`, `new Token('x')`, `new Token<T>()`, `Token.create` or `Token.createUnique` becomes `new Token<T>('x')`. `T` comes from the explicit type argument or a `Token<T>` annotation; otherwise from the types of the injection sites (constructor and property `@Inject` and `@Optional`, factory `deps`, `configToken` of a `DynamicModule<T>`), and when nothing consumes the token, from its registrations. When the sites disagree or are `any`, the token becomes `new Token<any>(...)` with the `untyped-token` TODO: 0.3's `Token<T = any>` typed an untyped token as `any`, so the output compiles as the input did. A symbol that is also used as a plain value, or a `Symbol.for` key another file reads, keeps its symbol and gets `untyped-token`. `a.equals(b)` becomes `a === b`, `toSymbol()` gets `token-to-symbol`, and `toString()` stays.

- [ ] Step 3: Write the site classifier

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/token-sites.ts`:

```ts
import { Node, type Type } from 'ts-morph';

import { coreCallName, type CoreTypes } from '../../core-api.js';

/**
 * Where a reference to a candidate token sits. A consumer says what type an
 * injection point expects, a provider says what a registration supplies,
 * and a neutral site says nothing about the type.
 */
export type TokenSite =
  | { readonly kind: 'consumer'; readonly type: Type | undefined }
  | { readonly kind: 'provider'; readonly type: Type | undefined }
  | { readonly kind: 'neutral' };

const CONTAINER_METHODS = new Set(['get', 'has', 'set', 'resolve']);

/** The instance type a class expression constructs. */
function instanceTypeOf(node: Node): Type | undefined {
  return node.getType().getConstructSignatures()[0]?.getReturnType();
}

/** The type a provider object `{ useValue | useClass | useFactory }` supplies. */
function providedType(object: Node): Type | undefined {
  if (!Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(object)) return undefined;
  const value = object.getProperty('useValue');
  if (value && Node.isPropertyAssignment(value))
    return value.getInitializerOrThrow().getType().getBaseTypeOfLiteralType();
  const cls = object.getProperty('useClass');
  if (cls && Node.isPropertyAssignment(cls))
    return instanceTypeOf(cls.getInitializerOrThrow());
  const factory = object.getProperty('useFactory');
  if (factory && Node.isPropertyAssignment(factory)) {
    return factory
      .getInitializerOrThrow()
      .getType()
      .getCallSignatures()[0]
      ?.getReturnType();
  }
  return undefined;
}

/** The type the factory parameter at `index` declares, for a deps entry of an inline factory. */
function factoryParamType(deps: Node, index: number): Type | undefined {
  const object = deps.getParent()?.getParent();
  const factory = Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(object)
    ? object.getProperty('useFactory')
    : undefined;
  const fn =
    factory && Node.isPropertyAssignment(factory)
      ? factory.getInitializer()
      : undefined;
  if (!fn || !(Node.isArrowFunction(fn) || Node.isFunctionExpression(fn)))
    return undefined;
  const param = fn.getParameters()[index];
  return param?.getTypeNode() ? param.getType() : undefined;
}

/**
 * Classifies one reference to a candidate token. Returns undefined when the
 * reference uses the value as something other than a token, such as an
 * object key or an argument to an unrelated function.
 */
export function classifyTokenSite(
  ref: Node,
  types: CoreTypes,
): TokenSite | undefined {
  const parent = ref.getParent();
  if (!parent) return undefined;

  if (
    Node.isImportSpecifier(parent) ||
    Node.isExportSpecifier(parent) ||
    Node.isExportAssignment(parent)
  ) {
    return { kind: 'neutral' };
  }
  // 0.4 keeps `token.toString()`, which returns the description.
  if (
    Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(parent) &&
    parent.getExpression() === ref &&
    parent.getName() === 'toString'
  ) {
    return { kind: 'neutral' };
  }

  if (Node.isCallExpression(parent) && parent.getArguments()[0] === ref) {
    const name = coreCallName(parent);
    const decorator = parent.getParent();
    if (Node.isDecorator(decorator)) {
      const target = decorator.getParent();
      if (
        (name === 'Inject' || name === 'Optional') &&
        (Node.isParameterDeclaration(target) ||
          Node.isPropertyDeclaration(target))
      ) {
        const type = target.getType();
        return {
          kind: 'consumer',
          type: name === 'Optional' ? type.getNonNullableType() : type,
        };
      }
      if (name === 'Service' || name === 'Provider')
        return { kind: 'provider', type: instanceTypeOf(target as Node) };
    }
    if (name === 'createModuleConfig') return { kind: 'neutral' };
    const callee = parent.getExpression();
    if (
      Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) &&
      CONTAINER_METHODS.has(callee.getName()) &&
      types.isContainer(callee.getExpression())
    ) {
      const second = parent.getArguments()[1];
      if (callee.getName() !== 'set' || !second) return { kind: 'neutral' };
      return {
        kind: 'provider',
        type: Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(second)
          ? providedType(second)
          : instanceTypeOf(second),
      };
    }
  }

  if (
    Node.isPropertyAssignment(parent) &&
    parent.getInitializer() === ref &&
    parent.getName() === 'token'
  ) {
    return { kind: 'provider', type: providedType(parent.getParentOrThrow()) };
  }

  if (Node.isArrayLiteralExpression(parent)) {
    const property = parent.getParent();
    if (Node.isPropertyAssignment(property)) {
      if (property.getName() === 'exports' || property.getName() === 'imports')
        return { kind: 'neutral' };
      if (property.getName() === 'deps') {
        return {
          kind: 'consumer',
          type: factoryParamType(
            parent,
            parent.getElements().indexOf(ref as never),
          ),
        };
      }
    }
  }

  if (
    Node.isPropertyDeclaration(parent) &&
    parent.getName() === 'configToken'
  ) {
    const cls = parent.getParent();
    const arg = Node.isClassDeclaration(cls)
      ? cls.getExtends()?.getTypeArguments()[0]
      : undefined;
    return { kind: 'consumer', type: arg?.getType() };
  }

  return undefined;
}
```

- [ ] Step 4: Write the transform

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/symbol-tokens.ts`:

```ts
import {
  type CallExpression,
  type Expression,
  Node,
  type SourceFile,
  SyntaxKind,
  type Type,
  type VariableDeclaration,
} from 'ts-morph';

import {
  coreCallName,
  coreExportName,
  coreTypesOf,
} from '../../../core-api.js';
import { renderType } from '../../../edit/render-type.js';
import type { MigrationContext, Transform } from '../../../transform.js';
import type { Migration04State } from '../state.js';
import { classifyTokenSite, type TokenSite } from '../token-sites.js';

type Context = MigrationContext<Migration04State>;

/** What a variable's initializer creates, when it creates a token. */
type Candidate =
  | {
      readonly kind: 'token';
      readonly typeArg: string | undefined;
      readonly description: string | undefined;
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: 'symbol';
      readonly description: string | undefined;
      readonly registry: boolean;
    };

function isGlobalSymbol(expr: Expression): boolean {
  if (!Node.isIdentifier(expr) || expr.getText() !== 'Symbol') return false;
  const declaration = expr.getSymbol()?.getDeclarations()[0];
  return !declaration || declaration.getSourceFile().isDeclarationFile();
}

function stringArg(call: Node): string | undefined {
  if (!Node.isCallExpression(call) && !Node.isNewExpression(call))
    return undefined;
  const first = call.getArguments()[0];
  return first && Node.isStringLiteral(first) ? first.getText() : undefined;
}

function candidateOf(declaration: VariableDeclaration): Candidate | undefined {
  const init = declaration.getInitializer();
  if (!init) return undefined;
  if (Node.isNewExpression(init) && coreCallName(init) === 'Token') {
    return {
      kind: 'token',
      typeArg: init.getTypeArguments()[0]?.getText(),
      description: stringArg(init),
    };
  }
  if (!Node.isCallExpression(init)) return undefined;
  const callee = init.getExpression();
  if (
    Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) &&
    coreExportName(callee.getExpression()) === 'Token'
  ) {
    if (callee.getName() === 'create') {
      return {
        kind: 'token',
        typeArg: init.getTypeArguments()[0]?.getText(),
        description: stringArg(init),
      };
    }
    if (callee.getName() === 'createUnique') {
      return {
        kind: 'token',
        typeArg: init.getTypeArguments()[0]?.getText(),
        description: undefined,
      };
    }
  }
  if (isGlobalSymbol(callee))
    return { kind: 'symbol', description: stringArg(init), registry: false };
  if (
    Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) &&
    callee.getName() === 'for' &&
    isGlobalSymbol(callee.getExpression())
  ) {
    return { kind: 'symbol', description: stringArg(init), registry: true };
  }
  return undefined;
}

/** The `T` of an explicit `Token<T>` annotation on the variable. */
function annotatedTypeArg(
  declaration: VariableDeclaration,
): string | undefined {
  const typeNode = declaration.getTypeNode();
  if (
    !typeNode ||
    !Node.isTypeReference(typeNode) ||
    coreExportName(typeNode.getTypeName()) !== 'Token'
  )
    return undefined;
  return typeNode.getTypeArguments()[0]?.getText();
}

/**
 * The one type every injection site agrees on. Sites that consume the
 * token decide; registrations decide only when nothing consumes it.
 */
function agreedType(sites: readonly TokenSite[]): Type | undefined {
  const of = (kind: 'consumer' | 'provider'): (Type | undefined)[] =>
    sites.flatMap((s) => (s.kind === kind ? [s.type] : []));
  const consumers = of('consumer');
  const types = consumers.length > 0 ? consumers : of('provider');
  if (
    types.length === 0 ||
    types.some((t) => t === undefined || t.isAny() || t.isUnknown())
  )
    return undefined;
  return new Set(types.map((t) => t?.getText())).size === 1
    ? types[0]
    : undefined;
}

/** Whether another `Symbol.for(key)` in the project reads the same global registry entry. */
function sharedRegistryKey(
  declaration: VariableDeclaration,
  key: string | undefined,
): boolean {
  if (key === undefined) return false;
  return declaration
    .getProject()
    .getSourceFiles()
    .some((file) =>
      file.getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.CallExpression).some((call) => {
        const callee = call.getExpression();
        return (
          call !== declaration.getInitializer() &&
          Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) &&
          callee.getName() === 'for' &&
          isGlobalSymbol(callee.getExpression()) &&
          stringArg(call) === key
        );
      }),
    );
}

function rewrite(
  declaration: VariableDeclaration,
  typeArg: string,
  description: string,
  context: Context,
): void {
  const edits = context.edits(declaration.getSourceFile());
  const typeNode = declaration.getTypeNode();
  if (
    typeNode &&
    !(
      Node.isTypeReference(typeNode) &&
      coreExportName(typeNode.getTypeName()) === 'Token'
    )
  ) {
    // `const X: symbol = Symbol('x')`: the annotation would contradict the new initializer.
    edits.replaceRange(
      declaration.getNameNode().getEnd(),
      typeNode.getEnd(),
      '',
    );
  }
  const init = declaration.getInitializerOrThrow();
  const next = `new Token<${typeArg}>(${description})`;
  if (init.getText() !== next) edits.replace(init, next);
}

function migrateDeclaration(
  declaration: VariableDeclaration,
  context: Context,
): void {
  const candidate = candidateOf(declaration);
  if (!candidate) return;
  const name = declaration.getName();
  const description = candidate.description ?? `'${name}'`;
  if (candidate.kind === 'token' && candidate.typeArg) {
    rewrite(declaration, candidate.typeArg, description, context);
    return;
  }

  const nameNode = declaration.getNameNode();
  if (!Node.isIdentifier(nameNode)) return;
  const types = coreTypesOf(context.project);
  const sites: TokenSite[] = [];
  let usedAsValue = false;
  for (const ref of nameNode.findReferencesAsNodes()) {
    if (ref === nameNode) continue;
    const site = classifyTokenSite(ref, types);
    if (site) sites.push(site);
    else usedAsValue = true;
  }

  if (candidate.kind === 'symbol') {
    if (!sites.some((s) => s.kind !== 'neutral')) return;
    if (
      usedAsValue ||
      (candidate.registry &&
        sharedRegistryKey(declaration, candidate.description))
    ) {
      context.todo(
        declaration,
        'untyped-token',
        `${name} is used as a token and as a symbol; declare a separate new Token<T>('${name}') for the token uses`,
      );
      return;
    }
  }

  const explicit = annotatedTypeArg(declaration);
  if (explicit) {
    rewrite(declaration, explicit, description, context);
    return;
  }
  const agreed = agreedType(sites);
  const rendered = agreed
    ? renderType(agreed, context.edits(declaration.getSourceFile()))
    : undefined;
  if (rendered) {
    rewrite(declaration, rendered, description, context);
    return;
  }
  rewrite(declaration, 'any', description, context);
  context.todo(
    declaration,
    'untyped-token',
    `${name} has no type the codemod can read from its injection sites; replace any in new Token<any>(${description})`,
  );
}

function needsParens(call: CallExpression): boolean {
  const parent = call.getParent();
  return (
    Node.isPrefixUnaryExpression(parent) ||
    Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(parent) ||
    Node.isBinaryExpression(parent) ||
    Node.isConditionalExpression(parent) ||
    Node.isAwaitExpression(parent)
  );
}

function migrateTokenMethods(file: SourceFile, context: Context): void {
  const types = coreTypesOf(context.project);
  const edits = context.edits(file);
  const calls = file
    .getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.CallExpression)
    .filter((call) => {
      const callee = call.getExpression();
      return (
        Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) &&
        (callee.getName() === 'equals' || callee.getName() === 'toSymbol') &&
        types.isToken(callee.getExpression())
      );
    });
  // Innermost first: an outer rewrite copies its operands through textOf, which takes the inner edits along.
  for (const call of calls.reverse()) {
    const callee = call.getExpression();
    if (!Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee)) continue;
    if (callee.getName() === 'toSymbol') {
      context.todo(
        call,
        'token-to-symbol',
        `${callee.getExpression().getText()}.toSymbol() has no 0.4 equivalent; compare tokens with ===`,
      );
      continue;
    }
    const other = call.getArguments()[0];
    if (!other) continue;
    const text = `${edits.textOf(callee.getExpression())} === ${edits.textOf(other)}`;
    edits.replace(call, needsParens(call) ? `(${text})` : text);
  }
}

export const symbolTokens: Transform<Migration04State> = {
  id: 'symbol-tokens',
  description:
    'Symbol tokens and the 0.3 Token helpers become new Token<T>(description).',
  run(file, context) {
    migrateTokenMethods(file, context);
    for (const declaration of file.getDescendantsOfKind(
      SyntaxKind.VariableDeclaration,
    )) {
      migrateDeclaration(declaration, context);
    }
  },
};
```

```ts
import type { Migration, Transform } from '../../transform.js';
import { createState, type Migration04State } from './state.js';
import { imports } from './transforms/imports.js';
import { propertyInjection } from './transforms/property-injection.js';
import { services } from './transforms/services.js';
import { symbolTokens } from './transforms/symbol-tokens.js';

/**
 * The 0.4 transform ids in the order the spec fixes. Later transforms read
 * the output of earlier ones, so the order is part of the contract.
 */
export const TRANSFORM_IDS = [
  'symbol-tokens',
  'services',
  'property-injection',
  'module-config',
  'container-bootstrap',
  'module-exports',
  'await-get',
  'container-leftovers',
  'imports',
  'tsconfig',
] as const;

const transforms: readonly Transform<Migration04State>[] = [
  symbolTokens,
  services,
  propertyInjection,
  imports,
];

export const migration04: Migration<Migration04State> = {
  version: '0.4',
  transforms,
  createState,
};
```

### Task 16: The `module-config` transform

Files:

- Create: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/modules.ts`, `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/provider-entries.ts`, `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/module-config.ts`
- Modify: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/index.ts`
- Test: fixture cases under `__fixtures__/module-config/`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: `serviceTokens` from `services` (Task 13); `coreCallName`, `coreExportName`, `declarationKey`, `declarationOf`, `findDeclaration` (Task 7); `planImport` (Task 8); `indentOf`, `reindent`, `TextEdits` (Task 4); `todoMarker` (Task 3).
- Produces: `dynamicModuleKey(expr, state)`, `isModuleDeclaration(node)`, `isModuleExpression(expr, state)` in `modules.ts`; `class ProvidersBuilder(state, bare?)` with `add(entry, token?)` and `build(): BuiltProviders`, `interface ArrayEntry`, `interface EntryIssue`, `classOf(expr)`, `commentsOf(element)`, `renderArray(entries, indent, { inline, issues? })`, `recordIssues(path, issues, context)`, `importKeys(file, keys, context): string[]` in `provider-entries.ts`; `moduleConfig: Transform<Migration04State>` with id `module-config`. It records `context.state.dynamicModules.set('file#Name', convertible)`, which `container-bootstrap` reads.

`@Module` provider objects become `provide()` calls, and a class `services` recorded with a token becomes the class plus `provide(TOKEN, { useExisting: Class })`, with an import of the token. When two tokens register one class, the earlier one becomes `provide(T, { useClass: C, deps })`: 0.3.1 built one instance per token and aliased the class to the last, and this keeps both. A `DynamicModule` subclass becomes `export const Mod = defineModule({ name, options, imports, providers, exports })` when it has an `@Module({ ... })`, a `configToken` constant and no member other than instance fields; the placeholder provider of its options token goes, because `with()` provides it now. `Mod.config(v)` becomes `Mod.with(v)`, `Mod.configAsync(fn)` becomes `Mod.with({ useFactory: fn, deps: [] })`, `createModuleConfig(Mod, c)` becomes `Mod.with(c)`, and `DynamicModule.forRoot({ ... })` assigned to a const becomes `defineModule({ name, ... })`. The async branch's configurable modules are `@Module` classes with a `configToken` field, and they convert the same way. The TODOs are `dynamic-module-typeof`, `dynamic-module-static`, `dynamic-module-unconfigured`, `dynamic-module-spread` and `dynamic-module-config-api`, as spec §13.2 lists them.

```ts
import { Inject, Module, Service, Token } from '@nexusdi/core';

export interface Logger {
  log(message: string): void;
}

export const LOGGER = new Token<Logger>('Logger');
export const LEVEL = new Token<string>('Level');
export const PREFIX = new Token<string>('Prefix');

@Service(LOGGER)
export class ConsoleLogger implements Logger {
  constructor(@Inject(LEVEL) private readonly level: string) {}

  log(message: string): void {
    console.log(`[${this.level}] ${message}`);
  }
}

@Module({
  providers: [
    ConsoleLogger, // the default logger
    { token: LEVEL, useValue: 'info' },
    // derived from the level
    { token: PREFIX, useFactory: (level: string) => `${level}:`, deps: [LEVEL] },
  ],
  exports: [LOGGER],
})
export class LoggingModule {}
```

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/__fixtures__/module-config/provider-objects/output.ts`:

```ts
import { Module, Token, Injectable, provide } from '@nexusdi/core';

export interface Logger {
  log(message: string): void;
}

export const LOGGER = new Token<Logger>('Logger');
export const LEVEL = new Token<string>('Level');
export const PREFIX = new Token<string>('Prefix');

@Injectable({ deps: [LEVEL] })
export class ConsoleLogger implements Logger {
  constructor(private readonly level: string) {}

  log(message: string): void {
    console.log(`[${this.level}] ${message}`);
  }
}

@Module({
  providers: [
    ConsoleLogger, // the default logger
    provide(LOGGER, { useExisting: ConsoleLogger }),
    provide(LEVEL, { useValue: 'info' }),
    // derived from the level
    provide(PREFIX, { useFactory: (level: string) => `${level}:`, deps: [LEVEL] }),
  ],
  exports: [LOGGER],
})
export class LoggingModule {}
```

```ts
import { Module, Token, Injectable, provide } from '@nexusdi/core';

export interface Store {
  name: string;
}

export const PRIMARY = new Token<Store>('Primary');
export const REPLICA = new Token<Store>('Replica');
export const NAME = new Token<string>('Name');

@Injectable({ deps: [NAME] })
export class MemoryStore implements Store {
  constructor(readonly name: string) {}
}

  providers: [
    provide(NAME, { useValue: 'memory' }),
    MemoryStore,
    provide(PRIMARY, { useClass: MemoryStore, deps: [NAME] }),

})
```

```ts
import { DynamicModule, Inject, Module, Service, Token } from '@nexusdi/core';

export interface DatabaseConfig {
  host: string;
}

export const DATABASE_CONFIG = new Token<DatabaseConfig>('DatabaseConfig');
export const DATABASE = new Token<Database>('Database');

@Service(DATABASE)
export class Database {
  constructor(@Inject(DATABASE_CONFIG) readonly config: DatabaseConfig) {}
}

/** Connects to the database. */
@Module({
  providers: [Database, { token: DATABASE_CONFIG, useValue: { host: '' } }],
  exports: [DATABASE],
})
export class DatabaseModule extends DynamicModule<DatabaseConfig> {
  protected readonly configToken = DATABASE_CONFIG;
  protected readonly moduleConfig = { providers: [Database] };
}
```

```ts
import { Module } from '@nexusdi/core';
import { DatabaseModule } from './database.module';

export const local = DatabaseModule.with({ host: 'localhost' });
export const remote = DatabaseModule.with({ useFactory: async () => ({ host: 'db' }), deps: [] });
// TODO(nexusdi-0.4): a spread of DatabaseModule.config() has no 0.4 equivalent; import DatabaseModule.with(...) instead (dynamic-module-spread)
export const spread = { ...DatabaseModule.with({ host: 'spread' }) };
// TODO(nexusdi-0.4): DatabaseModule.getConfigToken() has no 0.4 equivalent (dynamic-module-config-api)
export const token = DatabaseModule.getConfigToken();
// TODO(nexusdi-0.4): typeof DatabaseModule now names the type of a ModuleDefinition const (dynamic-module-typeof)
export type DatabaseModuleType = typeof DatabaseModule;

@Module({
  // TODO(nexusdi-0.4): DatabaseModule is imported without with(); 0.4 reports NEXUS_MODULE_OPTIONS_MISSING (dynamic-module-unconfigured)
  imports: [DatabaseModule],
})
export class AppModule {}
```

```ts
import { Token, defineModule, Injectable, provide } from '@nexusdi/core';

export interface DatabaseConfig {
  host: string;
}

export const DATABASE_CONFIG = new Token<DatabaseConfig>('DatabaseConfig');
export const DATABASE = new Token<Database>('Database');

@Injectable({ deps: [DATABASE_CONFIG] })
export class Database {
  constructor(readonly config: DatabaseConfig) {}
}

/** Connects to the database. */
export const DatabaseModule = defineModule({
  name: 'DatabaseModule',
  options: DATABASE_CONFIG,
  providers: [
    Database,
    provide(DATABASE, { useExisting: Database }),
  ],
  exports: [DATABASE],
});
```

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/modules.ts`:

```ts
import { Node } from 'ts-morph';

import { coreCallName, declarationKey, declarationOf } from '../../core-api.js';
import type { Migration04State } from './state.js';

/**
 * The key of the `DynamicModule` subclass an expression names. Keyed by
 * file and name, so it still matches once the class has become a const.
 */
export function dynamicModuleKey(
  expr: Node,
  state: Migration04State,
): string | undefined {
  const declaration = declarationOf(expr);
  if (!declaration) return undefined;
  const key = declarationKey(declaration);
  return state.dynamicModules.has(key) ? key : undefined;
}

/** Whether `declaration` defines a module: an `@Module` class or a `defineModule` const. */
export function isModuleDeclaration(declaration: Node | undefined): boolean {
  if (!declaration) return false;
  if (Node.isClassDeclaration(declaration)) {
    return declaration
      .getDecorators()
      .some((d) => coreCallName(d) === 'Module' || d.getName() === 'Module');
  }
  if (Node.isVariableDeclaration(declaration)) {
    const init = declaration.getInitializer();
    return (
      init !== undefined &&
      Node.isCallExpression(init) &&
      (coreCallName(init) === 'defineModule' ||
        init.getExpression().getText() === 'defineModule')
    );
  }
  return false;
}

/** Whether `expr` is a module reference: a module, or `Mod.with(...)` of one. */
export function isModuleExpression(
  expr: Node,
  state: Migration04State,
): boolean {
  if (Node.isCallExpression(expr)) {
    const callee = expr.getExpression();
    return (
      Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) &&
      callee.getName() === 'with' &&
      isModuleExpression(callee.getExpression(), state)
    );
  }
  return (
    isModuleDeclaration(declarationOf(expr)) ||
    dynamicModuleKey(expr, state) !== undefined
  );
}
```

- [ ] Step 4: Write the providers builder

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/provider-entries.ts`:

```ts
import {
  type ClassDeclaration,
  type Expression,
  Node,
  type SourceFile,
} from 'ts-morph';

import type { TodoCode } from '../../codes.js';
import {
  coreCallName,
  declarationKey,
  declarationOf,
  findDeclaration,
} from '../../core-api.js';
import { planImport } from '../../edit/imports.js';
import { todoMarker } from '../../markers.js';
import type { MigrationContext } from '../../transform.js';
import type { Migration04State } from './state.js';

/** A TODO a built entry needs, by the index of the entry in the output. */
export interface EntryIssue {
  readonly index: number;
  readonly code: TodoCode;
  readonly reason: string;
}

/** One entry of a rendered array, with the comments its source element carried. */
export interface ArrayEntry {
  readonly text: string;
  readonly leading: readonly string[];
  readonly trailing: string | undefined;
}

/** The class declaration an expression names, when it names one. */
export function classOf(expr: Node): ClassDeclaration | undefined {
  const declaration = declarationOf(expr);
  return declaration && Node.isClassDeclaration(declaration)
    ? declaration
    : undefined;
}

/** The comments on the lines above an array element, and the `//` comment after its comma. */
export function commentsOf(element: Node): {
  leading: string[];
  trailing: string | undefined;
} {
  const leading = element
    .getLeadingCommentRanges()
    .map((range) => range.getText());
  const text = element.getSourceFile().getFullText();
  const rest = /^[ \t]*,?[ \t]*(\/\/[^\n]*)/.exec(text.slice(element.getEnd()));
  return { leading, trailing: rest?.[1] };
}

/** The `deps` array `@Injectable` gives `cls`, or its parent's when `cls` has no constructor of its own. */
function depsArrayOf(cls: ClassDeclaration): Node | undefined {
  const decorator = cls
    .getDecorators()
    .find((d) => d.getName() === 'Injectable');
  const options = decorator?.getCallExpression()?.getArguments()[0];
  if (options && Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(options)) {
    const deps = options.getProperty('deps');
    return deps && Node.isPropertyAssignment(deps)
      ? deps.getInitializer()
      : undefined;
  }
  const base = cls.getBaseClass();
  return cls.getConstructors().length === 0 && base
    ? depsArrayOf(base)
    : undefined;
}

/** Whether 0.4 can build `cls` bare: it takes no required parameters or carries @Injectable. */
function buildable(cls: ClassDeclaration): boolean {
  if (depsArrayOf(cls)) return true;
  const ctor =
    cls.getConstructors().find((c) => c.hasBody()) ?? cls.getConstructors()[0];
  if (!ctor) {
    const base = cls.getBaseClass();
    return base ? buildable(base) : true;
  }
  return ctor
    .getParameters()
    .every(
      (p) => p.hasInitializer() || p.hasQuestionToken() || p.isRestParameter(),
    );
}

/** The text of `name` in a provider object, including the method form `useFactory() {}`. */
function propertyText(object: Node, name: string): string | undefined {
  if (!Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(object)) return undefined;
  const property = object.getProperty(name);
  if (property && Node.isPropertyAssignment(property))
    return property.getInitializerOrThrow().getText();
  if (property && Node.isShorthandPropertyAssignment(property))
    return property.getName();
  if (property && Node.isMethodDeclaration(property)) {
    const params = property
      .getParameters()
      .map((p) => p.getText())
      .join(', ');
    return `${property.isAsync() ? 'async ' : ''}(${params}) => ${property.getBody()?.getText() ?? '{}'}`;
  }
  return undefined;
}

type Comments = ReturnType<typeof commentsOf>;
const NO_COMMENTS: Comments = { leading: [], trailing: undefined };

type Item =
  | {
      readonly kind: 'text';
      readonly text: string;
      readonly token: string | undefined;
      readonly comments: Comments;
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: 'class';
      readonly name: string;
      readonly cls: ClassDeclaration | undefined;
      readonly comments: Comments;
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: 'alias';
      readonly token: string;
      readonly name: string;
      readonly cls: ClassDeclaration | undefined;
    };

/** What a builder produced: the entries, their TODOs, and the declarations the module file must import. */
export interface BuiltProviders {
  readonly entries: ArrayEntry[];
  readonly issues: EntryIssue[];
  /** `file#name` keys of declarations the entries name that the source list did not. */
  readonly imports: Set<string>;
}

/**
 * Builds a 0.4 `providers` list from 0.3 registrations, keeping 0.3.1's
 * instances.
 *
 * 0.3.1 registered `{ token: T, useClass: C }` and `@Service(T) class C`
 * under T, and aliased C to the token registered last. So `get(C)` and
 * `get(T)` return one instance, and a second token for C builds a second
 * instance. The 0.4 form: C listed bare, the last token as
 * `provide(T, { useExisting: C })`, and every earlier token as
 * `provide(T, { useClass: C, deps })` with C's own deps.
 */
export class ProvidersBuilder {
  private readonly items: Item[] = [];
  private readonly state: Migration04State;
  private readonly bareElsewhere: Set<string>;

  /** `bare` holds the class names the source list names bare, which keep their own position. */
  constructor(state: Migration04State, bare: Iterable<string> = []) {
    this.state = state;
    this.bareElsewhere = new Set(bare);
  }

  private listClass(
    expr: Expression,
    bareElement: boolean,
    comments: Comments,
  ): void {
    const name = expr.getText();
    const cls = classOf(expr);
    const listed = this.items.some(
      (i) => i.kind === 'class' && i.name === name,
    );
    if ((bareElement || !this.bareElsewhere.has(name)) && !listed)
      this.items.push({ kind: 'class', name, cls, comments });
    for (const token of cls
      ? (this.state.serviceTokens.get(declarationKey(cls)) ?? [])
      : [])
      this.alias(token.text, name, cls);
  }

  private alias(
    token: string,
    name: string,
    cls: ClassDeclaration | undefined,
  ): void {
    if (
      this.items.some(
        (i) => i.kind === 'alias' && i.token === token && i.name === name,
      )
    )
      return;
    this.items.push({ kind: 'alias', token, name, cls });
  }

  private text(
    text: string,
    token: string | undefined,
    comments: Comments,
  ): void {
    this.items.push({ kind: 'text', text, token, comments });
  }

  /**
   * Adds one registration. `token` is set when the registration names its
   * token outside the object, as `set(TOKEN, { useValue })` does. An
   * element of a source array keeps its comments.
   */
  add(entry: Expression, token?: string): void {
    const comments = Node.isArrayLiteralExpression(entry.getParent())
      ? commentsOf(entry)
      : NO_COMMENTS;
    if (
      Node.isCallExpression(entry) &&
      (coreCallName(entry) === 'provide' ||
        entry.getExpression().getText() === 'provide')
    ) {
      this.text(entry.getText(), entry.getArguments()[0]?.getText(), comments);
      return;
    }
    if (!Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(entry)) {
      if (!classOf(entry)) this.text(entry.getText(), undefined, comments);
      else if (token) {
        this.listClass(entry, false, NO_COMMENTS);
        this.alias(token, entry.getText(), classOf(entry));
      } else {
        this.listClass(entry, true, comments);
      }
      return;
    }
    const tokenText = token ?? propertyText(entry, 'token');
    const useClass = entry.getProperty('useClass');
    const useValue = propertyText(entry, 'useValue');
    const useFactory = propertyText(entry, 'useFactory');
    if (!tokenText) {
      this.text(entry.getText(), undefined, comments);
    } else if (useClass && Node.isPropertyAssignment(useClass)) {
      const cls = useClass.getInitializerOrThrow();
      this.listClass(cls, false, NO_COMMENTS);
      this.alias(tokenText, cls.getText(), classOf(cls));
    } else if (useValue !== undefined) {
      this.text(
        `provide(${tokenText}, { useValue: ${useValue} })`,
        tokenText,
        comments,
      );
    } else if (useFactory !== undefined) {
      this.text(
        `provide(${tokenText}, { useFactory: ${useFactory}, deps: ${propertyText(entry, 'deps') ?? '[]'} })`,
        tokenText,
        comments,
      );
    } else {
      this.text(entry.getText(), undefined, comments);
    }
  }

  build(): BuiltProviders {
    const entries: ArrayEntry[] = [];
    const tokens: (string | undefined)[] = [];
    const issues: EntryIssue[] = [];
    const imports = new Set<string>();
    const lastAlias = new Map<string, Item>();
    for (const item of this.items)
      if (item.kind === 'alias') lastAlias.set(item.name, item);

    for (const item of this.items) {
      if (item.kind === 'text') {
        entries.push({ text: item.text, ...item.comments });
        tokens.push(item.token);
        continue;
      }
      if (item.kind === 'class') {
        entries.push({ text: item.name, ...item.comments });
        tokens.push(item.name);
        if (item.cls && !buildable(item.cls)) {
          issues.push({
            index: entries.length - 1,
            code: 'undecorated-param',
            reason: `${item.name}'s constructor parameters had no @Inject and received undefined in 0.3.1; give it @Injectable({ deps })`,
          });
        }
        continue;
      }
      for (const token of item.cls
        ? (this.state.serviceTokens.get(declarationKey(item.cls)) ?? [])
        : []) {
        if (token.text === item.token && token.declaration)
          imports.add(token.declaration);
      }
      tokens.push(item.token);
      if (lastAlias.get(item.name) === item) {
        entries.push({
          text: `provide(${item.token}, { useExisting: ${item.name} })`,
          ...NO_COMMENTS,
        });
        continue;
      }
      const deps = item.cls ? depsArrayOf(item.cls) : undefined;
      entries.push({
        text: `provide(${item.token}, { useClass: ${item.name}${deps ? `, deps: ${deps.getText()}` : ''} })`,
        ...NO_COMMENTS,
      });
      for (const element of deps && Node.isArrayLiteralExpression(deps)
        ? deps.getElements()
        : []) {
        const target = Node.isCallExpression(element)
          ? element.getArguments()[0]
          : element;
        const declaration = target ? declarationOf(target) : undefined;
        if (declaration) imports.add(declarationKey(declaration));
      }
    }

    // 0.3.1 kept the last registration of a token; 0.4 rejects two providers of one token in one module.
    tokens.forEach((token, index) => {
      if (token !== undefined && tokens.indexOf(token, index + 1) !== -1) {
        issues.push({
          index,
          code: 'dynamic-module-graph',
          reason: `${token} is registered again below; 0.3.1 kept the last registration and 0.4 reports NEXUS_DUPLICATE_PROVIDER, so remove this one`,
        });
      }
    });
    // A second run finds the markers of the first among an entry's comments and adds none.
    const fresh = issues.filter(
      (i) => !entries[i.index]?.leading.includes(todoMarker(i.code, i.reason)),
    );
    return { entries, issues: fresh, imports };
  }
}

function toEntry(entry: string | ArrayEntry): ArrayEntry {
  return typeof entry === 'string'
    ? { text: entry, leading: [], trailing: undefined }
    : entry;
}

/**
 * Renders an array literal on a line indented `indent`: on one line when
 * `inline` is set and no entry has a comment or a TODO, otherwise one entry
 * per line, with its comments and TODO markers.
 */
export function renderArray(
  entries: readonly (string | ArrayEntry)[],
  indent: string,
  options: { inline: boolean; issues?: readonly EntryIssue[] },
): string {
  const all = entries.map(toEntry);
  const issues = options.issues ?? [];
  if (all.length === 0) return '[]';
  const commented = all.some(
    (e) => e.leading.length > 0 || e.trailing !== undefined,
  );
  if (options.inline && issues.length === 0 && !commented)
    return `[${all.map((e) => e.text).join(', ')}]`;
  const inner = `${indent}  `;
  const lines = all.flatMap((entry, index) => [
    ...entry.leading.map((comment) => `${inner}${comment}`),
    ...issues
      .filter((i) => i.index === index)
      .map((i) => `${inner}${todoMarker(i.code, i.reason)}`),
    `${inner}${entry.text},${entry.trailing ? ` ${entry.trailing}` : ''}`,
  ]);
  return `[\n${lines.join('\n')}\n${indent}]`;
}

/** Records the report entries for TODO markers `renderArray` wrote into a file. */
export function recordIssues(
  path: string,
  issues: readonly EntryIssue[],
  context: MigrationContext<Migration04State>,
): void {
  for (const issue of issues)
    context.todoInText(path, issue.code, issue.reason);
}

/**
 * Plans imports into `file` of every declaration `keys` names, and returns
 * the names it cannot import: those whose own file does not export them.
 */
export function importKeys(
  file: SourceFile,
  keys: Iterable<string>,
  context: MigrationContext<Migration04State>,
): string[] {
  const missing: string[] = [];
  for (const key of keys) {
    const declaration = findDeclaration(context.files, key);
    if (declaration && planImport(context.edits(file), declaration)) continue;
    missing.push(key.slice(key.lastIndexOf('#') + 1));
  }
  return missing;
}
```

- [ ] Step 5: Write the transform

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/module-config.ts`:

```ts
import {
  type CallExpression,
  type ClassDeclaration,
  Node,
  type ObjectLiteralExpression,
  type SourceFile,
  SyntaxKind,
} from 'ts-morph';

import {
  coreCallName,
  coreExportName,
  declarationKey,
} from '../../../core-api.js';
import {
  indentOf,
  reindent,
  type TextEdits,
} from '../../../edit/text-edits.js';
import type { MigrationContext, Transform } from '../../../transform.js';
import { dynamicModuleKey } from '../modules.js';
import {
  type BuiltProviders,
  classOf,
  importKeys,
  ProvidersBuilder,
  recordIssues,
  renderArray,
} from '../provider-entries.js';
import type { Migration04State } from '../state.js';

type Context = MigrationContext<Migration04State>;

/**
 * A configurable 0.3 module: a subclass of 0.3.1's `DynamicModule`, or an
 * async-branch `@Module` class that declares a `configToken` field, which
 * `createModuleConfig` read.
 */
function isDynamicModuleClass(cls: ClassDeclaration): boolean {
  const base = cls.getExtends()?.getExpression();
  if (base !== undefined && coreExportName(base) === 'DynamicModule')
    return true;
  return (
    moduleConfigOf(cls) !== undefined &&
    cls.getProperty('configToken') !== undefined
  );
}

function moduleConfigOf(
  cls: ClassDeclaration,
): ObjectLiteralExpression | undefined {
  const decorator = cls
    .getDecorators()
    .find((d) => coreCallName(d) === 'Module');
  const arg = decorator?.getCallExpression()?.getArguments()[0];
  return arg && Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(arg) ? arg : undefined;
}

/**
 * Why module-config cannot turn `cls` into a `defineModule` const, or
 * undefined when it can. It needs an `@Module({ ... })` argument, a
 * `configToken` that names a constant, and no member but instance fields.
 * An instance field is dropped: 0.3.1 read one only through the throwaway
 * instance `getConfigToken()` created.
 */
function blocker(cls: ClassDeclaration): string | undefined {
  if (!moduleConfigOf(cls)) return 'it has no @Module({ ... }) configuration';
  const init = cls.getProperty('configToken')?.getInitializer();
  if (
    !init ||
    (!Node.isIdentifier(init) && !Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(init))
  )
    return 'its configToken is not a Token constant';
  const others = cls
    .getMembers()
    .filter((m) => !(Node.isPropertyDeclaration(m) && !m.isStatic()));
  if (others.length > 0)
    return `it has ${others.map((m) => (Node.hasName(m) ? m.getName() : 'a constructor')).join(', ')}`;
  return undefined;
}

function arrayOf(config: ObjectLiteralExpression, name: string) {
  const property = config.getProperty(name);
  const init =
    property && Node.isPropertyAssignment(property)
      ? property.getInitializer()
      : undefined;
  return init && Node.isArrayLiteralExpression(init) ? init : undefined;
}

/** Converts a 0.3 `providers` array, leaving out the placeholder for `optionsToken`. */
function convertProviders(
  config: ObjectLiteralExpression,
  context: Context,
  optionsToken?: string,
): BuiltProviders | undefined {
  const array = arrayOf(config, 'providers');
  if (!array) return undefined;
  const elements = array.getElements();
  const builder = new ProvidersBuilder(
    context.state,
    elements.filter((e) => classOf(e)).map((e) => e.getText()),
  );
  for (const element of elements) {
    // `{ token: CONFIG, useValue: {} }` stood in until config() appended the real value; with() provides it now.
    const placeholder =
      optionsToken !== undefined &&
      Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(element) &&
      element.getProperty('token')?.getText().replace(/\s/g, '') ===
        `token:${optionsToken}`;
    if (!placeholder) builder.add(element);
  }
  return builder.build();
}

/** Plans the imports the built entries need, and marks those it cannot plan. */
function importFor(
  file: SourceFile,
  built: BuiltProviders,
  context: Context,
  anchor: Node,
): void {
  for (const name of importKeys(file, built.imports, context)) {
    context.todo(
      anchor,
      'dynamic-module-graph',
      `${name} is not exported from its file, so this module cannot import it; export it`,
    );
  }
}

/** Rewrites the `providers` of an `@Module` config in place. */
function rewriteProviders(
  config: ObjectLiteralExpression,
  context: Context,
): void {
  const array = arrayOf(config, 'providers');
  const built = convertProviders(config, context);
  if (!array || !built) return;
  const file = config.getSourceFile();
  const unchanged =
    array
      .getElements()
      .map((e) => e.getText())
      .join('\0') === built.entries.map((e) => e.text).join('\0');
  if (!unchanged || built.issues.length > 0) {
    const inline =
      !array.getText().includes('\n') && built.entries.length === 1;
    context
      .edits(file)
      .replace(
        array,
        renderArray(built.entries, indentOf(array), {
          inline,
          issues: built.issues,
        }),
      );
    recordIssues(file.getFilePath(), built.issues, context);
  }
  importFor(file, built, context, config);
}

/** The text of `config.name`'s initializer, copied to indentation `to`, with the file's pending edits inside it applied. */
function copied(
  edits: TextEdits,
  config: ObjectLiteralExpression,
  name: string,
  to: string,
): string | undefined {
  const property = config.getProperty(name);
  if (!property || !Node.isPropertyAssignment(property)) return undefined;
  return reindent(
    edits.textOf(property.getInitializerOrThrow()),
    indentOf(property),
    to,
  );
}

/** Turns a convertible `DynamicModule` subclass into a `defineModule` const. */
function convertDynamicModule(cls: ClassDeclaration, context: Context): void {
  const file = cls.getSourceFile();
  const edits = context.edits(file);
  const name = cls.getName() ?? 'Module';
  const indent = indentOf(cls);
  const inner = `${indent}  `;
  const config = moduleConfigOf(cls) as ObjectLiteralExpression;
  const optionsToken = cls
    .getPropertyOrThrow('configToken')
    .getInitializerOrThrow()
    .getText();
  const built = convertProviders(config, context, optionsToken);

  const lines = [
    `${inner}name: '${name}',`,
    `${inner}options: ${optionsToken},`,
  ];
  const imports = copied(edits, config, 'imports', inner);
  if (imports) lines.push(`${inner}imports: ${imports},`);
  if (built && built.entries.length > 0) {
    lines.push(
      `${inner}providers: ${renderArray(built.entries, inner, { inline: false, issues: built.issues })},`,
    );
    recordIssues(file.getFilePath(), built.issues, context);
  }
  const exports = copied(edits, config, 'exports', inner);
  if (exports) lines.push(`${inner}exports: ${exports},`);

  const isDefault = cls.isDefaultExport();
  const prefix = cls.isExported() && !isDefault ? 'export ' : '';
  const suffix = isDefault ? `\n${indent}export default ${name};` : '';
  edits.dropWithin(cls);
  edits.replace(
    cls,
    `${prefix}const ${name} = defineModule({\n${lines.join('\n')}\n${indent}});${suffix}`,
  );
  if (built) importFor(file, built, context, cls);
}

function notConverted(site: Node, name: string, context: Context): void {
  context.todo(
    site,
    'dynamic-module-static',
    `${name} stays a DynamicModule class, which 0.4 does not have; convert it to defineModule({ options }) by hand`,
  );
}

/** What `createModuleConfig(Mod, config)` passes to `Mod.with()`. */
function withValue(config: Node, edits: TextEdits): string {
  if (!Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(config)) return edits.textOf(config);
  const value = config.getProperty('useValue');
  if (value && Node.isPropertyAssignment(value))
    return edits.textOf(value.getInitializerOrThrow());
  const factory = config.getProperty('useFactory');
  if (factory && Node.isPropertyAssignment(factory)) {
    const deps = config.getProperty('deps');
    const depsText =
      deps && Node.isPropertyAssignment(deps)
        ? deps.getInitializerOrThrow().getText()
        : '[]';
    return `{ useFactory: ${edits.textOf(factory.getInitializerOrThrow())}, deps: ${depsText} }`;
  }
  return edits.textOf(config);
}

/** `DynamicModule.forRoot({ ... })` assigned to a const becomes `defineModule({ name, ... })`. */
function rewriteForRoot(call: CallExpression, context: Context): void {
  const config = call.getArguments()[0];
  const variable = call.getParent();
  if (
    !config ||
    !Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(config) ||
    !Node.isVariableDeclaration(variable)
  ) {
    context.todo(
      call,
      'dynamic-module-config-api',
      "DynamicModule.forRoot() is gone; write const Name = defineModule({ name: 'Name', ... })",
    );
    return;
  }
  const edits = context.edits(call.getSourceFile());
  edits.replace(call.getExpression(), 'defineModule');
  const first = config.getProperties()[0];
  const name = `name: '${variable.getName()}',`;
  if (first && config.getText().includes('\n'))
    edits.insert(first.getStart(), `${name}\n${indentOf(first)}`);
  else edits.insert(config.getStart() + 1, ` ${name}${first ? '' : ' '}`);
  rewriteProviders(config, context);
}

/** `Mod.config(v)`, `Mod.configAsync(fn)` and `createModuleConfig(Mod, c)` become `Mod.with(...)`. */
function rewriteConfigCall(call: CallExpression, context: Context): void {
  const edits = context.edits(call.getSourceFile());
  const callee = call.getExpression();

  if (coreCallName(call) === 'createModuleConfig') {
    const [target, config] = call.getArguments();
    if (!target || !config) return;
    const key = dynamicModuleKey(target, context.state);
    if (!key) {
      context.todo(
        call,
        'dynamic-module-config-api',
        `createModuleConfig(${target.getText()}, ...) configures a token, not a module; import the module's with(value) instead`,
      );
    } else if (!context.state.dynamicModules.get(key)) {
      notConverted(call, target.getText(), context);
    } else {
      edits.replace(
        call,
        `${target.getText()}.with(${withValue(config, edits)})`,
      );
    }
    return;
  }

  if (!Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee)) return;
  const method = callee.getName();
  if (
    method === 'forRoot' &&
    coreExportName(callee.getExpression()) === 'DynamicModule'
  ) {
    rewriteForRoot(call, context);
    return;
  }
  const key = dynamicModuleKey(callee.getExpression(), context.state);
  if (!key) return;
  const receiver = callee.getExpression().getText();
  if (method === 'getModuleConfig' || method === 'getConfigToken') {
    context.todo(
      call,
      'dynamic-module-config-api',
      `${receiver}.${method}() has no 0.4 equivalent`,
    );
    return;
  }
  if (method !== 'config' && method !== 'configAsync') return;
  if (!context.state.dynamicModules.get(key)) {
    notConverted(call, receiver, context);
    return;
  }
  edits.replace(callee.getNameNode(), 'with');
  const arg = call.getArguments()[0];
  if (method === 'configAsync' && arg) {
    edits.insert(arg.getStart(), '{ useFactory: ');
    edits.insert(arg.getEnd(), ', deps: [] }');
  }
  const parent = call.getParent();
  if (Node.isSpreadElement(parent) || Node.isSpreadAssignment(parent)) {
    context.todo(
      call,
      'dynamic-module-spread',
      `a spread of ${receiver}.${method}() has no 0.4 equivalent; import ${receiver}.with(...) instead`,
    );
  }
}

/** `@Module` arguments of classes that stay classes, whose providers this transform converts in place. */
function moduleConfigs(file: SourceFile): ObjectLiteralExpression[] {
  const configs: ObjectLiteralExpression[] = [];
  for (const cls of file.getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.ClassDeclaration)) {
    if (isDynamicModuleClass(cls)) continue;
    const config = moduleConfigOf(cls);
    if (config) configs.push(config);
  }
  return configs;
}

/** Bare imports of a configurable module, and `typeof` of one. */
function markModuleSites(
  file: SourceFile,
  context: Context,
  converting: readonly ClassDeclaration[],
): void {
  for (const id of file.getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.Identifier)) {
    if (!dynamicModuleKey(id, context.state)) continue;
    // Inside a class this run replaces, a marker would land in text that is going away.
    if (
      converting.some(
        (cls) => id.getStart() >= cls.getStart() && id.getEnd() <= cls.getEnd(),
      )
    )
      continue;
    const parent = id.getParent();
    const holder = parent?.getParent();
    if (
      Node.isArrayLiteralExpression(parent) &&
      Node.isPropertyAssignment(holder) &&
      holder.getName() === 'imports'
    ) {
      context.todo(
        id,
        'dynamic-module-unconfigured',
        `${id.getText()} is imported without with(); 0.4 reports NEXUS_MODULE_OPTIONS_MISSING`,
      );
    }
    if (Node.isTypeQuery(parent)) {
      context.todo(
        id,
        'dynamic-module-typeof',
        `typeof ${id.getText()} now names the type of a ModuleDefinition const`,
      );
    }
  }
}

export const moduleConfig: Transform<Migration04State> = {
  id: 'module-config',
  description:
    '@Module provider objects become provide() calls; DynamicModule subclasses become defineModule with options.',
  runProject(context) {
    // Call sites in every file need each module's fate before any file is rewritten.
    for (const file of context.files) {
      for (const cls of file.getDescendantsOfKind(
        SyntaxKind.ClassDeclaration,
      )) {
        const key = declarationKey(cls);
        if (
          isDynamicModuleClass(cls) &&
          !context.state.dynamicModules.has(key)
        ) {
          context.state.dynamicModules.set(key, blocker(cls) === undefined);
        }
      }
    }
  },
  run(file, context) {
    const classes = file
      .getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.ClassDeclaration)
      .filter(isDynamicModuleClass);
    const converting = classes.filter((cls) => blocker(cls) === undefined);
    // Call sites first, so a class conversion that copies `imports` takes their edits along.
    for (const call of file
      .getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.CallExpression)
      .reverse())
      rewriteConfigCall(call, context);
    for (const cls of classes) {
      const reason = blocker(cls);
      if (reason)
        notConverted(
          cls,
          `${cls.getName() ?? 'The module'} (${reason})`,
          context,
        );
      else convertDynamicModule(cls, context);
    }
    for (const config of moduleConfigs(file)) rewriteProviders(config, context);
    markModuleSites(file, context, converting);
  },
};
```

```ts
import type { Migration, Transform } from '../../transform.js';
import { createState, type Migration04State } from './state.js';
import { imports } from './transforms/imports.js';
import { moduleConfig } from './transforms/module-config.js';
import { propertyInjection } from './transforms/property-injection.js';
import { services } from './transforms/services.js';
import { symbolTokens } from './transforms/symbol-tokens.js';

/**
 * The 0.4 transform ids in the order the spec fixes. Later transforms read
 * the output of earlier ones, so the order is part of the contract.
 */
export const TRANSFORM_IDS = [
  'symbol-tokens',
  'services',
  'property-injection',
  'module-config',
  'container-bootstrap',
  'module-exports',
  'await-get',
  'container-leftovers',
  'imports',
  'tsconfig',
] as const;

const transforms: readonly Transform<Migration04State>[] = [
  symbolTokens,
  services,
  propertyInjection,
  moduleConfig,
  imports,
];

export const migration04: Migration<Migration04State> = {
  version: '0.4',
  transforms,
  createState,
};
```

### Task 17: The `container-bootstrap` transform

Files:

- Create: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/container-bootstrap.ts`
- Modify: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/index.ts`
- Test: fixture cases under `__fixtures__/container-bootstrap/`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: `ProvidersBuilder`, `classOf`, `importKeys`, `recordIssues`, `renderArray` (Task 16); `dynamicModuleKey`, `isModuleExpression` (Task 16); `coreCallName`, `coreTypesOf`, `declarationKey`, `declarationOf` (Task 7); `hasMarker`, `todoMarker` (Task 3); `indentOf`, `reindent` (Task 4).
- Produces: `containerBootstrap: Transform<Migration04State>` with id `container-bootstrap`. It pushes `'file#RootName'` onto `context.state.roots` for every root module it writes, which `module-exports` reads (Task 18).

`const c = new Nexus()` and the `c.set()` and `c.setMany()` statements after it in the same block, up to the first other use of `c`, become `const CRoot = defineModule({ name, imports, providers })` in place of the last `set()`. In an async function, `const c = await Nexus.create(CRoot)` follows it and the `new Nexus()` statement goes. At module level or in a synchronous function, the root module is written and `new Nexus()` keeps the `top-level-container` TODO, because the rewrite needs an `await` there (spec §13.2). `set(Module)` and `set(Mod.with(v))` become imports; `set(Class)`, `set(TOKEN, Impl)`, `set(TOKEN, { useValue | useClass | useFactory })` and `set({ token, ... })` become providers through the `ProvidersBuilder`; `set({ providers, imports })` contributes both. Every other `set()` on a container gets `set-after-startup`, or `dynamic-module-graph` inside a branch or loop. A creation that is not a `const` declaration gets `top-level-container`. A module registered twice gets `dynamic-module-graph` on the earlier registration, because 0.3.1 kept the last and 0.4 imports both.

```ts
import { Inject, Nexus, Service, Token } from '@nexusdi/core';

export const GREETING = new Token<string>('Greeting');

@Service()
export class Greeter {
  constructor(@Inject(GREETING) readonly greeting: string) {}
}

export async function start(): Promise<string> {
  const container = new Nexus();
  // the greeting everyone sees
  container.set(GREETING, { useValue: 'hello' });
  container.set(Greeter);
  return container.get(Greeter).greeting;
}
```

```ts
import { Nexus, Token, defineModule, Injectable, provide } from '@nexusdi/core';

export const GREETING = new Token<string>('Greeting');

@Injectable({ deps: [GREETING] })
export class Greeter {
  constructor(readonly greeting: string) {}
}

export async function start(): Promise<string> {
  // the greeting everyone sees
  const ContainerRoot = defineModule({
    name: 'ContainerRoot',
    providers: [
      provide(GREETING, { useValue: 'hello' }),
      Greeter,
    ],
  });
  const container = await Nexus.create(ContainerRoot);
  return container.get(Greeter).greeting;
}
```

```ts
import { Nexus, Token, defineModule } from '@nexusdi/core';

export const FLAG = new Token<boolean>('Flag');
export const MODE = new Token<string>('Mode');

export function configure(production: boolean): Nexus {
  // TODO(nexusdi-0.4): container is created in a synchronous function; make it async and create it with await Nexus.create(ContainerRoot) (top-level-container)
  const container = new Nexus();
  const ContainerRoot = defineModule({
    name: 'ContainerRoot',
  });
  if (production) {
    // TODO(nexusdi-0.4): this set() runs conditionally; choose the module before Nexus.create and list it in the root module (dynamic-module-graph)
    container.set(MODE, { useValue: 'production' });
  }
  return container;
}

export function late(container: Nexus): boolean {
  // TODO(nexusdi-0.4): container.set() after startup has no 0.4 equivalent; register it in a module, or use createTestingContainer().override() in a test (set-after-startup)
  container.set(FLAG, { useValue: true });
  return container.get(FLAG);
}
```

- [ ] Step 3: Write the transform

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/container-bootstrap.ts`:

```ts
import {
  type CallExpression,
  type Expression,
  Node,
  type PropertyAccessExpression,
  type SourceFile,
  type Statement,
  SyntaxKind,
  type VariableDeclaration,
} from 'ts-morph';

import type { TodoCode } from '../../../codes.js';
import {
  coreCallName,
  coreTypesOf,
  declarationKey,
  declarationOf,
} from '../../../core-api.js';
import { indentOf, reindent } from '../../../edit/text-edits.js';
import { hasMarker, todoMarker } from '../../../markers.js';
import type { MigrationContext, Transform } from '../../../transform.js';
import { dynamicModuleKey, isModuleExpression } from '../modules.js';
import {
  classOf,
  importKeys,
  ProvidersBuilder,
  recordIssues,
  renderArray,
} from '../provider-entries.js';
import type { Migration04State } from '../state.js';

type Context = MigrationContext<Migration04State>;

const REGISTER = new Set(['set', 'setMany']);

/** The `c.set(...)` or `await c.set(...)` call a statement is, for container `name`. */
function registrationOf(
  statement: Statement,
  name: string,
): CallExpression | undefined {
  if (!Node.isExpressionStatement(statement)) return undefined;
  let expr: Expression = statement.getExpression();
  if (Node.isAwaitExpression(expr)) expr = expr.getExpression();
  if (!Node.isCallExpression(expr)) return undefined;
  const callee = expr.getExpression();
  if (
    !Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) ||
    !REGISTER.has(callee.getName())
  )
    return undefined;
  return callee.getExpression().getText() === name ? expr : undefined;
}

function mentions(statement: Statement, name: string): boolean {
  return statement
    .getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.Identifier)
    .some((id) => id.getText() === name);
}

function rootNameFor(file: SourceFile, container: string): string {
  const base = `${container.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}${container.slice(1)}Root`;
  let name = base;
  for (let i = 2; file.getLocal(name); i++) name = `${base}${i}`;
  return name;
}

/** A module config object `{ providers, imports }`, as opposed to a provider object `{ token, ... }`. */
function isModuleConfigObject(expr: Node): boolean {
  return (
    Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(expr) &&
    expr.getProperty('token') === undefined &&
    (expr.getProperty('providers') !== undefined ||
      expr.getProperty('imports') !== undefined)
  );
}

interface RootImport {
  readonly text: string;
  /** The module the entry names, for spotting a module registered twice. */
  readonly module: string | undefined;
  readonly todo?: { readonly code: TodoCode; readonly reason: string };
}

/** The module `Mod` or `Mod.with(...)` names. */
function moduleKeyOf(expr: Expression): string | undefined {
  const target =
    Node.isCallExpression(expr) &&
    Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(expr.getExpression())
      ? (expr.getExpression() as PropertyAccessExpression).getExpression()
      : expr;
  const declaration = declarationOf(target);
  return declaration ? declarationKey(declaration) : undefined;
}

/** The root module's imports and providers, from the `set()` calls that registered them. */
function collect(
  calls: readonly CallExpression[],
  context: Context,
  indent: string,
) {
  const imports: RootImport[] = [];
  const providers = new ProvidersBuilder(context.state);
  const importOf = (
    expr: Expression,
    todo?: RootImport['todo'],
  ): RootImport => {
    const text = reindent(expr.getText(), indentOf(expr), indent);
    if (
      !todo &&
      Node.isIdentifier(expr) &&
      dynamicModuleKey(expr, context.state)
    ) {
      todo = {
        code: 'dynamic-module-unconfigured',
        reason: `${expr.getText()} is imported without with(); 0.4 reports NEXUS_MODULE_OPTIONS_MISSING`,
      };
    }
    return { text, module: moduleKeyOf(expr), todo };
  };
  const one = (arg: Expression): void => {
    if (isModuleConfigObject(arg) && Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(arg)) {
      // `set({ providers, imports })` flattened both into the container.
      for (const key of ['imports', 'providers'] as const) {
        const property = arg.getProperty(key);
        const array =
          property && Node.isPropertyAssignment(property)
            ? property.getInitializer()
            : undefined;
        for (const element of array && Node.isArrayLiteralExpression(array)
          ? array.getElements()
          : []) {
          if (key === 'imports') imports.push(importOf(element));
          else providers.add(element);
        }
      }
    } else if (isModuleExpression(arg, context.state)) {
      imports.push(importOf(arg));
    } else if (Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(arg) || classOf(arg)) {
      providers.add(arg);
    } else {
      imports.push(
        importOf(arg, {
          code: 'dynamic-module-graph',
          reason: `${arg.getText()} was registered with set(); check whether it is a module or a provider`,
        }),
      );
    }
  };
  for (const call of calls) {
    const args = call.getArguments() as Expression[];
    const [first, second] = args;
    if (
      (call.getExpression() as PropertyAccessExpression).getName() === 'setMany'
    )
      args.forEach(one);
    else if (first && second) providers.add(second, first.getText());
    else if (first) one(first);
  }
  // 0.3.1 let a later set() of a module replace an earlier one; 0.4 imports both and reports NEXUS_AMBIGUOUS_PROVIDER.
  const marked = imports.map((entry, index) =>
    entry.module !== undefined &&
    !entry.todo &&
    imports.slice(index + 1).some((later) => later.module === entry.module)
      ? {
          ...entry,
          todo: {
            code: 'dynamic-module-graph' as const,
            reason:
              'this module is registered again below; 0.3.1 kept the last registration, so remove this one',
          },
        }
      : entry,
  );
  return { imports: marked, providers: providers.build() };
}

/**
 * Turns `const c = new Nexus()` and the `c.set()` statements after it into
 * a root module. Returns the `set()` calls it consumed, or undefined when
 * the declaration has a shape the codemod does not rewrite.
 */
function bootstrap(
  creation: VariableDeclaration,
  context: Context,
): CallExpression[] | undefined {
  const statement = creation.getVariableStatement();
  const name = creation.getName();
  const block = statement?.getParent();
  if (
    !statement ||
    statement.getDeclarations().length !== 1 ||
    !(Node.isSourceFile(block) || Node.isBlock(block))
  )
    return undefined;

  const statements = block.getStatements();
  const registrations: { statement: Statement; call: CallExpression }[] = [];
  for (const next of statements.slice(statements.indexOf(statement) + 1)) {
    const call = registrationOf(next, name);
    if (call) registrations.push({ statement: next, call });
    else if (mentions(next, name)) break;
  }

  const file = creation.getSourceFile();
  const path = file.getFilePath();
  const edits = context.edits(file);
  const indent = indentOf(statement);
  const inner = `${indent}  `;
  const rootName = rootNameFor(file, name);
  const { imports, providers } = collect(
    registrations.map((r) => r.call),
    context,
    `${inner}  `,
  );
  const unexported = importKeys(file, providers.imports, context);

  // Every TODO in the generated text is written as a marker line into it and recorded here.
  const heading = unexported.map((missing) => {
    const reason = `${missing} is not exported from its file, so ${rootName} cannot import it; export it`;
    context.todoInText(path, 'dynamic-module-graph', reason);
    return `${todoMarker('dynamic-module-graph', reason)}\n${indent}`;
  });
  const lines = [`${inner}name: '${rootName}',`];
  if (imports.length > 0) {
    const entries = imports.flatMap((i) => {
      if (!i.todo) return [`${inner}  ${i.text},`];
      context.todoInText(path, i.todo.code, i.todo.reason);
      return [
        `${inner}  ${todoMarker(i.todo.code, i.todo.reason)}`,
        `${inner}  ${i.text},`,
      ];
    });
    lines.push(`${inner}imports: [\n${entries.join('\n')}\n${inner}],`);
  }
  if (providers.entries.length > 0) {
    lines.push(
      `${inner}providers: ${renderArray(providers.entries, inner, { inline: false, issues: providers.issues })},`,
    );
    recordIssues(path, providers.issues, context);
  }
  let rootText = `${heading.join('')}const ${rootName} = defineModule({\n${lines.join('\n')}\n${indent}});`;

  const fn = Node.isBlock(block) ? block.getParent() : undefined;
  const isAsync =
    fn !== undefined &&
    (Node.isFunctionDeclaration(fn) ||
      Node.isArrowFunction(fn) ||
      Node.isFunctionExpression(fn) ||
      Node.isMethodDeclaration(fn)) &&
    fn.isAsync();
  if (isAsync) {
    const typeNode = creation.getTypeNode();
    rootText += `\n${indent}${statement.getDeclarationKind()} ${name}${typeNode ? `: ${typeNode.getText()}` : ''} = await Nexus.create(${rootName});`;
    edits.removeStatement(statement);
  }

  // The root takes the place of the last set(); the other set() statements go, and the comments above them stay.
  const last = registrations.at(-1);
  for (const r of registrations.slice(0, -1))
    edits.removeStatement(r.statement);
  if (last) edits.replace(last.statement, rootText);
  else if (isAsync)
    edits.insert(statement.getStart(), `${rootText}\n${indent}`);
  else edits.insert(statement.getEnd(), `\n${indent}${rootText}`);
  context.state.roots.push(`${path}#${rootName}`);

  if (!isAsync) {
    context.todo(
      statement,
      'top-level-container',
      Node.isSourceFile(block)
        ? `${name} is created at module level; create it with await Nexus.create(${rootName}) where top-level await is available`
        : `${name} is created in a synchronous function; make it async and create it with await Nexus.create(${rootName})`,
    );
  }
  return registrations.map((r) => r.call);
}

/** Whether `call` sits inside a branch or a loop. */
function isConditional(call: CallExpression): boolean {
  return (
    call.getFirstAncestor(
      (n) =>
        Node.isIfStatement(n) ||
        Node.isSwitchStatement(n) ||
        Node.isConditionalExpression(n) ||
        Node.isForStatement(n) ||
        Node.isForOfStatement(n) ||
        Node.isForInStatement(n) ||
        Node.isWhileStatement(n) ||
        Node.isDoStatement(n) ||
        Node.isTryStatement(n),
    ) !== undefined
  );
}

export const containerBootstrap: Transform<Migration04State> = {
  id: 'container-bootstrap',
  description:
    'new Nexus() plus set() calls become a root defineModule and await Nexus.create(Root).',
  run(file, context) {
    const types = coreTypesOf(context.project);
    const registrations = file
      .getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.CallExpression)
      .filter((call) => {
        const callee = call.getExpression();
        return (
          Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) &&
          REGISTER.has(callee.getName()) &&
          types.isContainer(callee.getExpression())
        );
      });
    const consumed = new Set<CallExpression>();

    for (const expr of file.getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.NewExpression)) {
      if (
        coreCallName(expr) !== 'Nexus' ||
        hasMarker(expr, 'top-level-container')
      )
        continue;
      const parent = expr.getParent();
      const calls =
        Node.isVariableDeclaration(parent) && parent.getInitializer() === expr
          ? bootstrap(parent, context)
          : undefined;
      if (calls) {
        for (const call of calls) consumed.add(call);
        continue;
      }
      context.todo(
        expr,
        'top-level-container',
        'new Nexus() is gone in 0.4 and the codemod cannot rewrite this creation; build a root module and create the container with await Nexus.create(Root)',
      );
    }

    for (const call of registrations) {
      if (consumed.has(call)) continue;
      const callee = call.getExpression() as PropertyAccessExpression;
      if (isConditional(call)) {
        context.todo(
          call,
          'dynamic-module-graph',
          `this ${callee.getName()}() runs conditionally; choose the module before Nexus.create and list it in the root module`,
        );
      } else {
        context.todo(
          call,
          'set-after-startup',
          `${callee.getText()}() after startup has no 0.4 equivalent; register it in a module, or use createTestingContainer().override() in a test`,
        );
      }
    }
  },
};
```

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/index.ts`:

```ts
import type { Migration, Transform } from '../../transform.js';
import { createState, type Migration04State } from './state.js';
import { containerBootstrap } from './transforms/container-bootstrap.js';
import { imports } from './transforms/imports.js';
import { moduleConfig } from './transforms/module-config.js';
import { propertyInjection } from './transforms/property-injection.js';
import { services } from './transforms/services.js';
import { symbolTokens } from './transforms/symbol-tokens.js';

/**
 * The 0.4 transform ids in the order the spec fixes. Later transforms read
 * the output of earlier ones, so the order is part of the contract.
 */
export const TRANSFORM_IDS = [
  'symbol-tokens',
  'services',
  'property-injection',
  'module-config',
  'container-bootstrap',
  'module-exports',
  'await-get',
  'container-leftovers',
  'imports',
  'tsconfig',
] as const;

const transforms: readonly Transform<Migration04State>[] = [
  symbolTokens,
  services,
  propertyInjection,
  moduleConfig,
  containerBootstrap,
  imports,
];

export const migration04: Migration<Migration04State> = {
  version: '0.4',
  transforms,
  createState,
};
```

### Task 18: The `module-exports` transform and the static module graph

Files:

- Create: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/module-graph.ts`, `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/module-exports.ts`
- Modify: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/state.ts`, `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/index.ts`
- Test: multi-file fixture cases under `__fixtures__/module-exports/`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: `context.state.roots` (Task 17); `isModuleDeclaration` (Task 16); `renderArray` (Task 16); `coreCallName`, `coreTypesOf`, `declarationKey`, `declarationOf`, `findDeclaration` (Task 7); `nameOf`, `planImport` (Task 8); `indentOf`, `TextEdits` (Task 4).
- Produces: `interface Ref { key; text; declaration }`; `interface GraphModule { key; name; file; provides; needs; imports; exports; dynamic; configurable }`; `moduleConfigs(files)`; `class StaticModuleGraph(files)` with `modules`, `providersOf(token)`, `exported(module)`, `visible(module)`, `path(from, to)`; `Migration04State.moduleGraph: StaticModuleGraph | undefined`; `moduleExports: Transform<Migration04State>` with id `module-exports`.

0.3 put every provider of every module in one map; 0.4 encapsulates modules (spec §3.4). The transform reads every `defineModule` and `@Module` as transforms 1 to 5 left them, then, for each dependency of each provider and for each root `get(X)` and `has(X)`, makes `X` visible where it is needed: it exports `X` from the module that provides it and from each module on the import path, or adds the providing module to the importer's `imports`. It writes `module-exports-cycle` when that import would form a cycle, `dynamic-module-unconfigured` when the providing module takes options, and `dynamic-module-graph` for a computed, spread or conditional entry, an ambiguous provider, an export 0.4 rejects, or a token its file does not export. A `get(X)` on a receiver the checker types as `any` counts when `X` is a token a module provides (Review Focus 2): a route loader's container comes from a framework context whose generated types may be missing, and an extra export is harmless while a missing one fails at runtime.
