- [ ] Step 3: Write the graph

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/module-graph.ts`:

```ts
import {
  type ClassDeclaration,
  type Expression,
  Node,
  type ObjectLiteralExpression,
  type SourceFile,
  SyntaxKind,
} from 'ts-morph';

import { coreCallName, declarationKey, declarationOf } from '../../core-api.js';
import { isModuleDeclaration } from './modules.js';

/** A reference to a token or module as the source writes it. */
export interface Ref {
  /** `file#name` of the declaration. */
  readonly key: string;
  /** The text the source uses, such as `LOGGER_TOKEN`. */
  readonly text: string;
  /** The declaration, for importing it into another file. */
  readonly declaration: Node;
}

/** One module the codemod can read statically. */
export interface GraphModule {
  readonly key: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly file: SourceFile;
  /** Tokens it provides, including its own options token. */
  readonly provides: Map<string, Ref>;
  /** Each dependency of each of its providers, with the node that states it. */
  readonly needs: { readonly token: Ref; readonly site: Node }[];
  readonly imports: Map<string, Ref>;
  readonly exports: Map<string, Ref>;
  /** Set when an array is computed, spread or conditional: the codemod edits nothing about this module. */
  readonly dynamic: boolean;
  /** Set when the module takes options, so importing it needs `with()`. */
  readonly configurable: boolean;
}

const MODIFIERS = new Set(['optional', 'lazy', 'all']);

function refOf(expr: Node): Ref | undefined {
  const declaration = declarationOf(expr);
  if (!declaration || Node.isImportSpecifier(declaration)) return undefined;
  return {
    key: declarationKey(declaration),
    text: expr.getText(),
    declaration,
  };
}

/** The token a deps entry names: `X`, `optional(X)`, `lazy(X)` or `all(X)`. */
function depToken(entry: Node): Node {
  if (
    Node.isCallExpression(entry) &&
    MODIFIERS.has(entry.getExpression().getText())
  ) {
    return entry.getArguments()[0] ?? entry;
  }
  return entry;
}

function arrayProperty(
  config: ObjectLiteralExpression,
  name: string,
): { elements: Expression[]; dynamic: boolean } {
  const property = config.getProperty(name);
  if (!property) return { elements: [], dynamic: false };
  if (!Node.isPropertyAssignment(property))
    return { elements: [], dynamic: true };
  const init = property.getInitializer();
  if (!init || !Node.isArrayLiteralExpression(init))
    return { elements: [], dynamic: true };
  const elements = init.getElements();
  return {
    elements,
    dynamic: elements.some(
      (e) => Node.isSpreadElement(e) || Node.isConditionalExpression(e),
    ),
  };
}

/** The deps entries `@Injectable({ deps })` gives a class, following an undecorated subclass to its parent. */
function classDeps(cls: ClassDeclaration): Node[] {
  const decorator = cls
    .getDecorators()
    .find((d) => d.getName() === 'Injectable');
  const options = decorator?.getCallExpression()?.getArguments()[0];
  if (options && Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(options)) {
    const deps = options.getProperty('deps');
    const array =
      deps && Node.isPropertyAssignment(deps)
        ? deps.getInitializer()
        : undefined;
    return array && Node.isArrayLiteralExpression(array)
      ? array.getElements()
      : [];
  }
  const base = cls.getBaseClass();
  return base ? classDeps(base) : [];
}

function providerOf(
  entry: Expression,
): { token: Node; deps: Node[] } | undefined {
  if (
    Node.isCallExpression(entry) &&
    (coreCallName(entry) === 'provide' ||
      entry.getExpression().getText() === 'provide')
  ) {
    const [token, options] = entry.getArguments();
    if (!token) return undefined;
    const deps: Node[] = [];
    if (options && Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(options)) {
      const explicit = options.getProperty('deps');
      const array =
        explicit && Node.isPropertyAssignment(explicit)
          ? explicit.getInitializer()
          : undefined;
      if (array && Node.isArrayLiteralExpression(array))
        deps.push(...array.getElements());
      const existing = options.getProperty('useExisting');
      if (existing && Node.isPropertyAssignment(existing))
        deps.push(existing.getInitializerOrThrow());
      const useClass = options.getProperty('useClass');
      if (!explicit && useClass && Node.isPropertyAssignment(useClass)) {
        const cls = declarationOf(useClass.getInitializerOrThrow());
        if (cls && Node.isClassDeclaration(cls)) deps.push(...classDeps(cls));
      }
    } else {
      const cls = declarationOf(token);
      if (cls && Node.isClassDeclaration(cls)) deps.push(...classDeps(cls));
    }
    return { token, deps };
  }
  const cls = declarationOf(entry);
  if (cls && Node.isClassDeclaration(cls))
    return { token: entry, deps: classDeps(cls) };
  return undefined;
}

/** The module an `imports` entry names: `Mod` or `Mod.with(...)`. */
function importedModule(entry: Expression): Node | undefined {
  if (Node.isCallExpression(entry)) {
    const callee = entry.getExpression();
    if (Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) && callee.getName() === 'with')
      return callee.getExpression();
    return undefined;
  }
  return entry;
}

function readModule(
  key: string,
  name: string,
  config: ObjectLiteralExpression,
): GraphModule {
  const provides = new Map<string, Ref>();
  const needs: GraphModule['needs'][number][] = [];
  const imports = new Map<string, Ref>();
  const exports = new Map<string, Ref>();
  let dynamic = false;

  const options = config.getProperty('options');
  if (options && Node.isPropertyAssignment(options)) {
    const ref = refOf(options.getInitializerOrThrow());
    if (ref) provides.set(ref.key, ref);
  }

  const providers = arrayProperty(config, 'providers');
  dynamic ||= providers.dynamic;
  for (const entry of providers.elements) {
    const provider = providerOf(entry);
    const token = provider ? refOf(provider.token) : undefined;
    if (!provider || !token) {
      dynamic = true;
      continue;
    }
    provides.set(token.key, token);
    for (const dep of provider.deps) {
      const ref = refOf(depToken(dep));
      if (ref) needs.push({ token: ref, site: entry });
    }
  }

  const importList = arrayProperty(config, 'imports');
  dynamic ||= importList.dynamic;
  for (const entry of importList.elements) {
    const target = importedModule(entry);
    const ref = target ? refOf(target) : undefined;
    if (!ref || !isModuleDeclaration(ref.declaration)) {
      dynamic = true;
      continue;
    }
    imports.set(ref.key, ref);
  }

  const exportList = arrayProperty(config, 'exports');
  dynamic ||= exportList.dynamic;
  for (const entry of exportList.elements) {
    const ref = refOf(entry);
    if (ref) exports.set(ref.key, ref);
    else dynamic = true;
  }

  return {
    key,
    name,
    file: config.getSourceFile(),
    provides,
    needs,
    imports,
    exports,
    dynamic,
    configurable: options !== undefined,
  };
}

/** The config object of every module in `files`, by module key. */
export function moduleConfigs(
  files: readonly SourceFile[],
): Map<string, { name: string; config: ObjectLiteralExpression }> {
  const found = new Map<
    string,
    { name: string; config: ObjectLiteralExpression }
  >();
  for (const file of files) {
    for (const declaration of file.getDescendantsOfKind(
      SyntaxKind.VariableDeclaration,
    )) {
      if (!isModuleDeclaration(declaration)) continue;
      const call = declaration.getInitializer();
      const arg =
        call && Node.isCallExpression(call)
          ? call.getArguments()[0]
          : undefined;
      if (arg && Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(arg)) {
        found.set(declarationKey(declaration), {
          name: declaration.getName(),
          config: arg,
        });
      }
    }
    for (const cls of file.getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.ClassDeclaration)) {
      if (!isModuleDeclaration(cls)) continue;
      const decorator = cls
        .getDecorators()
        .find((d) => d.getName() === 'Module');
      const arg = decorator?.getCallExpression()?.getArguments()[0];
      if (arg && Node.isObjectLiteralExpression(arg)) {
        found.set(declarationKey(cls), {
          name: cls.getName() ?? 'Module',
          config: arg,
        });
      }
    }
  }
  return found;
}

/**
 * The module graph as the sources state it after transforms 1 to 5.
 *
 * 0.3 put every provider of every module in one map, so any provider could
 * depend on any other. 0.4 encapsulates modules, and this graph is what
 * module-exports reads to find each dependency that now crosses a module
 * boundary.
 */
export class StaticModuleGraph {
  readonly modules = new Map<string, GraphModule>();

  constructor(files: readonly SourceFile[]) {
    for (const [key, { name, config }] of moduleConfigs(files)) {
      this.modules.set(key, readModule(key, name, config));
    }
  }

  /** The modules whose own providers include `token`. */
  providersOf(token: string): GraphModule[] {
    return [...this.modules.values()].filter((m) => m.provides.has(token));
  }

  /** Everything `module` exports, following re-exported modules. */
  exported(module: GraphModule, seen = new Set<string>()): Set<string> {
    const out = new Set<string>();
    if (seen.has(module.key)) return out;
    seen.add(module.key);
    for (const key of module.exports.keys()) {
      const target = this.modules.get(key);
      if (target && module.imports.has(key)) {
        for (const inner of this.exported(target, seen)) out.add(inner);
      } else {
        out.add(key);
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** The tokens `module` can inject: its own and its imports' exports. */
  visible(module: GraphModule): Set<string> {
    const out = new Set(module.provides.keys());
    for (const key of module.imports.keys()) {
      const imported = this.modules.get(key);
      if (!imported) continue;
      for (const token of this.exported(imported)) out.add(token);
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** The import path from `from` to `to`, both included, or undefined. */
  path(from: GraphModule, to: GraphModule): GraphModule[] | undefined {
    const queue: GraphModule[][] = [[from]];
    const seen = new Set([from.key]);
    while (queue.length > 0) {
      const path = queue.shift() as GraphModule[];
      const last = path.at(-1) as GraphModule;
      if (last.key === to.key) return path;
      for (const key of last.imports.keys()) {
        const next = this.modules.get(key);
        if (!next || seen.has(key)) continue;
        seen.add(key);
        queue.push([...path, next]);
      }
    }
    return undefined;
  }
}
```

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/state.ts`:

```ts
import { SyntaxKind } from 'ts-morph';

import { coreCallName } from '../../core-api.js';
import type { MigrationContext } from '../../transform.js';
import type { StaticModuleGraph } from './module-graph.js';

/** A token named in `@Service(TOKEN)` or `@Provider(TOKEN)`. */
export interface ServiceToken {
  /** The token expression as written, such as `LOGGER_TOKEN`. */
  readonly text: string;
  /** `file#name` of the token's declaration, when it is a named declaration. */
  readonly declaration: string | undefined;
}

/** The state the 0.4 transforms share across files and projects. */
export interface Migration04State {
  /** `file#ClassName` of a service class to the tokens its decorator named. */
  readonly serviceTokens: Map<string, ServiceToken[]>;
  /** `file#ClassName` of each `DynamicModule` subclass to whether module-config converts it. */
  readonly dynamicModules: Map<string, boolean>;
  /** `file#name` of every root module container-bootstrap created. */
  readonly roots: string[];
  /** Whether the project applies decorators that do not come from `@nexusdi/core`. Computed once. */
  legacyDecorators: boolean | undefined;
  /** Built by module-exports from the sources as transforms 1 to 5 left them. */
  moduleGraph: StaticModuleGraph | undefined;
}

export function createState(): Migration04State {
  return {
    serviceTokens: new Map(),
    dynamicModules: new Map(),
    roots: [],
    legacyDecorators: undefined,
    moduleGraph: undefined,
  };
}

/**
 * Whether any file of the run applies a decorator `@nexusdi/core` did not
 * export. Such a project keeps experimentalDecorators, under which the 0.4
 * decorators throw NEXUS_LEGACY_DECORATORS.
 */
export function legacyDecoratorsInUse(
  context: MigrationContext<Migration04State>,
): boolean {
  context.state.legacyDecorators ??= context.files.some((file) =>
    file
      .getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.Decorator)
      .some(
        (d) =>
          coreCallName(d) === undefined && !OWN_DECORATORS.has(d.getName()),
      ),
  );
  return context.state.legacyDecorators;
}

/** The 0.4 decorators transforms write. Before `imports` runs they resolve to nothing. */
const OWN_DECORATORS = new Set(['Injectable', 'Inject', 'Module']);
```

- [ ] Step 5: Write the transform

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/module-exports.ts`:

```ts
import {
  type Expression,
  Node,
  type ObjectLiteralExpression,
  SyntaxKind,
} from 'ts-morph';

import {
  coreTypesOf,
  declarationKey,
  declarationOf,
  findDeclaration,
} from '../../../core-api.js';
import { nameOf, planImport } from '../../../edit/imports.js';
import { indentOf, type TextEdits } from '../../../edit/text-edits.js';
import type { MigrationContext, Transform } from '../../../transform.js';
import {
  type GraphModule,
  moduleConfigs,
  type Ref,
  StaticModuleGraph,
} from '../module-graph.js';
import { renderArray } from '../provider-entries.js';
import type { Migration04State } from '../state.js';

type Context = MigrationContext<Migration04State>;

/** A place that needs `token` visible in `module`. */
interface Request {
  readonly module: GraphModule;
  readonly token: Ref;
  readonly site: Node;
}

/** What module-exports adds to one module. */
interface Additions {
  readonly exports: Ref[];
  readonly imports: Ref[];
}

/** The root module a `get()` or `has()` receiver reads from. */
function rootOf(
  receiver: Expression,
  graph: StaticModuleGraph,
  roots: readonly string[],
): GraphModule | undefined {
  if (Node.isIdentifier(receiver)) {
    for (const declaration of receiver.getSymbol()?.getDeclarations() ?? []) {
      if (!Node.isVariableDeclaration(declaration)) continue;
      const create = declaration
        .getInitializer()
        ?.getFirstDescendantByKind(SyntaxKind.CallExpression);
      const arg =
        create?.getExpression().getText() === 'Nexus.create'
          ? create.getArguments()[0]
          : undefined;
      const root = arg ? declarationOf(arg) : undefined;
      if (root) return graph.modules.get(declarationKey(root));
    }
  }
  return roots.length === 1 ? graph.modules.get(roots[0] as string) : undefined;
}

/** Every root `get(X)` and `has(X)` of a token some module provides. */
function lookupRequests(context: Context, graph: StaticModuleGraph): Request[] {
  const requests: Request[] = [];
  for (const file of context.files) {
    const types = coreTypesOf(file.getProject());
    for (const call of file.getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.CallExpression)) {
      const callee = call.getExpression();
      if (
        !Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) ||
        !['get', 'has'].includes(callee.getName())
      )
        continue;
      const arg = call.getArguments()[0];
      const declaration = arg ? declarationOf(arg) : undefined;
      if (
        !arg ||
        !declaration ||
        graph.providersOf(declarationKey(declaration)).length === 0
      )
        continue;
      // A receiver the checker cannot type, such as a container read from a framework context
      // whose generated types are missing, counts when its argument is a token a module
      // provides: an extra export is harmless, and a missing one fails at runtime.
      const receiver = callee.getExpression();
      const untyped =
        receiver.getType().isAny() || receiver.getType().isUnknown();
      if (!untyped && !types.isContainer(receiver)) continue;
      const root = rootOf(receiver, graph, context.state.roots);
      if (root) {
        requests.push({
          module: root,
          token: {
            key: declarationKey(declaration),
            text: arg.getText(),
            declaration,
          },
          site: call,
        });
      } else if (context.state.roots.length > 1) {
        context.todo(
          call,
          'dynamic-module-graph',
          `the codemod cannot tell which root module this ${callee.getName()}(${arg.getText()}) reads; export ${arg.getText()} to it by hand`,
        );
      }
    }
  }
  return requests;
}

/**
 * Plans the exports and imports that keep each dependency 0.3 resolved
 * across modules visible under 0.4 encapsulation, and marks what it cannot
 * plan. The graph is updated as it goes, so a later request sees what an
 * earlier one added.
 */
function plan(
  graph: StaticModuleGraph,
  requests: readonly Request[],
  context: Context,
): Map<string, Additions> {
  const additions = new Map<string, Additions>();
  const of = (m: GraphModule): Additions => {
    const found = additions.get(m.key) ?? { exports: [], imports: [] };
    additions.set(m.key, found);
    return found;
  };
  const exportFrom = (m: GraphModule, token: Ref): void => {
    if (m.exports.has(token.key)) return;
    m.exports.set(token.key, token);
    of(m).exports.push(token);
  };

  for (const { module, token, site } of requests) {
    if (graph.visible(module).has(token.key)) continue;
    const owners = graph.providersOf(token.key);
    if (owners.length === 0) continue;
    if (owners.length > 1) {
      context.todo(
        site,
        'dynamic-module-graph',
        `${owners.map((o) => o.name).join(' and ')} each provide ${token.text}; import the one ${module.name} needs and export ${token.text} from it`,
      );
      continue;
    }
    const owner = owners[0] as GraphModule;
    if (module.dynamic || owner.dynamic) {
      context.todo(
        site,
        'dynamic-module-graph',
        `${module.dynamic ? module.name : owner.name} has computed entries, so the codemod cannot make ${token.text} visible in ${module.name}`,
      );
      continue;
    }
    const path = graph.path(module, owner);
    if (path) {
      // Every module between the two re-exports the token, so the one that needs it sees it.
      for (const step of path.slice(1)) exportFrom(step, token);
      continue;
    }
    if (graph.path(owner, module)) {
      context.todo(
        site,
        'module-exports-cycle',
        `${module.name} needs ${token.text} from ${owner.name}, which already imports ${module.name}`,
      );
      continue;
    }
    if (owner.configurable) {
      context.todo(
        site,
        'dynamic-module-unconfigured',
        `${module.name} needs ${token.text} from ${owner.name}, which takes options; call ${owner.name}.with() once and import the result in both places`,
      );
      continue;
    }
    const declaration = findDeclaration(context.files, owner.key);
    if (!declaration) continue;
    exportFrom(owner, token);
    const ref: Ref = { key: owner.key, text: owner.name, declaration };
    module.imports.set(owner.key, ref);
    of(module).imports.push(ref);
  }
  return additions;
}

/** Adds `names` to the `property` array of `config`, creating the property when it is absent. */
function append(
  edits: TextEdits,
  config: ObjectLiteralExpression,
  property: 'exports' | 'imports',
  names: readonly string[],
): void {
  const existing = config.getProperty(property);
  if (existing && Node.isPropertyAssignment(existing)) {
    const array = existing.getInitializerOrThrow();
    if (!Node.isArrayLiteralExpression(array)) return;
    const entries = [...array.getElements().map((e) => e.getText()), ...names];
    edits.replace(
      array,
      renderArray(entries, indentOf(existing), {
        inline: !array.getText().includes('\n'),
      }),
    );
    return;
  }
  const last = config.getProperties().at(-1);
  const entry = `${property}: [${names.join(', ')}]`;
  if (!last) {
    edits.insert(config.getStart() + 1, ` ${entry} `);
    return;
  }
  const text = config.getSourceFile().getFullText();
  const comma = text.slice(last.getEnd()).trimStart().startsWith(',');
  const afterLast = comma
    ? text.indexOf(',', last.getEnd()) + 1
    : last.getEnd();
  const multiline = config.getText().includes('\n');
  edits.insert(
    afterLast,
    `${comma ? '' : ','}${multiline ? `\n${indentOf(last)}` : ' '}${entry}${multiline ? ',' : ''}`,
  );
}

function apply(additions: Map<string, Additions>, context: Context): void {
  const configs = moduleConfigs(context.files);
  for (const [key, { exports, imports }] of additions) {
    const found = configs.get(key);
    if (!found) continue;
    const { config, name } = found;
    const edits = context.edits(config.getSourceFile());
    const names = (refs: readonly Ref[]): string[] =>
      refs.flatMap((ref) => {
        const declaration =
          findDeclaration(context.files, ref.key) ?? ref.declaration;
        if (planImport(edits, declaration))
          return [nameOf(declaration) ?? ref.text];
        context.todo(
          config,
          'dynamic-module-graph',
          `${ref.text} is not exported from its file, so ${name} cannot import it; export it`,
        );
        return [];
      });
    const importNames = names(imports);
    const exportNames = names(exports);
    if (importNames.length > 0) append(edits, config, 'imports', importNames);
    if (exportNames.length > 0) append(edits, config, 'exports', exportNames);
  }
}

/** Marks exports 0.4 rejects with NEXUS_INVALID_EXPORT, and modules the codemod cannot read. */
function markModules(graph: StaticModuleGraph, context: Context): void {
  const configs = moduleConfigs(context.files);
  for (const module of graph.modules.values()) {
    const config = configs.get(module.key)?.config;
    if (!config) continue;
    if (module.dynamic) {
      context.todo(
        config,
        'dynamic-module-graph',
        `${module.name} has computed, spread or conditional entries; the codemod adds no exports or imports for it`,
      );
      continue;
    }
    const visible = graph.visible(module);
    const invalid = [...module.exports.values()].filter(
      (ref) => !visible.has(ref.key) && !module.imports.has(ref.key),
    );
    const property = config.getProperty('exports');
    if (invalid.length > 0 && property) {
      context.todo(
        property,
        'dynamic-module-graph',
        `${module.name} exports ${invalid.map((r) => r.text).join(', ')}, which it neither provides nor imports; 0.4 reports NEXUS_INVALID_EXPORT`,
      );
    }
  }
}

export const moduleExports: Transform<Migration04State> = {
  id: 'module-exports',
  description:
    'Adds the exports and imports 0.4 module encapsulation needs for dependencies 0.3 resolved across modules.',
  runProject(context) {
    // The graph spans every project of the run, so the first project's call does the work.
    if (context.state.moduleGraph) return;
    const graph = new StaticModuleGraph(context.files);
    context.state.moduleGraph = graph;
    markModules(graph, context);
    const requests: Request[] = [];
    for (const module of graph.modules.values()) {
      for (const need of module.needs)
        requests.push({ module, token: need.token, site: need.site });
    }
    requests.push(...lookupRequests(context, graph));
    apply(plan(graph, requests, context), context);
  },
};
```

```ts
import type { Migration, Transform } from '../../transform.js';
import { createState, type Migration04State } from './state.js';
import { containerBootstrap } from './transforms/container-bootstrap.js';
import { imports } from './transforms/imports.js';
import { moduleConfig } from './transforms/module-config.js';
import { moduleExports } from './transforms/module-exports.js';
import { propertyInjection } from './transforms/property-injection.js';
import { services } from './transforms/services.js';
import { symbolTokens } from './transforms/symbol-tokens.js';

/**
 * The 0.4 transform ids in the order the spec fixes. Later transforms read
 * the output of earlier ones, so the order is part of the contract.
 */
export const TRANSFORM_IDS = [
  'symbol-tokens',
  'services',
  'property-injection',
  'module-config',
  'container-bootstrap',
  'module-exports',
  'await-get',
  'container-leftovers',
  'imports',
  'tsconfig',
] as const;

const transforms: readonly Transform<Migration04State>[] = [
  symbolTokens,
  services,
  propertyInjection,
  moduleConfig,
  containerBootstrap,
  moduleExports,
  imports,
];

export const migration04: Migration<Migration04State> = {
  version: '0.4',
  transforms,
  createState,
};
```

```ts
import { Nexus, Service } from '@nexusdi/core';

@Service()
export class Engine {
  start(): string {
    return 'started';
  }
}

export async function run(container: Nexus): Promise<string> {
  const engine = await container.get(Engine);
  const again = (await container.get(Engine)).start();
  return engine.start() + again;
}
```

- [ ] Step 3: Write the transform

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/await-get.ts`:

```ts
import { Node, SyntaxKind } from 'ts-morph';

import { coreTypesOf } from '../../../core-api.js';
import type { Transform } from '../../../transform.js';
import type { Migration04State } from '../state.js';

/**
 * `await container.get(X)` becomes `container.get(X)`.
 *
 * The async branch made `get()` return a promise. 0.4's `get()` is
 * synchronous, so the `await` is dead code that keeps its function async
 * for no reason a reader can see.
 */
export const awaitGet: Transform<Migration04State> = {
  id: 'await-get',
  description: 'Drops await from container.get(), which is synchronous in 0.4.',
  run(file, context) {
    const types = coreTypesOf(context.project);
    const edits = context.edits(file);
    const awaits = file
      .getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.AwaitExpression)
      .filter((expr) => {
        const call = expr.getExpression();
        const callee = Node.isCallExpression(call)
          ? call.getExpression()
          : undefined;
        return (
          callee !== undefined &&
          Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) &&
          callee.getName() === 'get' &&
          types.isContainer(callee.getExpression())
        );
      });
    // Innermost first: an outer rewrite copies its operand through textOf, which takes the inner edit along.
    for (const expr of awaits.reverse()) {
      const text = edits.textOf(expr.getExpression());
      const parent = expr.getParent();
      // `(await c.get(X)).run()` becomes `c.get(X).run()`.
      if (
        Node.isParenthesizedExpression(parent) &&
        Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(parent.getParent())
      )
        edits.replace(parent, text);
      else edits.replace(expr, text);
    }
  },
};
```

- Modify: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/index.ts`
- Test: `__fixtures__/container-leftovers/leftovers/`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: `coreTypesOf` (Task 7); `TodoCode` (Task 1).
- Produces: `containerLeftovers: Transform<Migration04State>` with id `container-leftovers`.

`resolve` gets `container-resolve`, `clear` gets `container-clear`, `list` gets `container-list`, `createChildContainer` and `child` get `child-container`, and the async branch's `dispose` and `init` get `container-lifecycle` (spec §13.2 at `b5ab435`). `has` stays.

```ts
import { Nexus, Service } from '@nexusdi/core';

@Service()
export class Job {}

export async function shutdown(container: Nexus): Promise<void> {
  const job = container.resolve(Job);
  const listing = container.list();
  const child = container.createChildContainer();
  const scope = container.child();
  await container.init();
  container.clear();
  await container.dispose();
  void [job, listing, child, scope];
}
```

```ts
import { Nexus } from '@nexusdi/core';

export class Job {}

export async function shutdown(container: Nexus): Promise<void> {
  // TODO(nexusdi-0.4): container.resolve() is gone in 0.4; register the class with provide(C, { deps, lifetime: 'transient' }) and get() it (container-resolve)
  const job = container.resolve(Job);
  // TODO(nexusdi-0.4): container.list() is gone in 0.4; ship.graph() returns the compiled graph as JSON (container-list)
  const listing = container.list();
  // TODO(nexusdi-0.4): container.createChildContainer() is gone in 0.4; a child container becomes a scope: await ship.createScope() (child-container)
  const child = container.createChildContainer();
  // TODO(nexusdi-0.4): container.child() is gone in 0.4; a child container becomes a scope: await ship.createScope() (child-container)
  const scope = container.child();
  // TODO(nexusdi-0.4): container.init() is gone in 0.4; Nexus.create builds and initialises every singleton (container-lifecycle)
  await container.init();
  // TODO(nexusdi-0.4): container.clear() is gone in 0.4; dispose the container with await ship[Symbol.asyncDispose]() and create a new one (container-clear)
  container.clear();
  // TODO(nexusdi-0.4): container.dispose() is gone in 0.4; use await ship[Symbol.asyncDispose]() or await using (container-lifecycle)
  await container.dispose();
  void [job, listing, child, scope];
}
```

- [ ] Step 3: Write the transform

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/container-leftovers.ts`:

```ts
import { Node, SyntaxKind } from 'ts-morph';

import type { TodoCode } from '../../../codes.js';
import { coreTypesOf } from '../../../core-api.js';
import type { Transform } from '../../../transform.js';
import type { Migration04State } from '../state.js';

/** 0.3 container methods 0.4 does not have, and what each becomes. */
const LEFTOVERS: ReadonlyMap<
  string,
  { readonly code: TodoCode; readonly advice: string }
> = new Map([
  [
    'resolve',
    {
      code: 'container-resolve',
      advice:
        "register the class with provide(C, { deps, lifetime: 'transient' }) and get() it",
    },
  ],
  [
    'clear',
    {
      code: 'container-clear',
      advice:
        'dispose the container with await ship[Symbol.asyncDispose]() and create a new one',
    },
  ],
  [
    'list',
    {
      code: 'container-list',
      advice: 'ship.graph() returns the compiled graph as JSON',
    },
  ],
  [
    'dispose',
    {
      code: 'container-lifecycle',
      advice: 'use await ship[Symbol.asyncDispose]() or await using',
    },
  ],
  [
    'init',
    {
      code: 'container-lifecycle',
      advice: 'Nexus.create builds and initialises every singleton',
    },
  ],
  [
    'createChildContainer',
    {
      code: 'child-container',
      advice: 'a child container becomes a scope: await ship.createScope()',
    },
  ],
  [
    'child',
    {
      code: 'child-container',
      advice: 'a child container becomes a scope: await ship.createScope()',
    },
  ],
]);

export const containerLeftovers: Transform<Migration04State> = {
  id: 'container-leftovers',
  description:
    'Marks resolve, clear, list, dispose, init, createChildContainer and child, which 0.4 does not have.',
  run(file, context) {
    const types = coreTypesOf(context.project);
    for (const call of file.getDescendantsOfKind(SyntaxKind.CallExpression)) {
      const callee = call.getExpression();
      if (!Node.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee)) continue;
      const leftover = LEFTOVERS.get(callee.getName());
      if (!leftover || !types.isContainer(callee.getExpression())) continue;
      context.todo(
        call,
        leftover.code,
        `${callee.getText()}() is gone in 0.4; ${leftover.advice}`,
      );
    }
  },
};
```

```ts
import type { Migration, Transform } from '../../transform.js';
import { createState, type Migration04State } from './state.js';
import { awaitGet } from './transforms/await-get.js';
import { containerBootstrap } from './transforms/container-bootstrap.js';
import { containerLeftovers } from './transforms/container-leftovers.js';
import { imports } from './transforms/imports.js';
import { moduleConfig } from './transforms/module-config.js';
import { moduleExports } from './transforms/module-exports.js';
import { propertyInjection } from './transforms/property-injection.js';
import { services } from './transforms/services.js';
import { symbolTokens } from './transforms/symbol-tokens.js';

/**
 * The 0.4 transform ids in the order the spec fixes. Later transforms read
 * the output of earlier ones, so the order is part of the contract.
 */
export const TRANSFORM_IDS = [
  'symbol-tokens',
  'services',
  'property-injection',
  'module-config',
  'container-bootstrap',
  'module-exports',
  'await-get',
  'container-leftovers',
  'imports',
  'tsconfig',
] as const;

const transforms: readonly Transform<Migration04State>[] = [
  symbolTokens,
  services,
  propertyInjection,
  moduleConfig,
  containerBootstrap,
  moduleExports,
  awaitGet,
  containerLeftovers,
  imports,
];

export const migration04: Migration<Migration04State> = {
  version: '0.4',
  transforms,
  createState,
};
```

### Task 21: The `tsconfig` transform

Files:

- Create: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/tsconfig.ts`
- Modify: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/index.ts`
- Test: fixture cases under `__fixtures__/tsconfig/`, each with a `tsconfig.json`, so the harness loads it through `loadProjects`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: `readJsonc`, `removeJsonc`, `setJsonc`, `commentAboveJsonc` (Task 9); `legacyDecoratorsInUse` (Task 10); `todoMarker` (Task 3); `context.tsconfigs`, `context.readTextFile`, `context.writeTextFile`, `context.todoInText` (Task 5).
- Produces: `tsconfig: Transform<Migration04State>` with id `tsconfig`.

Every tsconfig the run loaded, and every config in their `extends` chains that is not inside `node_modules`, loses `experimentalDecorators: true` and `emitDecoratorMetadata: true`. A config that extends a base in `node_modules` which turns a flag on gets the flag set to `false`, since the base would turn it back on. A flag already `false` stays, which keeps a second run from moving it. When the project applies decorators from another package, the flags stay, `experimentalDecorators` gets the `legacy-decorators-in-use` TODO as a JSONC comment, and `services` has marked each `@Injectable` it wrote (spec §13.2).

- [ ] Step 3: Write the transform

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/transforms/tsconfig.ts`:

```ts
import { dirname, join, resolve } from 'node:path';

import {
  commentAboveJsonc,
  readJsonc,
  removeJsonc,
  setJsonc,
} from '../../../edit/jsonc.js';
import { todoMarker } from '../../../markers.js';
import type { MigrationContext, Transform } from '../../../transform.js';
import { legacyDecoratorsInUse, type Migration04State } from '../state.js';

type Context = MigrationContext<Migration04State>;

const FLAGS = ['experimentalDecorators', 'emitDecoratorMetadata'] as const;

function isOwned(path: string): boolean {
  return !path.split(/[\\/]/).includes('node_modules');
}

/**
 * Where an `extends` entry points: a relative path, or a package specifier
 * looked up in every node_modules above the config, the way tsc does.
 */
function resolveExtends(
  from: string,
  spec: string,
  exists: (path: string) => boolean,
): string | undefined {
  const candidates = (base: string) => [
    base,
    `${base}.json`,
    join(base, 'tsconfig.json'),
  ];
  if (spec.startsWith('.') || spec.startsWith('/')) {
    return candidates(resolve(dirname(from), spec)).find(exists);
  }
  for (let dir = dirname(from); ; dir = dirname(dir)) {
    const found = candidates(join(dir, 'node_modules', spec)).find(exists);
    if (found) return found;
    if (dirname(dir) === dir) return undefined;
  }
}

function extendsOf(text: string): string[] {
  const value = readJsonc(text, ['extends']);
  if (typeof value === 'string') return [value];
  return Array.isArray(value)
    ? value.filter((v): v is string => typeof v === 'string')
    : [];
}

/** Whether the config at `path`, through its `extends` chain, sets `flag` to true. */
function effectiveFlag(
  path: string,
  flag: string,
  context: Context,
  seen = new Set<string>(),
): boolean {
  if (seen.has(path)) return false;
  seen.add(path);
  const text = context.readTextFile(path);
  if (text === undefined) return false;
  const own = readJsonc(text, ['compilerOptions', flag]);
  if (typeof own === 'boolean') return own;
  // Later entries of an extends array win, so the nearest setting is found reading backwards.
  for (const spec of extendsOf(text).reverse()) {
    const base = resolveExtends(
      path,
      spec,
      (p) => context.readTextFile(p) !== undefined,
    );
    if (base && effectiveFlag(base, flag, context, seen)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/** Every config the run loaded, then every config in their `extends` chains, without repeats. */
function chain(context: Context): string[] {
  const all: string[] = [];
  const visit = (path: string): void => {
    if (all.includes(path)) return;
    const text = context.readTextFile(path);
    if (text === undefined) return;
    all.push(path);
    for (const spec of extendsOf(text)) {
      const base = resolveExtends(
        path,
        spec,
        (p) => context.readTextFile(p) !== undefined,
      );
      if (base) visit(base);
    }
  };
  for (const path of context.tsconfigs) visit(path);
  return all;
}

const LEGACY_REASON =
  'experimentalDecorators stays on for the other decorators in this project; @Injectable, @Inject and @Module throw NEXUS_LEGACY_DECORATORS under it, so register NexusDI classes with provide() and defineModule()';

function keepFlags(path: string, context: Context): void {
  const text = context.readTextFile(path) ?? '';
  if (readJsonc(text, ['compilerOptions', 'experimentalDecorators']) !== true)
    return;
  const marker = todoMarker('legacy-decorators-in-use', LEGACY_REASON).slice(3);
  const next = commentAboveJsonc(
    text,
    ['compilerOptions', 'experimentalDecorators'],
    marker,
  );
  if (next === text) return;
  context.writeTextFile(path, next);
  context.todoInText(path, 'legacy-decorators-in-use', LEGACY_REASON);
}

function dropFlags(
  path: string,
  context: Context,
  owned: readonly string[],
): void {
  let text = context.readTextFile(path) ?? '';
  const before = text;
  // A flag set to false stays: it is the override an earlier run wrote, or the user's own.
  for (const flag of FLAGS) {
    if (readJsonc(text, ['compilerOptions', flag]) === true)
      text = removeJsonc(text, ['compilerOptions', flag]);
  }
  // A base in node_modules that turns a flag on would turn it back on; override it here.
  for (const spec of extendsOf(text)) {
    const base = resolveExtends(
      path,
      spec,
      (p) => context.readTextFile(p) !== undefined,
    );
    if (!base || owned.includes(base)) continue;
    for (const flag of FLAGS) {
      if (
        readJsonc(text, ['compilerOptions', flag]) === undefined &&
        effectiveFlag(base, flag, context)
      ) {
        text = setJsonc(text, ['compilerOptions', flag], false);
      }
    }
  }
  if (text !== before) context.writeTextFile(path, text);
}

export const tsconfig: Transform<Migration04State> = {
  id: 'tsconfig',
  description:
    'Removes experimentalDecorators and emitDecoratorMetadata from every tsconfig the project owns.',
  runProject(context) {
    const configs = chain(context);
    const owned = configs.filter(isOwned);
    const legacy = legacyDecoratorsInUse(context);
    for (const path of owned) {
      if (legacy) keepFlags(path, context);
      else dropFlags(path, context, owned);
    }
  },
};
```

```ts
import type { Migration, Transform } from '../../transform.js';
import { createState, type Migration04State } from './state.js';
import { awaitGet } from './transforms/await-get.js';

  'symbol-tokens',
  'services',

  'container-bootstrap',
  'module-exports',
  'await-get',
  'container-leftovers',
  'imports',
  'tsconfig',
] as const;

const transforms: readonly Transform<Migration04State>[] = [
  symbolTokens,
  services,
  propertyInjection,
  moduleConfig,
  containerBootstrap,
  moduleExports,
  awaitGet,
  containerLeftovers,
  imports,
  tsconfig,
];

export const migration04: Migration<Migration04State> = {
  version: '0.4',
  transforms,
  createState,
};
```

### Task 22: The CLI and the code coverage check

Files:

Interfaces:

- Consumes: `MIGRATIONS` (Task 10); `loadProjects` (Task 6); `buildReport`, `renderMarkdown`, `renderSummary`, `renderDiff` (Task 11); `CodemodError`, `UsageError` (Task 2); every transform (Tasks 12 to 21).
- Produces: `USAGE`; `interface CliIo { cwd; stdout(text); stderr(text) }`; `main(argv, io): number`; the bin `dist/cli.js`.

`main` takes its working directory and output streams as arguments, so the tests run the whole CLI against a temporary project without a child process. The migration runs in memory first; files and the report are written only after it completes, so a failing transform leaves the project untouched.

```ts
import {
  mkdirSync,
  mkdtempSync,
  readFileSync,
  rmSync,
  writeFileSync,
} from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';

import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { main } from './main.js';

const SERVICE = `import { Inject, Service, Token } from '@nexusdi/core';

export const LOGGER = new Token<string>('Logger');

@Service()
export class Greeter {
  constructor(@Inject(LOGGER) readonly logger: string) {}
}
`;

let dir: string;
let out: string;
let err: string;

function run(...argv: string[]): number {
  out = '';
  err = '';
  return main(argv, {
    cwd: dir,
    stdout: (t) => void (out += t),
    stderr: (t) => void (err += t),
  });
}

beforeEach(() => {
  dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'nexusdi-codemod-'));
  mkdirSync(join(dir, 'src'));
  writeFileSync(
    join(dir, 'tsconfig.json'),
    '{\n  "compilerOptions": {\n    "strict": true,\n    "experimentalDecorators": true\n  },\n  "include": ["src"]\n}\n',
  );
  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'src', 'greeter.ts'), SERVICE);
});

afterEach(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));

describe('main', () => {
  it('migrates the project, writes the JSON report and exits 0', () => {
    expect(run('0.4')).toBe(0);
    expect(readFileSync(join(dir, 'src', 'greeter.ts'), 'utf8')).toContain(
      '@Injectable({ deps: [LOGGER] })',
    );
    expect(readFileSync(join(dir, 'tsconfig.json'), 'utf8')).toBe(
      '{\n  "compilerOptions": {\n    "strict": true,\n  },\n  "include": ["src"]\n}\n',
    );
    const report = JSON.parse(
      readFileSync(join(dir, 'nexusdi-0.4-report.json'), 'utf8'),
    ) as { migration: string; summary: { filesChanged: number } };
    expect(report.migration).toBe('0.4');
    expect(report.summary.filesChanged).toBe(2);
    expect(out).toBe('2 files changed, 0 TODOs, 0 notes.\n');
  });

  it('prints the diff the real run would apply and writes nothing with --dry-run', () => {
    expect(run('0.4', '--dry-run')).toBe(0);
    expect(readFileSync(join(dir, 'src', 'greeter.ts'), 'utf8')).toBe(SERVICE);
    expect(out).toContain('--- a/src/greeter.ts\n+++ b/src/greeter.ts\n');
    expect(out).toContain('+@Injectable({ deps: [LOGGER] })');
    expect(() => readFileSync(join(dir, 'nexusdi-0.4-report.json'))).toThrow();
  });

  it('writes a Markdown report to a --report path ending in .md', () => {
    expect(run('0.4', '--report', 'migration.md')).toBe(0);
    expect(readFileSync(join(dir, 'migration.md'), 'utf8')).toMatch(
      /^# NexusDI 0.4 migration report/,
    );
  });

  it('runs only the transforms --only names', () => {
    expect(run('0.4', '--only', 'tsconfig')).toBe(0);
    expect(readFileSync(join(dir, 'src', 'greeter.ts'), 'utf8')).toBe(SERVICE);
  });

  it('exits 2 for a migration it does not have, an unknown option or an unknown transform', () => {
    expect(run('0.9')).toBe(2);
    expect(err).toContain('no migration to 0.9');
    expect(run('0.4', '--bogus')).toBe(2);
    expect(run('0.4', '--only', 'nope')).toBe(2);
    expect(run()).toBe(2);
  });

  it('exits 1 when the project fails to load', () => {
    expect(run('0.4', '--tsconfig', 'missing.json')).toBe(1);
    expect(err).toContain('cannot load the project');
  });
});
```

```ts
import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { NOTE_CODES, TODO_CODES } from '../../codes.js';
import type { FixtureReport } from '../../testing/harness.js';

const FIXTURES = join(import.meta.dirname, '__fixtures__');

/** Every report.json under the fixtures, whatever the transform or corpus. */
function reports(): FixtureReport[] {
  const found: FixtureReport[] = [];
  const walk = (dir: string): void => {
    for (const name of readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
      if (!name.isDirectory()) continue;
      const path = join(dir, name.name);
      const report = join(path, 'report.json');
      if (existsSync(report))
        found.push(JSON.parse(readFileSync(report, 'utf8')) as FixtureReport);
      walk(path);
    }
  };
  walk(FIXTURES);
  return found;
}

const used = reports().flatMap((r) =>
  r.files.flatMap((f) => [
    ...f.todos.map((t) => t.code),
    ...f.notes.map((n) => n.code),
  ]),
);

describe('the fixture reports', () => {
  it('show every TODO and note code at least once', () => {
    const codes: string[] = [...TODO_CODES, ...NOTE_CODES];
    expect(codes.filter((code) => !used.includes(code))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('use no code outside TODO_CODES and NOTE_CODES', () => {
    const codes: string[] = [...TODO_CODES, ...NOTE_CODES];
    expect([...new Set(used)].filter((code) => !codes.includes(code))).toEqual(
      [],
    );
  });
});
```

- [ ] Step 3: Write the CLI function

`libs/codemod/src/main.ts`:

```ts
import { existsSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { parseArgs } from 'node:util';

import { CodemodError, UsageError } from './errors.js';
import { MIGRATIONS } from './migrations/index.js';
import { loadProjects } from './project/load-projects.js';
import { renderDiff } from './report/diff.js';
import { buildReport, renderMarkdown, renderSummary } from './report/report.js';

export const USAGE = `Usage: npx @nexusdi/codemod <migration> [paths...] [options]

Migrations: ${[...MIGRATIONS.keys()].join(', ')}

Options:
  --tsconfig <path>   the project to migrate (default: ./tsconfig.json)
  --dry-run           print a unified diff and write nothing
  --report <path>     where to write the report, .json or .md (default: ./nexusdi-<migration>-report.json)
  --only <id,...>     run only these transforms
  --help              print this message
`;

/** Where the CLI reads and writes, so tests can run it without a process. */
export interface CliIo {
  readonly cwd: string;
  stdout(text: string): void;
  stderr(text: string): void;
}

function codemodVersion(): string {
  const manifest = JSON.parse(
    readFileSync(new URL('../package.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8'),
  ) as { version: string };
  return manifest.version;
}

function parse(argv: readonly string[]) {
  try {
    return parseArgs({
      args: [...argv],
      allowPositionals: true,
      strict: true,
      options: {
        tsconfig: { type: 'string' },
        'dry-run': { type: 'boolean', default: false },
        report: { type: 'string' },
        only: { type: 'string' },
        help: { type: 'boolean', default: false },
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    throw new UsageError(
      error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
    );
  }
}

/**
 * Runs the CLI and returns its exit code: 0 when the migration completes,
 * TODOs included; 1 when the project fails to load or a transform throws;
 * 2 when the command line is wrong.
 */
export function main(argv: readonly string[], io: CliIo): number {
  try {
    const { values, positionals } = parse(argv);
    if (values.help) {
      io.stdout(USAGE);
      return 0;
    }
    const [version, ...paths] = positionals;
    if (!version) throw new UsageError(`name a migration\n\n${USAGE}`);
    const migration = MIGRATIONS.get(version);
    if (!migration)
      throw new UsageError(
        `no migration to ${version}; the codemod has ${[...MIGRATIONS.keys()].join(', ')}`,
      );

    const tsconfig = resolve(io.cwd, values.tsconfig ?? 'tsconfig.json');
    const only = values.only
      ?.split(',')
      .map((id) => id.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);
    const workspace = loadProjects({
      tsconfig,
      paths: paths.map((p) => resolve(io.cwd, p)),
    });
    for (const warning of workspace.warnings)
      io.stderr(`warning: ${warning}\n`);

    const result = migration.run({
      projects: workspace.projects,
      tsconfigs: workspace.tsconfigs,
      only,
      readTextFile: (path) =>
        existsSync(path) ? readFileSync(path, 'utf8') : undefined,
    });
    const report = buildReport(result, {
      migration: version,
      codemodVersion: codemodVersion(),
      cwd: io.cwd,
    });

    if (values['dry-run']) {
      io.stdout(renderDiff(result.files, io.cwd));
    } else {
      for (const file of result.files) writeFileSync(file.path, file.after);
      const reportPath = resolve(
        io.cwd,
        values.report ?? `nexusdi-${version}-report.json`,
      );
      writeFileSync(
        reportPath,
        reportPath.endsWith('.md')
          ? renderMarkdown(report)
          : `${JSON.stringify(report, null, 2)}\n`,
      );
    }
    io.stdout(renderSummary(report));
    return 0;
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof CodemodError) {
      io.stderr(`@nexusdi/codemod: ${error.message}\n`);
      return error.exitCode;
    }
    io.stderr(
      `@nexusdi/codemod: ${error instanceof Error ? (error.stack ?? error.message) : String(error)}\n`,
    );
    return 1;
  }
}
```

### Task 23: The docs and 0.3 test-file corpus

Files:

- Create: `libs/codemod/scripts/extract-corpus.mjs`, `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/corpus.test.ts`
- Create (generated): `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/__fixtures__/corpus-docs/<page>-<nn>/{input.ts,output.ts,report.json}` and `__fixtures__/corpus-tests/<source>-<file>/{input.ts,case.json,output.ts,report.json}`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: the fixture test (Task 10), every transform.
- Produces: `blocksOf(markdown)`, `withCoreImport(code)`, `asPackageImports(code)` in the script; about 330 corpus cases.

Spec §13.2 takes the before-and-after material from the 0.3 docs, the 0.3.1 test files and the async-branch test files. The script writes each docs block and each test file as a case whose first line names its source. Docs snippets usually leave out the `@nexusdi/core` import, and the codemod recognises the 0.3 API by it, so the script adds the import a block leaves out. 0.3 test files import core through relative paths, which the script points at `@nexusdi/core`, as a user's code imports it. The expected outputs are generated and reviewed: nobody writes 330 goldens by hand, and the review is what makes them a record. The corpus is exempt from the typecheck of Task 24, because its inputs do not compile against 0.3 either.

A prototype run of this plan's code over the corpus found three defects the hand-written fixtures missed (a reason that quoted multi-line code broke its marker, a type-only import of 0.3.1's `Provider` decorator was removed as unused, and a TODO repeated after `container-bootstrap` moved the marked code); the code in Tasks 3, 5 and 12 has those fixes.

- [ ] Step 1: Write the script

`libs/codemod/scripts/extract-corpus.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Writes the 0.3 corpus the fixture test runs the whole migration over:
 * every TypeScript block of the 0.3 docs, and every test file of 0.3.1 and
 * of the async branch. Each one becomes a case whose input.ts starts with a
 * comment naming its source, so a failing case leads back to its page.
 *
 *   node libs/codemod/scripts/extract-corpus.mjs
 *
 * The expected outputs are written by running the fixture test once with
 * NEXUSDI_UPDATE_FIXTURES=1, and reviewed before they are committed.
 */
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdirSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';

const FIXTURES = join(
  import.meta.dirname,
  '..',
  'src',
  'migrations',
  '0.4',
  '__fixtures__',
);

/** The refs the corpus reads, as the spec names them. */
export const SOURCES = {
  docs: { ref: '6d5e4f3', dir: 'docs/docs' },
  tests: [
    {
      ref: 'fix/core-export-map',
      dir: 'libs/core/src',
      core: 'core-0.3.1',
      prefix: '0.3.1',
    },
    {
      ref: 'archive/async-container',
      dir: 'libs/core/src',
      core: 'core-async-branch',
      prefix: 'async',
    },
  ],
};

/** The 0.3 exports a snippet may use without importing them. */
const EXPORTS = [
  'Nexus',
  'Token',
  'Module',
  'Service',
  'Provider',
  'Inject',
  'Optional',
  'DynamicModule',
  'createModuleConfig',
  'ContainerException',
  'NoProvider',
  'InvalidToken',
  'InvalidProvider',
  'InvalidModule',
];

const git = (...args) =>
  execFileSync('git', args, { encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 64 * 1024 * 1024 });

/** Every fenced ts, typescript or tsx block of a Markdown page. */
export function blocksOf(markdown) {
  return [
    ...markdown.matchAll(/^```(?:ts|typescript|tsx)[^\n]*\n([\s\S]*?)^```/gm),
  ].map((m) => m[1]);
}

/**
 * A docs block with the @nexusdi/core import it leaves out added. The
 * codemod recognises the 0.3 API by its imports, and most docs snippets
 * show the code and leave the import to the reader.
 */
export function withCoreImport(code) {
  const existing =
    /import\s*\{([^}]*)\}\s*from\s*['"]@nexusdi\/core['"];?/.exec(code);
  const imported = existing
    ? existing[1].split(',').map((s) =>
        s
          .trim()
          .split(/\s+as\s+/)
          .pop(),
      )
    : [];
  const body = existing ? code.replace(existing[0], '') : code;
  const used = EXPORTS.filter((name) =>
    new RegExp(`(^|[^.\\w])@?${name}\\b`).test(body),
  );
  const declared = used.filter((name) =>
    new RegExp(`\\b(class|interface|const|let|function)\\s+${name}\\b`).test(
      body,
    ),
  );
  const missing = used.filter(
    (name) => !declared.includes(name) && !imported.includes(name),
  );
  if (missing.length === 0) return code;
  if (existing)
    return code.replace(
      existing[0],
      `import { ${[
        ...existing[1]
          .split(',')
          .map((s) => s.trim())
          .filter(Boolean),
        ...missing,
      ].join(', ')} } from '@nexusdi/core';`,
    );
  return `import { ${missing.join(', ')} } from '@nexusdi/core';\n\n${code}`;
}

/** A 0.3 test file with its relative imports of core modules pointed at the package, as a user's code imports it. */
export function asPackageImports(code) {
  return code.replace(
    /from\s+['"](\.\.?\/)+(index|container|token|module|types|helpers|guards|constants|dynamic-module|decorators(\/\w+)?|exceptions(\/[\w.-]+)?)(\.js)?['"]/g,
    "from '@nexusdi/core'",
  );
}

function writeCase(group, name, source, code, core) {
  const dir = join(FIXTURES, group, name);
  mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'input.ts'), `// Source: ${source}\n${code}`);
  if (core !== 'core-0.3.1')
    writeFileSync(
      join(dir, 'case.json'),
      `${JSON.stringify({ core }, null, 2)}\n`,
    );
}

function main() {
  rmSync(join(FIXTURES, 'corpus-docs'), { recursive: true, force: true });
  rmSync(join(FIXTURES, 'corpus-tests'), { recursive: true, force: true });

  const { ref, dir } = SOURCES.docs;
  for (const page of git('ls-tree', '-r', '--name-only', ref, dir)
    .split('\n')
    .filter((p) => p.endsWith('.md'))) {
    const slug = page.slice(dir.length + 1, -3).replace(/\//g, '-');
    blocksOf(git('show', `${ref}:${page}`)).forEach((block, i) => {
      writeCase(
        'corpus-docs',
        `${slug}-${String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`,
        `${page} at ${ref}, block ${i + 1}`,
        withCoreImport(block),
        'core-0.3.1',
      );
    });
  }

  for (const { ref: testRef, dir: testDir, core, prefix } of SOURCES.tests) {
    for (const file of git('ls-tree', '-r', '--name-only', testRef, testDir)
      .split('\n')
      .filter((p) => p.endsWith('.test.ts'))) {
      const name = `${prefix}-${file.slice(testDir.length + 1, -'.test.ts'.length).replace(/\//g, '-')}`;
      writeCase(
        'corpus-tests',
        name,
        `${file} at ${testRef}`,
        asPackageImports(git('show', `${testRef}:${file}`)),
        core,
      );
    }
  }
}

if (import.meta.url === `file://${process.argv[1]}`) main();
```

- [ ] Step 2: Write the failing invariant test

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/corpus.test.ts`:

```ts
import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { commentLinesAbove, TODO_PREFIX } from '../../markers.js';

const FIXTURES = join(import.meta.dirname, '__fixtures__');

/**
 * The public 0.3 exports, of 0.3.1 and of the async branch, that 0.4 does
 * not have under the same name (spec §13.1). A migrated file may import
 * one only under a TODO marker.
 */
const V03_ONLY = new Set([
  'Service',
  'Provider',
  'Optional',
  'DynamicModule',
  'createModuleConfig',
  'IContainer',
  'TokenType',
  'ProviderType',
  'ModuleProvider',
  'ProviderConfig',
  'ModuleConfig',
  'ContainerException',
  'NoProvider',
  'InvalidToken',
  'InvalidProvider',
  'InvalidModule',
  'Constructor',
  'ClassProvider',
  'ValueProvider',
  'FactoryProvider',
  'ProviderConfigObject',
  'RegistrationOptions',
  'Disposable',
  'AsyncDisposable',
  'InjectionMetadata',
  'SYMBOL_METADATA',
  'METADATA_KEYS',
  'setMetadata',
  'getMetadata',
  'isToken',
  'isSymbol',
  'isTokenType',
  'isProvider',
  'isFactory',
  'isConstructor',
  'isService',
  'isContainer',
  'isModuleClass',
  'isModuleConfig',
  'isPromise',
]);

function corpusOutputs(): { name: string; text: string }[] {
  return ['corpus-docs', 'corpus-tests'].flatMap((group) => {
    const dir = join(FIXTURES, group);
    if (!existsSync(dir)) return [];
    return readdirSync(dir)
      .filter((name) => existsSync(join(dir, name, 'output.ts')))
      .map((name) => ({
        name: `${group}/${name}`,
        text: readFileSync(join(dir, name, 'output.ts'), 'utf8'),
      }));
  });
}

/**
 * The names a file imports from `@nexusdi/core` without a TODO marker
 * above the import. `Provider` counts only as a value import: 0.4 has a
 * `Provider` type, and 0.3.1 had a `Provider` decorator.
 */
function unmarkedImports(text: string): string[] {
  const names: string[] = [];
  for (const match of text.matchAll(
    /^import\s+(type\s+)?\{([^}]*)\}\s+from\s+'@nexusdi\/core';/gm,
  )) {
    if (
      commentLinesAbove(text, match.index).some((line) =>
        line.startsWith(TODO_PREFIX),
      )
    )
      continue;
    for (const part of (match[2] ?? '').split(',')) {
      const typeOnly = match[1] !== undefined || /^\s*type\s/.test(part);
      const name = part
        .trim()
        .replace(/^type\s+/, '')
        .split(/\s+as\s+/)[0];
      if (name && !(name === 'Provider' && typeOnly)) names.push(name);
    }
  }
  return names;
}

describe('the 0.3 corpus', () => {
  it('holds a migrated output for every docs block and 0.3 test file', () => {
    expect(corpusOutputs().length).toBeGreaterThan(250);
  });

  it('imports no 0.3-only name from @nexusdi/core outside a TODO-marked import', () => {
    const stray = corpusOutputs().flatMap(({ name, text }) =>
      unmarkedImports(text)
        .filter((imported) => V03_ONLY.has(imported))
        .map((imported) => `${name}: ${imported}`),
    );
    expect(stray).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

Run: `npx nx test @nexusdi/codemod -- src/migrations/0.4/corpus.test.ts`
Expected: FAIL: `holds a migrated output for every docs block and 0.3 test file` finds 0 outputs.

```bash
NEXUSDI_UPDATE_FIXTURES=1 npx nx test @nexusdi/codemod -- src/migrations/0.4/fixtures.test.ts
```

Expected: the script writes about 276 `corpus-docs` cases and 23 `corpus-tests` cases. The update run reports every new case as failed, because it loaded the cases before it wrote their outputs.

- [ ] Step 4: Review the generated outputs

Run: `git add -N libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/__fixtures__/corpus-* && git diff --stat -- libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/__fixtures__`

Read every `output.ts` against its `input.ts`, and reject the output when any of these holds:

- An `@Service`, `@Provider`, constructor `@Inject` or `@Optional` remains outside a TODO-marked statement.
- A `{ token, useClass | useValue | useFactory }` inside `providers` of an `@Module` or `defineModule` remains.
- A `new Nexus()` remains without a `top-level-container` marker above its statement.
- A `.config(` or `.configAsync(` on a module the case converted remains.
- A marker line is split across lines, or a report line or column points at a line that is not the statement under the marker.

A rejected output means a transform defect: fix it in the transform's own task file, add a hand-written case to that transform's fixtures that reproduces it, and regenerate.

### Task 24: Every fixture output compiles against `@nexusdi/core` 0.4 (needs the engine)

Engine dependency: rebase onto `feat/core-0.4` once its public entry declares `Nexus` (with `static create`, `get`, `has`), `Token`, `InjectionToken`, `Provider`, `ProviderEntry`, `ModuleDefinition`, `provide`, `defineModule` with `with()`, `optional`, `lazy`, `all`, `Injectable`, `Inject`, `Module`, `NexusError`, `MissingProviderError`, `NotVisibleError`, `InvalidTokenError`, `InvalidProviderError` and `InvalidModuleError` (engine Tasks 3 to 8, 18 and 31).

Files:

- Create: `libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/typecheck.test.ts`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: `loadCases`, `CaseConfig.typecheck` (Task 10); `commentLinesAbove`, `TODO_PREFIX` (Task 3); the fixture outputs of Tasks 12 to 21.
- Produces: nothing other tasks use.

The test compiles every fixture output in one ts-morph program against the workspace's `@nexusdi/core`, which resolves to the 0.4 sources through the `@nexusdi/source` condition, with `experimentalDecorators` off. A diagnostic is allowed only inside a statement that carries a TODO marker. A case opts out only through a `typecheck` reason in its `case.json`, and the second test lists those reasons so an opt-out is a visible decision.

- [ ] Step 1: Rebase onto the engine

```bash
git rebase feat/core-0.4
```

Expected: conflicts at most in `scripts/verify-packaging.mjs` (the engine rewrites its consumer; keep the engine's version and re-apply the two-entry `LIBS` from Task 1) and `.fallowrc.jsonc` (keep both packages in `publicPackages` and the codemod's `ignorePatterns`). Then `npx nx run-many -t build test -p @nexusdi/core @nexusdi/codemod` passes: the codemod's own tests do not read `@nexusdi/core`.

- [ ] Step 2: Write the test

`libs/codemod/src/migrations/0.4/typecheck.test.ts`:

```ts
import { existsSync, readdirSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, relative } from 'node:path';

import { Node, Project, ts } from 'ts-morph';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { commentLinesAbove, TODO_PREFIX } from '../../markers.js';
import { loadCases } from '../../testing/harness.js';

const FIXTURES = join(import.meta.dirname, '__fixtures__');

/** The corpus inputs do not compile against 0.3 either, so their outputs are not checked. */
const CORPUS = new Set(['corpus-docs', 'corpus-tests']);

/** The output files of every case that takes part, with the reason a case opts out. */
function outputs(): { files: string[]; skipped: string[] } {
  const files: string[] = [];
  const skipped: string[] = [];
  for (const transform of readdirSync(FIXTURES).sort()) {
    if (CORPUS.has(transform)) continue;
    for (const fixture of loadCases(join(FIXTURES, transform))) {
      if (fixture.config.typecheck) {
        skipped.push(
          `${transform}/${fixture.name}: ${fixture.config.typecheck}`,
        );
        continue;
      }
      const single = join(fixture.dir, 'output.ts');
      if (existsSync(single)) {
        files.push(single);
        continue;
      }
      const walk = (dir: string): void => {
        for (const name of readdirSync(dir)) {
          const path = join(dir, name);
          if (name === 'node_modules') continue;
          if (statSync(path).isDirectory()) walk(path);
          else if (/\.tsx?$/.test(name)) files.push(path);
        }
      };
      walk(join(fixture.dir, 'output'));
    }
  }
  return { files, skipped };
}

/** Whether a statement enclosing `node` carries a TODO marker: code the codemod left for the user. */
function underMarker(node: Node): boolean {
  const text = node.getSourceFile().getFullText();
  for (
    let current: Node | undefined = node;
    current;
    current = current.getParent()
  ) {
    if (Node.isSourceFile(current)) return false;
    if (
      commentLinesAbove(text, current.getStart(true)).some((line) =>
        line.startsWith(TODO_PREFIX),
      )
    )
      return true;
  }
  return false;
}

describe('the fixture outputs', () => {
  it('compile against @nexusdi/core 0.4 everywhere outside a TODO-marked statement', () => {
    const { files } = outputs();
    // The workspace's @nexusdi/core, through its @nexusdi/source condition: the 0.4 sources.
    const project = new Project({
      compilerOptions: {
        strict: true,
        noEmit: true,
        skipLibCheck: true,
        target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022,
        module: ts.ModuleKind.ESNext,
        moduleResolution: ts.ModuleResolutionKind.Bundler,
        customConditions: ['@nexusdi/source'],
        lib: [
          'lib.es2022.d.ts',
          'lib.esnext.decorators.d.ts',
          'lib.esnext.disposable.d.ts',
        ],
        jsx: ts.JsxEmit.ReactJSX,
        types: [],
      },
    });
    project.addSourceFilesAtPaths(files);

    const unexpected = project
      .getPreEmitDiagnostics()
      .filter((d) => {
        const file = d.getSourceFile();
        const start = d.getStart();
        if (!file || start === undefined || !files.includes(file.getFilePath()))
          return true;
        const node = file.getDescendantAtPos(start);
        return !node || !underMarker(node);
      })
      .map((d) => {
        const file = d.getSourceFile();
        const where = file
          ? `${relative(FIXTURES, file.getFilePath())}:${d.getLineNumber()}`
          : '(global)';
        return `${where} ${ts.flattenDiagnosticMessageText(d.compilerObject.messageText, ' ')}`;
      });

    expect(unexpected).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('opt out of the check only with a stated reason', () => {
    expect(outputs().skipped).toEqual([
      'symbol-tokens/shared-symbol: the symbol stays a symbol until its TODO is resolved, so the @Injectable that injects it does not compile',
      'tsconfig/legacy-decorators: the TypeORM-style decorator is a legacy decorator, which compiles only under the experimentalDecorators this project keeps',
    ]);
  });
});
```

Run: `npx nx test @nexusdi/codemod -- src/migrations/0.4/typecheck.test.ts`
Expected: PASS, 2 tests.

A listed diagnostic outside a TODO-marked statement means a transform writes code 0.4 rejects, or the engine's types differ from spec §4. Compare the diagnostic with spec §4.3 and §4.4. When the transform is wrong, fix it in its task's file, regenerate that case with `NEXUSDI_UPDATE_FIXTURES=1`, review the diff, and run the whole suite. When the engine's type differs from the spec, stop and report it to the owner with the diagnostic and the spec section.

Engine dependency: the 0.4 runtime and the hand port of the example, both on `feat/core-0.4`: `Nexus.create` building singletons, module encapsulation with `exports`, `with()` options, `useExisting` aliases, the runtime reading `@Injectable` metadata, and `ProviderEntry` (engine Tasks 8, 10 to 18, 31 and 36). Rebase onto `feat/core-0.4` after its Task 36.

Files:

- Create: `libs/codemod/src/e2e/react-ssr.test.ts`
- Create: `libs/codemod/e2e/react-ssr.resolved/app/shared/container.ts`, `tests/routes/home.spec.tsx`, `tests/routes/users.spec.tsx`, `tests/routes/users.empty.spec.tsx`, `tests/routes/users.error.spec.tsx`, `vite.config.ts`, `vite.decorators.ts`
- Create (generated, then reviewed): `libs/codemod/e2e/react-ssr.expected/**` and `libs/codemod/e2e/react-ssr.expected/nexusdi-0.4-report.json`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: `main` (Task 22); `TODO_PREFIX` (Task 3).
- Produces: nothing other tasks use.

The test takes `examples/react-ssr` as it was before the engine's hand port (see "Decisions"), writes it to `tmp/codemod-e2e-react-ssr` two levels below the workspace root so its `../../tsconfig.base.json` resolves, generates its React Router types, and runs the codemod on `app` and `tests`. It then checks the output against the reviewed golden, copies the hand resolution of every file that carries a TODO over it, and typechecks, tests and builds the app against the workspace's 0.4 core. The resolution adds one thing no TODO asks for: a Vite plugin that lowers standard decorators (Review Focus 1). `tmp` is in `.gitignore`, so Nx does not read the copy as a project.

- [ ] Step 1: Write the hand resolution of the TODO files

`libs/codemod/e2e/react-ssr.resolved/app/shared/container.ts`:

```ts
import { defineModule, Nexus } from '@nexusdi/core';
import { createContext } from 'react-router';
import type { Route } from '../+types/root';
import { UsersModule } from '../modules/users/users.module';
import { LoggerModule } from '../modules/logger/logger.module';

const env = getEnvironment();

const GlobalContainerRoot = defineModule({
  name: 'GlobalContainerRoot',
  imports: [
    LoggerModule.with({
      level: env === 'production' ? 'info' : 'debug',
      format: env === 'production' ? 'json' : 'text',
      enableConsole: true,
      enableFile: env === 'production',
      filePath: env === 'production' ? '/var/log/app.log' : undefined,
    }),
    UsersModule.with({
      apiUrl:
        env === 'production'
          ? process.env.USERS_API_URL || 'https://api.example.com/users'
          : 'http://localhost:3001/api/users',
      cacheEnabled: env === 'production',
      cacheTTL: env === 'production' ? 3600 : 300,
      maxUsersPerPage: env === 'production' ? 50 : 10,
      enableMockData: env !== 'production',
    }),
  ],
});

// Global container instance for SSR. Nexus.create is async, so the promise is
// made once, at import, and each request awaits it.
const globalContainer = Nexus.create(GlobalContainerRoot);
// A startup failure surfaces at the first request's await; this handler keeps
// it from also being reported as an unhandled rejection at import time.
globalContainer.catch(() => undefined);

// Create a context for the DI container
export const containerContext = createContext<Nexus>();

function getEnvironment(): 'development' | 'production' | 'test' {
  if (typeof process !== 'undefined' && process.env.NODE_ENV) {
    return process.env.NODE_ENV as 'development' | 'production' | 'test';
  }
  return 'development';
}

// Container middleware - provides DI container to downstream middleware/loaders
export const containerMiddleware: Route.MiddlewareFunction = async (
  { context },
  next,
) => {
  console.log('Container middleware called');

  try {
    const container = await globalContainer;
    console.log('Container retrieved in middleware:', container);

    context.set(containerContext, container);
    console.log('Container set in context');

    const response = await next();
    console.log('Container middleware completed');

    return response;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error in container middleware:', error);
    throw error;
  }
};
```

`libs/codemod/e2e/react-ssr.resolved/tests/routes/home.spec.tsx`:

```tsx
// Example: Minimal route test for Home using NexusDI and React Router v7
// This test demonstrates how to provide a DI container for a single route using createRoutesStub.
import React from 'react';
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import {
  createRoutesStub,
  RouterContext,
  RouterContextProvider,
} from 'react-router';
import {
  defineModule,
  Nexus,
  provide,
  type ProviderEntry,
} from '@nexusdi/core';
import Home, { loader as homeLoader } from '../../app/routes/home';
import {
  USER_SERVICE_TOKEN,
  type IUserService,
} from '../../app/modules/users/users.types';
import {
  LOGGER_SERVICE_TOKEN,
  type ILoggerService,
} from '../../app/modules/logger/logger.types';
import { containerContext } from '../../app/shared/container';

// Mocks
const mockUserService: IUserService = {
  getUsers: vi.fn(),
  getUserById: vi.fn(),
  createUser: vi.fn(),
};
const mockLoggerService: ILoggerService = {
  log: vi.fn(),
  debug: vi.fn(),
  info: vi.fn(),
  warn: vi.fn(),
  error: vi.fn(),
};

/** A router context holding a container built from `providers`, as the loader reads it. */
async function contextWith(
  ...providers: ProviderEntry[]
): Promise<Map<RouterContext, unknown>> {
  const container = await Nexus.create(
    defineModule({ name: 'HomeRouteTest', providers }),
  );
  return new Map<RouterContext, unknown>([[containerContext, container]]);
}

describe('Home route (framework mode, minimal DI example)', () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
  });

  it('renders the home page with both providers registered', async () => {
    const contextMap = await contextWith(
      provide(USER_SERVICE_TOKEN, { useValue: mockUserService }),
      provide(LOGGER_SERVICE_TOKEN, { useValue: mockLoggerService }),
    );

    const Stub = createRoutesStub(
      [
        {
          path: '/',
          Component: Home,
          loader: homeLoader,
        },
      ],
      new RouterContextProvider(contextMap),
    );
    render(<Stub initialEntries={['/']} />);
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getAllByText('Registered')).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(screen.getByText(/Container Status/i)).toBeDefined();
    });
  });

  it('renders the home page with only one provider registered', async () => {
    // USER_SERVICE_TOKEN is not provided
    const contextMap = await contextWith(
      provide(LOGGER_SERVICE_TOKEN, { useValue: mockLoggerService }),
    );

    const Stub = createRoutesStub(
      [
        {
          path: '/',
          Component: Home,
          loader: homeLoader,
        },
      ],
      new RouterContextProvider(contextMap),
    );
    render(<Stub initialEntries={['/']} />);
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText('Registered')).toBeDefined();
      expect(screen.getByText('Not Registered')).toBeDefined();
      expect(screen.getByText(/Container Status/i)).toBeDefined();
    });
  });
});
```

```tsx
  USER_SERVICE_TOKEN,
  type IUserService,
  type User,
} from '../../app/modules/users/users.types';

  { id: '2', name: 'Another User', email: 'another@example.com' },

  info: vi.fn(),
  warn: vi.fn(),

}

describe('Users route (framework mode, minimal DI example)', () => {

    vi.clearAllMocks();
  });

  it('renders the users page with users from DI', async () => {
    const contextMap = await contextWith(
      provide(USER_SERVICE_TOKEN, { useValue: mockUserService }),
      provide(LOGGER_SERVICE_TOKEN, { useValue: mockLoggerService }),
    );
    const Stub = createRoutesStub(
      [
        {
          path: '/users',
          Component: Users,
          loader: usersLoader,
        },
      ],
      new RouterContextProvider(contextMap),
    );
    render(<Stub initialEntries={['/users']} />);
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText(/User List/i)).toBeDefined();
      expect(screen.getByText('Test User')).toBeDefined();
      expect(screen.getByText('Another User')).toBeDefined();
    });
  });
});
```
