`libs/codemod/e2e/react-ssr.resolved/vite.decorators.ts`:

```ts
import ts from 'typescript';
import type { Plugin } from 'vite';

/**
 * Lowers TC39 standard decorators before Vite's Oxc transform reads a file.
 *
 * Oxc lowers only legacy (experimentalDecorators) decorators, and no Node
 * release or browser runs standard decorators natively, so `@Injectable`
 * reaches the runtime as a syntax error unless something lowers it first.
 * TypeScript's own transpiler does, with the emit a `tsc` build produces.
 * Only files with a line that starts with `@` are touched.
 */
export function standardDecorators(): Plugin {
  return {
    name: 'standard-decorators',
    enforce: 'pre',
    transform(code, id) {
      const file = id.split('?')[0] ?? id;
      if (!/\.tsx?$/.test(file) || !/^\s*@[A-Za-z]/m.test(code)) return null;
      const output = ts.transpileModule(code, {
        fileName: file,
        compilerOptions: {
          target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022,
          module: ts.ModuleKind.ESNext,
          jsx: ts.JsxEmit.Preserve,
          sourceMap: true,
          inlineSources: true,
        },
      });
      return {
        code: output.outputText.replace(/\/\/# sourceMappingURL=.*$/m, ''),
        map: output.sourceMapText ? JSON.parse(output.sourceMapText) : null,
      };
    },
  };
}
```

`libs/codemod/e2e/react-ssr.resolved/vite.config.ts`:

```ts
/// <reference types='vitest' />
import { defineConfig } from 'vite';
import { reactRouter } from '@react-router/dev/vite';
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import { standardDecorators } from './vite.decorators';

export default defineConfig(() => ({
  root: import.meta.dirname,
  cacheDir: '../../node_modules/.vite/examples/react-router',
  // Vite's own tsconfig auto-discovery walks parent directories with no
  // boundary at the workspace root, which can escape a git worktree
  // entirely. Naming the file explicitly (used by the vitest dev-server
  // transform path; the react-router build path resolves this correctly
  // on its own) keeps it from resolving anything but this project's own.
  tsconfig: './tsconfig.spec.json',
  server: {
    port: 4200,
    host: 'localhost',
  },
  preview: {
    port: 4200,
    host: 'localhost',
  },
  // NexusDI 0.4's decorators are standard decorators, which Vite's Oxc transform does not lower.
  plugins: [
    standardDecorators(),
    tailwindcss(),
    !process.env.VITEST && reactRouter(),
  ],
  // Uncomment this if you are using workers.
  // worker: {
  //  plugins: [ nxViteTsPaths() ],
  // },
  build: {
    outDir: './dist',
    emptyOutDir: true,
    reportCompressedSize: true,
    commonjsOptions: {
      transformMixedEsModules: true,
    },
  },
  test: {
    watch: false,
    globals: true,
    environment: 'jsdom',
    include: ['{src,tests}/**/*.{test,spec}.{js,mjs,cjs,ts,mts,cts,jsx,tsx}'],
    reporters: ['default'],
    coverage: {
      reportsDirectory: './test-output/vitest/coverage',
      provider: 'v8' as const,
    },
  },
}));
```

- [ ] Step 2: Write the test

`libs/codemod/src/e2e/react-ssr.test.ts`:

```ts
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import {
  cpSync,
  existsSync,
  mkdirSync,
  readdirSync,
  readFileSync,
  rmSync,
  statSync,
  writeFileSync,
} from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, join, relative } from 'node:path';

import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { TODO_PREFIX } from '../markers.js';
import { main } from '../main.js';

const WORKSPACE = join(import.meta.dirname, '..', '..', '..', '..');
const E2E = join(import.meta.dirname, '..', '..', 'e2e');
const EXPECTED = join(E2E, 'react-ssr.expected');
const RESOLVED = join(E2E, 'react-ssr.resolved');
// Two levels below the workspace root, so the example's `../../tsconfig.base.json` resolves as it does in place.
const APP = join(WORKSPACE, 'tmp', 'codemod-e2e-react-ssr');
const EXAMPLE = 'examples/react-ssr';
const SERVICE = `${EXAMPLE}/app/modules/logger/logger.service.ts`;
const REPORT = 'nexusdi-0.4-report.json';
const UPDATE = process.env['NEXUSDI_UPDATE_FIXTURES'] === '1';

/**
 * Files the resolution adds beyond the files that carry a TODO: the Vite
 * plugin that lowers the standard decorators the migration writes, which
 * Vite 8's Oxc transform leaves as they are.
 */
const TOOLING = ['vite.config.ts', 'vite.decorators.ts'];

function run(command: string, args: string[], cwd = WORKSPACE): string {
  return execFileSync(command, args, {
    cwd,
    encoding: 'utf8',
    stdio: 'pipe',
    maxBuffer: 256 * 1024 * 1024,
  });
}

/**
 * The commit whose `examples/react-ssr` is the 0.3 app: HEAD while the
 * example still uses `@Service`, and otherwise the parent of the commit
 * that ported it by hand to 0.4.
 */
function exampleRef(): string {
  const override = process.env['NEXUSDI_E2E_REF'];
  if (override) return override;
  if (readFileSync(join(WORKSPACE, SERVICE), 'utf8').includes('@Service('))
    return 'HEAD';
  const port = run('git', [
    'log',
    '-1',
    '--format=%H',
    '-S',
    '@Service(',
    '--',
    SERVICE,
  ]).trim();
  if (!port)
    throw new Error(
      `no commit in this history removes @Service( from ${SERVICE}; set NEXUSDI_E2E_REF`,
    );
  return `${port}^`;
}

function tree(root: string): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  const walk = (dir: string): void => {
    for (const name of readdirSync(dir)) {
      const path = join(dir, name);
      if (statSync(path).isDirectory()) walk(path);
      else out.push(relative(root, path));
    }
  };
  walk(root);
  return out.sort();
}

let exitCode = -1;
let stdout = '';
const base = readFileSync(join(WORKSPACE, 'tsconfig.base.json'), 'utf8');

beforeAll(() => {
  rmSync(APP, { recursive: true, force: true });
  mkdirSync(APP, { recursive: true });
  run('sh', [
    '-c',
    `git archive --format=tar "$0" "$1" | tar -x --strip-components=2 -C "$2"`,
    exampleRef(),
    EXAMPLE,
    APP,
  ]);
  run('npx', ['react-router', 'typegen'], APP);
  exitCode = main(['0.4', 'app', 'tests'], {
    cwd: APP,
    stdout: (text) => void (stdout += text),
    stderr: () => undefined,
  });
}, 300_000);

afterAll(() => {
  // NEXUSDI_E2E_KEEP=1 keeps the migrated app for a side-by-side read with the hand port.
  if (process.env['NEXUSDI_E2E_KEEP'] !== '1')
    rmSync(APP, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

describe('the codemod on the 0.3 react-ssr example', () => {
  it('migrates the app and writes the report it is expected to', () => {
    expect(exitCode, stdout).toBe(0);
    const report = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(APP, REPORT), 'utf8')) as {
      codemodVersion: string;
      files: { path: string }[];
    };
    report.codemodVersion = '(version)';
    if (UPDATE) {
      rmSync(EXPECTED, { recursive: true, force: true });
      for (const { path } of report.files) {
        mkdirSync(dirname(join(EXPECTED, path)), { recursive: true });
        cpSync(join(APP, path), join(EXPECTED, path));
      }
      writeFileSync(
        join(EXPECTED, REPORT),
        `${JSON.stringify(report, null, 2)}\n`,
      );
    }
    expect(report).toEqual(
      JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(EXPECTED, REPORT), 'utf8')),
    );
    for (const path of tree(EXPECTED).filter((p) => p !== REPORT)) {
      expect(readFileSync(join(APP, path), 'utf8'), path).toBe(
        readFileSync(join(EXPECTED, path), 'utf8'),
      );
    }
  });

  it('resolves every file that carries a TODO, and nothing else but the decorator tooling', () => {
    const report = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(APP, REPORT), 'utf8')) as {
      files: { path: string; todos: unknown[] }[];
    };
    const withTodos = report.files
      .filter((f) => f.todos.length > 0)
      .map((f) => f.path)
      .sort();
    expect(tree(RESOLVED).filter((p) => !TOOLING.includes(p))).toEqual(
      withTodos,
    );
    for (const path of tree(RESOLVED))
      cpSync(join(RESOLVED, path), join(APP, path));
    const left = tree(APP).filter(
      (p) =>
        /\.(ts|tsx|json)$/.test(p) &&
        !p.startsWith('node_modules/') &&
        readFileSync(join(APP, p), 'utf8').includes(TODO_PREFIX),
    );
    expect(left.filter((p) => p !== REPORT)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('typechecks, passes its own tests and builds against the workspace core', () => {
    run(
      'npx',
      [
        'tsc',
        '-p',
        'tsconfig.app.json',
        '--noEmit',
        '--composite',
        'false',
        '--incremental',
        'false',
      ],
      APP,
    );
    run('npx', ['vitest', 'run', '--config', 'vite.config.ts'], APP);
    run('npx', ['react-router', 'build'], APP);
    expect(existsSync(join(APP, 'build', 'server', 'index.js'))).toBe(true);
  }, 600_000);

  it('leaves the workspace tsconfig.base.json as it was', () => {
    expect(readFileSync(join(WORKSPACE, 'tsconfig.base.json'), 'utf8')).toBe(
      base,
    );
  });
});
```

### Task 26: The README and the packed-tarball check

Files:

- Modify: `libs/codemod/README.md` (replace the generator's), `scripts/verify-packaging.mjs`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: the built package (Tasks 1 to 22).
- Produces: nothing other tasks use.

`scripts/verify-packaging.mjs` already packs and installs `@nexusdi/codemod` (Task 1 added it to `LIBS`). This task adds what only the tarball can show: the `.` entry resolves with types under nodenext, and `npx @nexusdi/codemod` runs the packed bin, with its dependencies installed from the registry, against a 0.3 project (spec §17, "The codemod's bin runs").

- [ ] Step 1: Write the README

`libs/codemod/README.md`:

````md
# @nexusdi/codemod

Migrates a NexusDI 0.3 codebase to the 0.4 API. It rewrites what a mechanical rewrite can change and marks the rest with a `// TODO(nexusdi-0.4): <reason> (<code>)` comment and a report entry.

```sh
npx @nexusdi/codemod@next 0.4
```

## Usage

```sh
npx @nexusdi/codemod 0.4 [paths...] [options]
```

| Option              | Default                     | What it does                                              |
| ------------------- | --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| `--tsconfig <path>` | `./tsconfig.json`           | The project to migrate, with every config it references   |
| `--dry-run`         |                             | Prints a unified diff and writes nothing                  |
| `--report <path>`   | `./nexusdi-0.4-report.json` | Where the report goes; a path ending in `.md` is Markdown |
| `--only <id,...>`   | every transform             | Runs only the named transforms, in migration order        |

`[paths...]` restricts the run to those files and directories. A named file that no tsconfig includes, such as a test directory, is migrated too.

The exit code is `0` when the migration completes, TODOs included, `1` when the project fails to load or a transform fails, and `2` when the command line is wrong. Nothing is written unless the whole migration succeeds.

Run your formatter after the codemod: it keeps your formatting where it edits in place, and writes new code, such as a root module, in two-space indentation.

## What it changes

The transforms run in this order, each over every file:

| Transform             | What it does                                                                                                                                      |
| --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `symbol-tokens`       | Symbol tokens, `Token.create` and `Token.createUnique` become `new Token<T>(description)`; `a.equals(b)` becomes `a === b`                        |
| `services`            | `@Service` and `@Provider` become `@Injectable({ deps })`; constructor `@Inject` and `@Optional` become `deps`                                    |
| `property-injection`  | Marks property `@Inject` and `@Optional` for a move to `@Inject(X) accessor`                                                                      |
| `module-config`       | `@Module` provider objects become `provide()` calls; `DynamicModule` subclasses become `defineModule` with `options`; `config()` becomes `with()` |
| `container-bootstrap` | `new Nexus()` and its `set()` calls become a root `defineModule` and `await Nexus.create(Root)`                                                   |
| `module-exports`      | Adds the `exports` and `imports` 0.4 module encapsulation needs for dependencies 0.3 resolved across modules                                      |
| `await-get`           | `await container.get(X)` becomes `container.get(X)`                                                                                               |
| `container-leftovers` | Marks `resolve`, `clear`, `list`, `createChildContainer`, `child`, `dispose` and `init`                                                           |
| `imports`             | Rewrites `@nexusdi/core` imports to the 0.4 names and removes unused ones                                                                         |
| `tsconfig`            | Removes `experimentalDecorators` and `emitDecoratorMetadata` from every tsconfig the project owns                                                 |

The codemod recognises the 0.3 API by its imports from `@nexusdi/core`, including re-exports through your own files.

## Codes

Every TODO and note carries a code. The package exports the lists as `TODO_CODES` and `NOTE_CODES`.

| Code                          | What to do                                                                                                         |
| ----------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `untyped-token`               | Replace `any` in `new Token<any>(...)` with the type the token carries, or split a symbol used as a value too      |
| `token-to-symbol`             | `toSymbol()` is gone; compare tokens with `===`                                                                    |
| `undecorated-param`           | The parameter received `undefined` in 0.3.1; put its token in `deps` in place of `TODO_<name>`                     |
| `provider-singleton-false`    | 0.3.1 ignored `singleton: false`; add `lifetime: 'transient'` if you want a new instance per `get()`               |
| `provider-scope-option`       | 0.3.1 ignored `scope`; choose a `lifetime`                                                                         |
| `provider-type-option`        | 0.3.1 ignored `type`, and 0.4 has none; remove it                                                                  |
| `property-injection`          | Move the field to `@Inject(X) accessor field!: T` and check what reads it as an own property                       |
| `dynamic-module-typeof`       | `typeof Mod` now names a `ModuleDefinition`                                                                        |
| `dynamic-module-static`       | Convert the `DynamicModule` class to `defineModule({ options })` by hand                                           |
| `dynamic-module-unconfigured` | Import `Mod.with(value)`; 0.4 reports `NEXUS_MODULE_OPTIONS_MISSING` for a bare configurable module                |
| `dynamic-module-spread`       | Import `Mod.with(value)` in place of spreading a `config()` result                                                 |
| `dynamic-module-config-api`   | `getModuleConfig`, `getConfigToken`, `forRoot` and `createModuleConfig` of a token are gone                        |
| `top-level-container`         | Create the container with `await Nexus.create(Root)` where `await` is available                                    |
| `set-after-startup`           | Register in a module, `load()` a module, or use `createTestingContainer().override()` in a test                    |
| `module-exports-cycle`        | The import this dependency needs would form a module import cycle; move the provider                               |
| `dynamic-module-graph`        | The codemod could not read or change the module graph here; add the `exports` and `imports` by hand                |
| `container-resolve`           | Register the class with `provide(C, { deps, lifetime: 'transient' })` and `get()` it                               |
| `container-clear`             | Dispose the container with `await ship[Symbol.asyncDispose]()` and create a new one                                |
| `container-list`              | `ship.graph()` returns the compiled graph                                                                          |
| `child-container`             | A child container becomes a scope: `await ship.createScope()`                                                      |
| `container-lifecycle`         | `init()` is part of `Nexus.create`; `dispose()` becomes `await ship[Symbol.asyncDispose]()`                        |
| `removed-provider-type`       | The 0.3 provider types are gone; use `Provider<T>` or `ProviderEntry`                                              |
| `removed-metadata-api`        | The metadata helpers and the 0.3 decorators are gone                                                               |
| `removed-guard`               | The 0.3 type guards are gone                                                                                       |
| `error-message-match`         | 0.4 messages start with a code; match on `error.code`                                                              |
| `legacy-decorators-in-use`    | The project keeps `experimentalDecorators`, under which `@Injectable` throws; use `provide()` and `defineModule()` |
| `undecorated-param-injected`  | Note: the parameter received `undefined` in 0.3.1, and 0.4 injects it                                              |

## License

MIT
````

- [ ] Step 2: Add the codemod checks to the packaging script

In `scripts/verify-packaging.mjs`, add `mkdirSync` to the `node:fs` import, and insert this block directly before `console.log('\nPackaging verified.');`:

```js
  // The codemod's entry and its bin, as npm publishes them. The bin runs
  // through npx from the installed tarball, with ts-morph, jsonc-parser and
  // diff resolved from the consumer's own install.
  console.log('Checking @nexusdi/codemod from its tarball…');
  writeFileSync(
    join(dir, 'codemod-consumer.ts'),
    `import { NOTE_CODES, TODO_CODES, type TodoCode } from '@nexusdi/codemod';
const first: TodoCode = TODO_CODES[0];
void [first, NOTE_CODES];
`,
  );
  writeFileSync(
    join(dir, 'codemod.tsconfig.json'),
    JSON.stringify({
      compilerOptions: { strict: true, target: 'es2022', module: 'nodenext', moduleResolution: 'nodenext', noEmit: true, skipLibCheck: true },
      include: ['codemod-consumer.ts'],
    }),
  );
  run('npx', ['tsc', '-p', 'codemod.tsconfig.json'], dir);
  console.log('  ✓ @nexusdi/codemod exposes TODO_CODES and NOTE_CODES with types under nodenext');

  const legacy = join(dir, 'legacy-app');
  mkdirSync(join(legacy, 'src'), { recursive: true });
  writeFileSync(
    join(legacy, 'tsconfig.json'),
    '{\n  "compilerOptions": {\n    "strict": true,\n    "experimentalDecorators": true\n  },\n  "include": ["src"]\n}\n',
  );
  writeFileSync(
    join(legacy, 'src', 'greeter.ts'),
    `import { Inject, Service, Token } from '@nexusdi/core';

export const NAME = new Token<string>('Name');

@Service()
export class Greeter {
  constructor(@Inject(NAME) readonly name: string) {}
}
`,
  );
  const dryRun = run('npx', ['--no', '@nexusdi/codemod', '0.4', '--tsconfig', 'legacy-app/tsconfig.json', '--dry-run'], dir);
  if (!dryRun.includes('+@Injectable({ deps: [NAME] })')) {
    throw new Error(`npx @nexusdi/codemod --dry-run printed no rewrite:\n${dryRun}`);
  }
  run('npx', ['--no', '@nexusdi/codemod', '0.4', '--tsconfig', 'legacy-app/tsconfig.json'], dir);
  const migrated = readFileSync(join(legacy, 'src', 'greeter.ts'), 'utf8');
  const report = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dir, 'nexusdi-0.4-report.json'), 'utf8'));
  if (!migrated.includes('@Injectable({ deps: [NAME] })') || report.summary.filesChanged !== 2) {
    throw new Error(`npx @nexusdi/codemod did not migrate the 0.3 project:\n${migrated}`);
  }
  console.log('  ✓ npx @nexusdi/codemod runs from the tarball and migrates a 0.3 project');
```

### Task 27: The bootstrap publish, as a manual step for the owner

Files:

- Modify: `RELEASING.md`

Interfaces:

- Consumes: the whole package.
- Produces: the runbook the owner follows once, before `@nexusdi/codemod` can publish from CI.

npm lets a package configure a trusted publisher only after the package exists, so `@nexusdi/codemod`'s first version goes out from the owner's machine, as `RELEASING.md` already describes for `@nexusdi/core`. Spec §14 has the codemod published at `0.4.0-rc.0` on `next` alongside core, so the bootstrap publish is that version.

- [ ] Step 1: Add the runbook section

In `RELEASING.md`, directly after the "Then tag the commit you published from and configure the trusted publisher as above" block of "1. Configure a trusted publisher for @nexusdi/core", add:

````md
### 1b. Bootstrap @nexusdi/codemod

`@nexusdi/codemod` ships at the same version and dist-tag as `@nexusdi/core`,
starting with `0.4.0-rc.0` on `next`. It has never been published, so the
owner publishes that first version by hand, from a maintainer's machine with
their own 2FA, before the core `0.4.0-rc.0` release runs.

First open a pull request that sets the version, and merge it:

```bash
npm pkg set version=0.4.0-rc.0 --workspace @nexusdi/codemod
git commit -am "chore(codemod): set the bootstrap version 0.4.0-rc.0"
```

Then publish from the merged `main` and tag the commit:

```bash
git switch main && git pull
npx nx build @nexusdi/codemod
npm run verify:packaging
cd libs/codemod
npm publish --access public --tag next --no-provenance --otp=<code-from-your-authenticator>
cd ../..
git tag '@nexusdi/codemod@0.4.0-rc.0'
git push origin '@nexusdi/codemod@0.4.0-rc.0'
```

`--tag next` keeps a release candidate off `latest`. `npm view @nexusdi/codemod dist-tags`
should then show `next: 0.4.0-rc.0`. Configure the trusted publisher for
`@nexusdi/codemod` with the same fields as for `@nexusdi/core` above.

From `0.4.0-rc.1` on, run **Release** with `projects: core, codemod` and the
same `specifier` for both, so `npx @nexusdi/codemod@next 0.4` always matches
`@nexusdi/core@next`. An RC that breaks the previous RC ships the matching
codemod change in the same release (spec §14).
````

- [ ] Step 2: Check the formatting

Run: `npx prettier --check RELEASING.md`
Expected: exit 0.

```bash
git add RELEASING.md
git commit -m "docs(release): add the bootstrap publish of @nexusdi/codemod
```
